`internal/notify/channel.go`:

```go
// Package notify tells people what Pulsix did (spec 2026-09-18 §5, §7):
// notification channels (e-mail through the instance SMTP, a generic
// webhook), a delivery queue shared by every manager, schedule
// notification rules and the weekly digest. Messages carry names, results,
// counts, durations and links — never log output, variables or secrets.
package notify

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// Sentinels the API maps to HTTP.
var (
	ErrNotFound         = errors.New("not found")
	ErrInvalidInput     = errors.New("invalid input")
	ErrNameTaken        = errors.New("name already in use")
	ErrRevisionMismatch = errors.New("revision mismatch")
	// ErrMailUnavailable: the instance has no outgoing mail settings, so
	// no e-mail channel can be created (spec §5: "the kind is unavailable
	// and the UI says why").
	ErrMailUnavailable = errors.New("this instance has no outgoing mail settings; an instance administrator sets them under Settings → Outgoing mail")
)

// Kind is a channel's delivery mechanism. v0.4 adds more.
type Kind string

const (
	KindEmail   Kind = "email"
	KindWebhook Kind = "webhook"
)

// Channel is one stored channel. Config is the storage form: for webhooks
// the URL, every header value and the signing secret are sealed with
// AAD(OrgID, ID); View is the secret-free reading of it.
type Channel struct {
	ID, OrgID            uuid.UUID
	Kind                 Kind
	Name                 string
	Enabled              bool
	Config               json.RawMessage
	CreatedBy            *uuid.UUID
	CreatedAt, UpdatedAt time.Time
	Revision             int64
}

// EmailConfig is an e-mail channel's settings (nothing in it is secret).
type EmailConfig struct {
	Recipients []string `json:"recipients"`
}

// webhookConfig is a webhook channel's stored settings.
type webhookConfig struct {
	Method        string         `json:"method"`
	URLSealed     []byte         `json:"url_sealed"`
	URLDisplay    string         `json:"url_display"`
	Headers       []storedHeader `json:"headers,omitempty"`
	BodyTemplate  string         `json:"body_template,omitempty"`
	SigningSealed []byte         `json:"signing_sealed,omitempty"`
}

type storedHeader struct {
	Name        string `json:"name"`
	ValueSealed []byte `json:"value_sealed"`
}

// WebhookView is what a reader sees of a webhook channel: the URL only as
// scheme and host (a path can carry a token, as Slack's do), header names
// without values, and whether requests are signed.
type WebhookView struct {
	Method       string
	URLDisplay   string
	HeaderNames  []string
	BodyTemplate string
	Signed       bool
}

// View decodes the channel's settings into their secret-free form: exactly
// one of the two results is set, matching Kind.
func (c Channel) View() (*EmailConfig, *WebhookView, error) {
	switch c.Kind {
	case KindEmail:
		var e EmailConfig
		if err := json.Unmarshal(c.Config, &e); err != nil {
			return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("decode e-mail channel: %w", err)
		}
		return &e, nil, nil
	case KindWebhook:
		var w webhookConfig
		if err := json.Unmarshal(c.Config, &w); err != nil {
			return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("decode webhook channel: %w", err)
		}
		v := &WebhookView{Method: w.Method, URLDisplay: w.URLDisplay, BodyTemplate: w.BodyTemplate, Signed: len(w.SigningSealed) > 0}
		for _, h := range w.Headers {
			v.HeaderNames = append(v.HeaderNames, h.Name)
		}
		return nil, v, nil
	}
	return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown channel kind %q", c.Kind)
}

// HeaderInput is one webhook header as submitted. A nil Value on update
// keeps the stored value of the header with the same name.
type HeaderInput struct {
	Name  string
	Value *string
}

// WebhookInput is a webhook channel as submitted. A nil URL or
// SigningSecret on update keeps the stored one.
type WebhookInput struct {
	Method        string
	URL           *string
	Headers       []HeaderInput
	BodyTemplate  string
	Signed        bool
	SigningSecret *string
}

// Input is what an operator submits for create and update; exactly one
// of Email and Webhook matches Kind.
type Input struct {
	Kind    Kind
	Name    string
	Enabled bool
	Email   *EmailConfig
	Webhook *WebhookInput
}

// AAD is the associated data a channel's secrets are sealed with.
func AAD(orgID, id uuid.UUID) []byte {
	return []byte("channel:" + orgID.String() + ":" + id.String())
}
```

`internal/notify/validate.go`:

```go
package notify

import (
	"fmt"
	"net/mail"
	"net/url"
	"regexp"
	"strings"
	"unicode"
	"unicode/utf8"
)

const (
	maxNameRunes      = 100
	maxRecipients     = 20
	maxURLLength      = 2048
	maxHeaders        = 20
	maxHeaderValue    = 4096
	minSigningSecret  = 16
	maxSigningSecret  = 256
	maxTemplateLength = 16 << 10
)

// headerName is RFC 9110's token: the characters a header name may hold.
var headerName = regexp.MustCompile("^[A-Za-z0-9!#$%&'*+.^_`|~-]+$")

// reservedHeader reports whether a (lower-case) header name is set by the
// transport; the X-Pulsix- prefix is Pulsix's own (checkHeader). An
// operator's header must replace neither.
func reservedHeader(lower string) bool {
	switch lower {
	case "host", "content-length", "transfer-encoding", "connection", "upgrade", "te", "trailer",
		"keep-alive", "proxy-authorization", "proxy-connection":
		return true
	}
	return false
}

func normalizeName(name string) (string, error) {
	n := strings.TrimSpace(name)
	if n == "" || utf8.RuneCountInString(n) > maxNameRunes {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: name must be 1-%d characters", ErrInvalidInput, maxNameRunes)
	}
	for _, r := range n {
		if unicode.IsControl(r) {
			return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: name must not contain control characters", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
	}
	return n, nil
}

// normalizeEmail checks the recipient list: 1-20 plain addresses
// (no display names), duplicates dropped, order kept.
func normalizeEmail(in *EmailConfig) (EmailConfig, error) {
	if in == nil || len(in.Recipients) == 0 {
		return EmailConfig{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: an e-mail channel needs at least one recipient", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	if len(in.Recipients) > maxRecipients {
		return EmailConfig{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: at most %d recipients", ErrInvalidInput, maxRecipients)
	}
	out := EmailConfig{Recipients: make([]string, 0, len(in.Recipients))}
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, raw := range in.Recipients {
		r := strings.TrimSpace(raw)
		addr, err := mail.ParseAddress(r)
		if err != nil || addr.Name != "" || addr.Address != r {
			return EmailConfig{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: %q is not a plain e-mail address", ErrInvalidInput, r)
		}
		key := strings.ToLower(r)
		if !seen[key] {
			seen[key] = true
			out.Recipients = append(out.Recipients, r)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

// normalizeMethod defaults to POST and accepts POST or PUT.
func normalizeMethod(m string) (string, error) {
	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(m)) {
	case "", "POST":
		return "POST", nil
	case "PUT":
		return "PUT", nil
	}
	return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: method must be POST or PUT", ErrInvalidInput)
}

// checkURL validates a webhook URL and returns its display form. Only
// syntax is checked here: the destination address is checked when a
// request is actually made (internal/outbound), because a name can
// resolve differently by then.
func checkURL(raw string) (string, error) {
	u, err := url.Parse(strings.TrimSpace(raw))
	switch {
	case err != nil, len(raw) > maxURLLength:
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: url is not a valid URL of at most %d characters", ErrInvalidInput, maxURLLength)
	case u.Scheme != "http" && u.Scheme != "https":
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: url must start with http:// or https://", ErrInvalidInput)
	case u.Host == "" || u.Hostname() == "":
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: url needs a host", ErrInvalidInput)
	case u.User != nil:
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: url must not carry a user name or password; put credentials in a header", ErrInvalidInput)
	case u.Fragment != "":
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: url must not carry a fragment", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	display := u.Scheme + "://" + u.Host
	if (u.Path != "" && u.Path != "/") || u.RawQuery != "" {
		display += "/…"
	}
	return display, nil
}

// checkHeader validates one header name and, when given, its value.
func checkHeader(name string, value *string) error {
	if !headerName.MatchString(name) {
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: %q is not a valid header name", ErrInvalidInput, name)
	}
	lower := strings.ToLower(name)
	if reservedHeader(lower) || strings.HasPrefix(lower, "x-pulsix-") {
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: header %s is set by Pulsix and cannot be configured", ErrInvalidInput, name)
	}
	if value != nil && (len(*value) > maxHeaderValue || strings.ContainsAny(*value, "\r\n")) {
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: the value of header %s must be one line of at most %d characters", ErrInvalidInput, name, maxHeaderValue)
	}
	return nil
}

func checkSigningSecret(secret string) error {
	if n := len(secret); n < minSigningSecret || n > maxSigningSecret {
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: the signing secret must be %d-%d characters", ErrInvalidInput, minSigningSecret, maxSigningSecret)
	}
	return nil
}
```

`internal/notify/event.go`:

```go
package notify

import (
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// Category is what a schedule rule listens for (spec §7): failure,
// change (at least one host reported changed), success, skipped
// (skipped_* and missed decisions).
type Category string

const (
	CategoryFailure Category = "failure"
	CategoryChange  Category = "change"
	CategorySuccess Category = "success"
	CategorySkipped Category = "skipped"
)

// Categories lists every category in its canonical order.
func Categories() []Category {
	return []Category{CategoryFailure, CategoryChange, CategorySuccess, CategorySkipped}
}

// ParseCategory accepts exactly the four category names.
func ParseCategory(s string) (Category, bool) {
	for _, c := range Categories() {
		if string(c) == s {
			return c, true
		}
	}
	return "", false
}

// EventKind names what a message is about.
type EventKind string

const (
	EventRunFinished     EventKind = "run.finished"
	EventScheduleSkipped EventKind = "schedule.skipped"
	EventScheduleFailed  EventKind = "schedule.failed"
	EventDigest          EventKind = "digest.weekly"
	EventTest            EventKind = "test"
)

// Event is the fixed structure every message is rendered from — the
// default webhook body is its JSON form, a body template reads its
// fields. It deliberately has no field for log output, variables or
// secrets. Nil sections are absent for the kind.
type Event struct {
	Kind       EventKind        `json:"kind"`
	At         time.Time        `json:"at"`
	Org        OrgRef           `json:"org"`
	Categories []Category       `json:"categories,omitempty"`
	Schedule   *NamedRef        `json:"schedule,omitempty"`
	Template   *NamedRef        `json:"template,omitempty"`
	Run        *RunSummary      `json:"run,omitempty"`
	Decision   *DecisionSummary `json:"decision,omitempty"`
	Digest     *DigestSummary   `json:"digest,omitempty"`
	URL        string           `json:"url,omitempty"`
}

// OrgRef names the organization a message is about.
type OrgRef struct {
	ID   uuid.UUID `json:"id"`
	Slug string    `json:"slug"`
	Name string    `json:"name"`
}

// NamedRef is a schedule or template by id and name.
type NamedRef struct {
	ID   uuid.UUID `json:"id"`
	Name string    `json:"name"`
}

// RunSummary is a finished run group: its result (succeeded, failed,
// cancelled), host counts from the stored per-host results, and timing.
type RunSummary struct {
	ID              uuid.UUID  `json:"id"`
	Result          string     `json:"result"`
	Hosts           int        `json:"hosts"`
	HostsOK         int        `json:"hosts_ok"`
	HostsChanged    int        `json:"hosts_changed"`
	HostsFailed     int        `json:"hosts_failed"`
	StartedAt       *time.Time `json:"started_at,omitempty"`
	FinishedAt      *time.Time `json:"finished_at,omitempty"`
	DurationSeconds int64      `json:"duration_seconds"`
}

// DecisionSummary is a schedule occurrence that did not start a run.
type DecisionSummary struct {
	Outcome string    `json:"outcome"`
	Reason  string    `json:"reason,omitempty"`
	DueAt   time.Time `json:"due_at"`
}

// DigestSummary is the weekly digest's content (spec §7).
type DigestSummary struct {
	From      time.Time        `json:"from"`
	To        time.Time        `json:"to"`
	Timezone  string           `json:"timezone"`
	Schedules []DigestSchedule `json:"schedules"`
	Slowest   []DigestRun      `json:"slowest"`
}

// DigestSchedule is one schedule's week.
type DigestSchedule struct {
	ID       uuid.UUID `json:"id"`
	Name     string    `json:"name"`
	Runs     int       `json:"runs"`
	Failures int       `json:"failures"`
	Skipped  int       `json:"skipped"`
}

// DigestRun is one of the week's slowest runs. RunID is the run's first
// job (the run view); the outbox turns it into URL.
type DigestRun struct {
	Schedule        string    `json:"schedule"`
	RunID           uuid.UUID `json:"run_id"`
	StartedAt       time.Time `json:"started_at"`
	DurationSeconds int64     `json:"duration_seconds"`
	URL             string    `json:"url"`
}

// runCategories maps a finished run group to the categories it belongs
// to: its result (a cancelled run counts as a failure — it did not do its
// job) and change when any host reported changed.
func runCategories(result string, hostsChanged int) []Category {
	out := []Category{CategorySuccess}
	if result != "succeeded" {
		out = []Category{CategoryFailure}
	}
	if hostsChanged > 0 {
		out = append(out, CategoryChange)
	}
	return out
}

// decisionCategory maps a schedule decision that started no run to its
// category and event kind; ok is false for triggered (the run itself is
// reported when its group finishes).
func decisionCategory(outcome string) (Category, EventKind, bool) {
	switch outcome {
	case "skipped_overlap", "skipped_maintenance", "missed":
		return CategorySkipped, EventScheduleSkipped, true
	case "failed":
		return CategoryFailure, EventScheduleFailed, true
	}
	return "", "", false
}
```

`internal/notify/render.go`:

```go
package notify

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"text/template"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// maxBodyBytes bounds a rendered webhook body.
const maxBodyBytes = 64 << 10

var errBodyTooLarge = errors.New("the rendered body exceeds 64 KiB")

// limitedBuffer refuses to grow past maxBodyBytes, so a template cannot
// build an unbounded body.
type limitedBuffer struct{ bytes.Buffer }

func (b *limitedBuffer) Write(p []byte) (int, error) {
	if b.Len()+len(p) > maxBodyBytes {
		return 0, errBodyTooLarge
	}
	return b.Buffer.Write(p)
}

// templateFuncs are the helpers a body template may call: json renders a
// value as JSON (a string becomes a quoted, escaped JSON string), so
// `{"text": {{json .Schedule.Name}}}` stays valid JSON whatever the name.
func templateFuncs() template.FuncMap {
	return template.FuncMap{
		"json": func(v any) (string, error) {
			b, err := json.Marshal(v)
			return string(b), err
		},
	}
}

func parseBodyTemplate(tmpl string) (*template.Template, error) {
	return template.New("body").Funcs(templateFuncs()).Option("missingkey=error").Parse(tmpl)
}

// renderWebhookBody renders a webhook body: the event's JSON when tmpl is
// empty, the template over the event otherwise.
func renderWebhookBody(tmpl string, ev Event) ([]byte, error) {
	if tmpl == "" {
		return json.Marshal(ev)
	}
	t, err := parseBodyTemplate(tmpl)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parse body template: %w", err)
	}
	var buf limitedBuffer
	if err := t.Execute(&buf, ev); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("render body template: %w", err)
	}
	return buf.Bytes(), nil
}

// checkBodyTemplate validates a body template at save time: it must parse
// and render against a fully populated sample event, so a misspelled
// field fails now, not on the first real message.
func checkBodyTemplate(tmpl string) error {
	if tmpl == "" {
		return nil
	}
	if len(tmpl) > maxTemplateLength {
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: the body template must be at most %d bytes", ErrInvalidInput, maxTemplateLength)
	}
	if _, err := renderWebhookBody(tmpl, sampleEvent()); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrInvalidInput, err)
	}
	return nil
}

// sampleEvent fills every section with example values: templates are
// validated against it and "Send test" sends it (as kind test).
func sampleEvent() Event {
	started := time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 1, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	finished := started.Add(4*time.Minute + 12*time.Second)
	id := uuid.MustParse("00000000-0000-7000-8000-000000000001")
	return Event{
		Kind: EventRunFinished, At: finished,
		Org:        OrgRef{ID: id, Slug: "example", Name: "Example"},
		Categories: []Category{CategoryFailure, CategoryChange},
		Schedule:   &NamedRef{ID: id, Name: "Nightly OS patching"},
		Template:   &NamedRef{ID: id, Name: "patch-all"},
		Run: &RunSummary{
			ID: id, Result: "failed", Hosts: 12, HostsOK: 11, HostsChanged: 9, HostsFailed: 1,
			StartedAt: &started, FinishedAt: &finished, DurationSeconds: 252,
		},
		Decision: &DecisionSummary{Outcome: "skipped_maintenance", Reason: "every targeted host is in a maintenance window", DueAt: started},
		Digest: &DigestSummary{
			From: started.AddDate(0, 0, -7), To: started, Timezone: "Europe/Berlin",
			Schedules: []DigestSchedule{{ID: id, Name: "Nightly OS patching", Runs: 2, Failures: 1, Skipped: 1}},
			Slowest:   []DigestRun{{Schedule: "Nightly OS patching", RunID: id, StartedAt: started, DurationSeconds: 252, URL: "https://pulsix.example/o/example/runs/" + id.String()}},
		},
		URL: "https://pulsix.example/o/example/runs/" + id.String(),
	}
}

// renderEmail renders the plain-text e-mail for an event.
func renderEmail(ev Event) (subject, body string) {
	var b strings.Builder
	line := func(label, value string) {
		if value != "" {
			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "%-10s %s\n", label+":", value)
		}
	}
	name := func(r *NamedRef) string {
		if r == nil {
			return ""
		}
		return r.Name
	}
	switch ev.Kind {
	case EventRunFinished:
		result := "succeeded"
		if ev.Run != nil {
			result = ev.Run.Result
		}
		subject = fmt.Sprintf("[Pulsix] %s: %s", name(ev.Schedule), result)
		if ev.Run != nil && ev.Run.HostsChanged > 0 && result == "succeeded" {
			subject += " with changes"
		}
		line("Schedule", name(ev.Schedule))
		line("Template", name(ev.Template))
		if r := ev.Run; r != nil {
			line("Result", r.Result)
			line("Hosts", fmt.Sprintf("%d (ok %d, changed %d, failed %d)", r.Hosts, r.HostsOK, r.HostsChanged, r.HostsFailed))
			if r.StartedAt != nil {
				line("Started", r.StartedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC1123))
			}
			line("Duration", (time.Duration(r.DurationSeconds) * time.Second).String())
		}
	case EventScheduleSkipped, EventScheduleFailed:
		verb := "skipped"
		if ev.Kind == EventScheduleFailed {
			verb = "could not start"
		}
		subject = fmt.Sprintf("[Pulsix] %s: %s", name(ev.Schedule), verb)
		line("Schedule", name(ev.Schedule))
		line("Template", name(ev.Template))
		if d := ev.Decision; d != nil {
			line("Outcome", d.Outcome)
			line("Reason", d.Reason)
			line("Due", d.DueAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC1123))
		}
	case EventDigest:
		subject = fmt.Sprintf("[Pulsix] Weekly schedule digest for %s", ev.Org.Name)
		if d := ev.Digest; d != nil {
			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "Scheduled runs from %s to %s (%s)\n\n", d.From.Format("2 Jan"), d.To.Format("2 Jan 2006"), d.Timezone)
			if len(d.Schedules) == 0 {
				b.WriteString("No schedules.\n")
			}
			for _, s := range d.Schedules {
				fmt.Fprintf(&b, "%s: %d run(s), %d failure(s), %d skipped or missed\n", s.Name, s.Runs, s.Failures, s.Skipped)
			}
			if len(d.Slowest) > 0 {
				b.WriteString("\nSlowest runs:\n")
				for _, r := range d.Slowest {
					fmt.Fprintf(&b, "%s, %s: %s  %s\n", r.Schedule, r.StartedAt.UTC().Format("Mon 2 Jan 15:04 MST"), time.Duration(r.DurationSeconds)*time.Second, r.URL)
				}
			}
		}
	default:
		subject = fmt.Sprintf("[Pulsix] Test notification from %s", ev.Org.Name)
		b.WriteString("This is a test message from a Pulsix notification channel. It worked.\n")
	}
	if ev.URL != "" {
		fmt.Fprintf(&b, "\n%s\n", ev.URL)
	}
	return subject, b.String()
}
```

`internal/notify/service_test.go` (the fakes here are reused by every later notify test):

```go
package notify_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/notify"
)

var ctx = context.Background()

// fakeBox "seals" by prefixing the AAD, so tests can see what was sealed
// with which associated data, and refuses to open with the wrong one.
type fakeBox struct{}

func (fakeBox) SealAAD(plain, aad []byte) ([]byte, error) {
	return append(append(append([]byte{}, aad...), '|'), plain...), nil
}

func (fakeBox) OpenAAD(sealed, aad []byte) ([]byte, error) {
	prefix := append(append([]byte{}, aad...), '|')
	if !bytes.HasPrefix(sealed, prefix) {
		return nil, errors.New("wrong aad")
	}
	return sealed[len(prefix):], nil
}

type fakeMail struct {
	mu         sync.Mutex
	configured bool
	sent       [][]string
	err        error
}

func (m *fakeMail) SendMany(_ context.Context, to []string, subject, body string) error {
	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()
	if m.err != nil {
		return m.err
	}
	m.sent = append(m.sent, append(append([]string{}, to...), subject, body))
	return nil
}

func (m *fakeMail) Configured(context.Context) (bool, error) { return m.configured, nil }

type fakeChannels struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	byID map[uuid.UUID]notify.Channel
}

func newFakeChannels() *fakeChannels { return &fakeChannels{byID: map[uuid.UUID]notify.Channel{}} }

func (f *fakeChannels) CreateChannel(_ context.Context, c notify.Channel) (notify.Channel, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	for _, o := range f.byID {
		if o.OrgID == c.OrgID && o.Name == c.Name {
			return notify.Channel{}, notify.ErrNameTaken
		}
	}
	c.Revision = 1
	f.byID[c.ID] = c
	return c, nil
}

func (f *fakeChannels) GetChannel(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (notify.Channel, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	c, ok := f.byID[id]
	if !ok || c.OrgID != orgID {
		return notify.Channel{}, notify.ErrNotFound
	}
	return c, nil
}

func (f *fakeChannels) ListChannels(_ context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]notify.Channel, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	var out []notify.Channel
	for _, c := range f.byID {
		if c.OrgID == orgID {
			out = append(out, c)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (f *fakeChannels) UpdateChannel(_ context.Context, c notify.Channel, revision int64) (notify.Channel, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	cur, ok := f.byID[c.ID]
	if !ok {
		return notify.Channel{}, notify.ErrNotFound
	}
	if cur.Revision != revision {
		return notify.Channel{}, notify.ErrRevisionMismatch
	}
	c.Revision = revision + 1
	f.byID[c.ID] = c
	return c, nil
}

func (f *fakeChannels) DeleteChannel(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	if c, ok := f.byID[id]; !ok || c.OrgID != orgID {
		return notify.ErrNotFound
	}
	delete(f.byID, id)
	return nil
}

type world struct {
	svc      *notify.Service
	channels *fakeChannels
	mail     *fakeMail
	org      uuid.UUID
	actor    uuid.UUID
}

func newWorld(t *testing.T) *world {
	t.Helper()
	w := &world{channels: newFakeChannels(), mail: &fakeMail{configured: true}, org: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), actor: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())}
	w.svc = notify.NewService(notify.Deps{
		Channels: w.channels, Box: fakeBox{}, Mail: w.mail,
		Now: func() time.Time { return time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC) }, Rand: rand.Reader,
	})
	return w
}

func str(s string) *string { return &s }

func webhookInput(name string) notify.Input {
	return notify.Input{Kind: notify.KindWebhook, Name: name, Enabled: true, Webhook: &notify.WebhookInput{
		URL:           str("https://hooks.example.test/services/T0/B0/xyz"),
		Headers:       []notify.HeaderInput{{Name: "Authorization", Value: str("Bearer t0ken")}},
		BodyTemplate:  `{"text": {{json .Schedule.Name}}}`,
		Signed:        true,
		SigningSecret: str("0123456789abcdef0123"),
	}}
}

func TestCreateEmailChannel(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	c, err := w.svc.CreateChannel(ctx, w.org, w.actor, notify.Input{Kind: notify.KindEmail, Name: "ops mail", Enabled: true, Email: &notify.EmailConfig{Recipients: []string{"ops@example.test"}}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	email, hook, err := c.View()
	if err != nil || hook != nil || email.Recipients[0] != "ops@example.test" || *c.CreatedBy != w.actor {
		t.Fatalf("view = %+v %+v %v", email, hook, err)
	}
	w.mail.configured = false
	if _, err := w.svc.CreateChannel(ctx, w.org, w.actor, notify.Input{Kind: notify.KindEmail, Name: "second", Email: &notify.EmailConfig{Recipients: []string{"a@example.test"}}}); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrMailUnavailable) {
		t.Fatalf("e-mail channel without instance mail = %v, want ErrMailUnavailable", err)
	}
	if ok, _ := w.svc.MailAvailable(ctx); ok {
		t.Fatal("MailAvailable must follow the instance settings")
	}
}

func TestCreateWebhookChannelSealsEverySecret(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	c, err := w.svc.CreateChannel(ctx, w.org, w.actor, webhookInput("ops hook"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, secret := range []string{"services/T0/B0/xyz", "Bearer t0ken", "0123456789abcdef0123"} {
		if bytes.Contains(c.Config, []byte(secret)) {
			t.Fatalf("stored config carries %q in the clear: %s", secret, c.Config)
		}
	}
	var stored struct {
		URLSealed []byte `json:"url_sealed"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(c.Config, &stored); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if url, err := (fakeBox{}).OpenAAD(stored.URLSealed, notify.AAD(w.org, c.ID)); err != nil || string(url) != "https://hooks.example.test/services/T0/B0/xyz" {
		t.Fatalf("the url is not sealed with the channel's AAD: %q, %v", url, err)
	}
	_, hook, err := c.View()
	if err != nil || hook.URLDisplay != "https://hooks.example.test/…" || hook.Method != "POST" || !hook.Signed ||
		len(hook.HeaderNames) != 1 || hook.HeaderNames[0] != "Authorization" {
		t.Fatalf("view = %+v, %v", hook, err)
	}
}

func TestUpdateWebhookKeepsSecretsLeftOut(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	c, err := w.svc.CreateChannel(ctx, w.org, w.actor, webhookInput("ops hook"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	in := webhookInput("ops hook renamed")
	in.Webhook.URL, in.Webhook.SigningSecret = nil, nil
	in.Webhook.Headers = []notify.HeaderInput{{Name: "authorization"}}
	up, err := w.svc.UpdateChannel(ctx, w.org, c.ID, c.Revision, in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !bytes.Contains(up.Config, []byte(`"url_display":"https://hooks.example.test/…"`)) {
		t.Fatalf("url not kept: %s", up.Config)
	}
	_, hook, _ := up.View()
	if !hook.Signed || hook.HeaderNames[0] != "authorization" || up.Name != "ops hook renamed" {
		t.Fatalf("updated view = %+v", hook)
	}
	// A new header without a value, signing without any secret, a kind
	// change and a stale revision are refused.
	in.Webhook.Headers = []notify.HeaderInput{{Name: "X-New"}}
	if _, err := w.svc.UpdateChannel(ctx, w.org, c.ID, up.Revision, in); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrInvalidInput) {
		t.Fatalf("header without value = %v", err)
	}
	in.Kind = notify.KindEmail
	if _, err := w.svc.UpdateChannel(ctx, w.org, c.ID, up.Revision, in); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrInvalidInput) {
		t.Fatalf("kind change = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.UpdateChannel(ctx, w.org, c.ID, c.Revision, webhookInput("x")); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrRevisionMismatch) {
		t.Fatalf("stale revision = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestWebhookInputRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	cases := map[string]func(in *notify.Input){
		"no url":  func(in *notify.Input) { in.Webhook.URL = nil },
		"bad url": func(in *notify.Input) { in.Webhook.URL = str("ftp://x.test") },
		"header twice": func(in *notify.Input) {
			in.Webhook.Headers = append(in.Webhook.Headers, notify.HeaderInput{Name: "AUTHORIZATION", Value: str("x")})
		},
		"reserved header": func(in *notify.Input) {
			in.Webhook.Headers = []notify.HeaderInput{{Name: "X-Pulsix-Event", Value: str("x")}}
		},
		"broken template":    func(in *notify.Input) { in.Webhook.BodyTemplate = "{{.Nope}}" },
		"signed, no secret":  func(in *notify.Input) { in.Webhook.SigningSecret = nil },
		"short secret":       func(in *notify.Input) { in.Webhook.SigningSecret = str("short") },
		"email settings too": func(in *notify.Input) { in.Email = &notify.EmailConfig{} },
		"bad method":         func(in *notify.Input) { in.Webhook.Method = "DELETE" },
		"bad name":           func(in *notify.Input) { in.Name = " " },
		"unknown kind":       func(in *notify.Input) { in.Kind = "sms" },
	}
	for name, mutate := range cases {
		in := webhookInput("hook " + name)
		mutate(&in)
		if _, err := w.svc.CreateChannel(ctx, w.org, w.actor, in); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrInvalidInput) {
			t.Errorf("%s: err = %v, want ErrInvalidInput", name, err)
		}
	}
}

func TestChannelListGetDelete(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	c, err := w.svc.CreateChannel(ctx, w.org, w.actor, webhookInput("ops hook"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.CreateChannel(ctx, w.org, w.actor, webhookInput("ops hook")); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrNameTaken) {
		t.Fatalf("duplicate name = %v", err)
	}
	if list, err := w.svc.ListChannels(ctx, w.org); err != nil || len(list) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("list = %v, %v", list, err)
	}
	if got, err := w.svc.GetChannel(ctx, w.org, c.ID); err != nil || got.ID != c.ID {
		t.Fatalf("get = %v", err)
	}
	if err := w.svc.DeleteChannel(ctx, w.org, c.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.GetChannel(ctx, w.org, c.ID); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("after delete = %v", err)
	}
}
```

`internal/notify/service.go`:

```go
package notify

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// ChannelStore persists channels (org-scoped).
type ChannelStore interface {
	CreateChannel(ctx context.Context, c Channel) (Channel, error)
	GetChannel(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Channel, error)
	ListChannels(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Channel, error)
	UpdateChannel(ctx context.Context, c Channel, revision int64) (Channel, error)
	DeleteChannel(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error
}

// Sealer seals and opens channel secrets (crypto.Box).
type Sealer interface {
	SealAAD(plain, aad []byte) ([]byte, error)
	OpenAAD(sealed, aad []byte) ([]byte, error)
}

// Mailer is the instance SMTP (mail.SMTP).
type Mailer interface {
	SendMany(ctx context.Context, to []string, subject, body string) error
	Configured(ctx context.Context) (bool, error)
}

// Deps wires the service. Now, Rand and Log default when nil.
type Deps struct {
	Channels ChannelStore
	Box      Sealer
	Mail     Mailer
	Now      func() time.Time
	Rand     io.Reader
	Log      *slog.Logger
}

// Service manages channels.
type Service struct{ d Deps }

// NewService wires the service.
func NewService(d Deps) *Service {
	if d.Now == nil {
		d.Now = time.Now
	}
	if d.Rand == nil {
		d.Rand = rand.Reader
	}
	if d.Log == nil {
		d.Log = slog.Default()
	}
	return &Service{d: d}
}

func (s *Service) newID() uuid.UUID {
	id, err := uuid.NewV7FromReader(s.d.Rand)
	if err != nil {
		panic(err) //nolint:forbidigo // entropy failure is unrecoverable, like uuid.Must
	}
	return id
}

// MailAvailable reports whether e-mail channels can be used on this
// instance (it has outgoing mail settings).
func (s *Service) MailAvailable(ctx context.Context) (bool, error) {
	return s.d.Mail.Configured(ctx)
}

// CreateChannel validates, seals and stores a new channel.
func (s *Service) CreateChannel(ctx context.Context, orgID, actor uuid.UUID, in Input) (Channel, error) {
	id := s.newID()
	name, err := normalizeName(in.Name)
	if err != nil {
		return Channel{}, err
	}
	if in.Kind == KindEmail {
		ok, err := s.d.Mail.Configured(ctx)
		if err != nil {
			return Channel{}, fmt.Errorf("check outgoing mail: %w", err)
		}
		if !ok {
			return Channel{}, ErrMailUnavailable
		}
	}
	cfg, err := s.buildConfig(orgID, id, in, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return Channel{}, err
	}
	return s.d.Channels.CreateChannel(ctx, Channel{
		ID: id, OrgID: orgID, Kind: in.Kind, Name: name, Enabled: in.Enabled, Config: cfg, CreatedBy: &actor,
	})
}

// UpdateChannel replaces a channel's settings when revision is current.
// The kind never changes; secrets submitted as nil keep their stored value.
func (s *Service) UpdateChannel(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, revision int64, in Input) (Channel, error) {
	cur, err := s.d.Channels.GetChannel(ctx, orgID, id)
	if err != nil {
		return Channel{}, err
	}
	if cur.Revision != revision {
		return Channel{}, ErrRevisionMismatch
	}
	if in.Kind != cur.Kind {
		return Channel{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: the kind of a channel cannot change; create a new channel instead", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	name, err := normalizeName(in.Name)
	if err != nil {
		return Channel{}, err
	}
	cfg, err := s.buildConfig(orgID, id, in, &cur)
	if err != nil {
		return Channel{}, err
	}
	cur.Name, cur.Enabled, cur.Config = name, in.Enabled, cfg
	return s.d.Channels.UpdateChannel(ctx, cur, revision)
}

func (s *Service) GetChannel(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Channel, error) {
	return s.d.Channels.GetChannel(ctx, orgID, id)
}

func (s *Service) ListChannels(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Channel, error) {
	return s.d.Channels.ListChannels(ctx, orgID)
}

// DeleteChannel removes a channel; its rules, digest membership and
// delivery log go with it (foreign key cascades).
func (s *Service) DeleteChannel(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	return s.d.Channels.DeleteChannel(ctx, orgID, id)
}

// buildConfig turns an input into the stored config. cur is the channel
// being updated (nil on create): its sealed values are kept where the
// input leaves them out.
func (s *Service) buildConfig(orgID, id uuid.UUID, in Input, cur *Channel) (json.RawMessage, error) {
	switch in.Kind {
	case KindEmail:
		if in.Webhook != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: an e-mail channel takes no webhook settings", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
		e, err := normalizeEmail(in.Email)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		return json.Marshal(e)
	case KindWebhook:
		if in.Email != nil || in.Webhook == nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: a webhook channel takes webhook settings only", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
		var stored webhookConfig
		if cur != nil {
			if err := json.Unmarshal(cur.Config, &stored); err != nil {
				return nil, fmt.Errorf("decode stored webhook channel: %w", err)
			}
		}
		w, err := s.buildWebhook(AAD(orgID, id), *in.Webhook, stored, cur != nil)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		return json.Marshal(w)
	}
	return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: kind must be email or webhook", ErrInvalidInput)
}

func (s *Service) buildWebhook(aad []byte, in WebhookInput, stored webhookConfig, update bool) (webhookConfig, error) {
	var out webhookConfig
	var err error
	if out.Method, err = normalizeMethod(in.Method); err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	switch {
	case in.URL != nil:
		if out.URLDisplay, err = checkURL(*in.URL); err != nil {
			return out, err
		}
		if out.URLSealed, err = s.d.Box.SealAAD([]byte(strings.TrimSpace(*in.URL)), aad); err != nil {
			return out, fmt.Errorf("seal webhook url: %w", err)
		}
	case update:
		out.URLSealed, out.URLDisplay = stored.URLSealed, stored.URLDisplay
	default:
		return out, fmt.Errorf("%w: a webhook channel needs a url", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	if len(in.Headers) > maxHeaders {
		return out, fmt.Errorf("%w: at most %d headers", ErrInvalidInput, maxHeaders)
	}
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, h := range in.Headers {
		if err := checkHeader(h.Name, h.Value); err != nil {
			return out, err
		}
		key := strings.ToLower(h.Name)
		if seen[key] {
			return out, fmt.Errorf("%w: header %s is listed twice", ErrInvalidInput, h.Name)
		}
		seen[key] = true
		sh := storedHeader{Name: h.Name}
		if h.Value != nil {
			if sh.ValueSealed, err = s.d.Box.SealAAD([]byte(*h.Value), aad); err != nil {
				return out, fmt.Errorf("seal header value: %w", err)
			}
		} else {
			kept := false
			for _, old := range stored.Headers {
				if strings.EqualFold(old.Name, h.Name) {
					sh.ValueSealed, kept = old.ValueSealed, true
				}
			}
			if !kept {
				return out, fmt.Errorf("%w: header %s needs a value", ErrInvalidInput, h.Name)
			}
		}
		out.Headers = append(out.Headers, sh)
	}
	if err := checkBodyTemplate(in.BodyTemplate); err != nil {
		return out, err
	}
	out.BodyTemplate = in.BodyTemplate
	if in.Signed {
		switch {
		case in.SigningSecret != nil:
			if err := checkSigningSecret(*in.SigningSecret); err != nil {
				return out, err
			}
			if out.SigningSealed, err = s.d.Box.SealAAD([]byte(*in.SigningSecret), aad); err != nil {
				return out, fmt.Errorf("seal signing secret: %w", err)
			}
		case len(stored.SigningSealed) > 0:
			out.SigningSealed = stored.SigningSealed
		default:
			return out, fmt.Errorf("%w: signed requests need a signing secret", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

```go
package notify_test

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/testdb"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/notify"
)

func seedOrg(t *testing.T, d *db.DB, slug string) uuid.UUID {
	t.Helper()
	o, err := gen.New(d.Pool()).CreateOrganization(ctx, gen.CreateOrganizationParams{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Slug: slug, Name: slug})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return o.ID
}

func TestChannelStoreRoundTripAndIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	orgA, orgB := seedOrg(t, d, "notify-a"), seedOrg(t, d, "notify-b")
	st := notify.NewSQLStore(d)
	scopedA, scopedB := db.WithOrgScope(ctx, orgA), db.WithOrgScope(ctx, orgB)
	cfg, _ := json.Marshal(notify.EmailConfig{Recipients: []string{"ops@example.test"}})
	c, err := st.CreateChannel(scopedA, notify.Channel{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: orgA, Kind: notify.KindEmail, Name: "ops", Enabled: true, Config: cfg})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if c.Revision != 1 || c.Kind != notify.KindEmail || c.CreatedAt.IsZero() {
		t.Fatalf("created = %+v", c)
	}
	if _, err := st.CreateChannel(scopedA, notify.Channel{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: orgA, Kind: notify.KindEmail, Name: "ops", Config: cfg}); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrNameTaken) {
		t.Fatalf("duplicate name = %v", err)
	}
	// Another organization's scope sees nothing, even asking with orgA's id.
	if _, err := st.GetChannel(scopedB, orgA, c.ID); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("cross-org get = %v", err)
	}
	if list, err := st.ListChannels(scopedB, orgA); err != nil || len(list) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("cross-org list = %v, %v", list, err)
	}
	c.Name, c.Enabled = "ops mail", false
	up, err := st.UpdateChannel(scopedA, c, 1)
	if err != nil || up.Revision != 2 || up.Name != "ops mail" || up.Enabled {
		t.Fatalf("update = %+v, %v", up, err)
	}
	if _, err := st.UpdateChannel(scopedA, c, 1); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrRevisionMismatch) {
		t.Fatalf("stale update = %v", err)
	}
	c.ID = uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	if _, err := st.UpdateChannel(scopedA, c, 1); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown update = %v", err)
	}
	if err := st.DeleteChannel(scopedA, orgA, up.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := st.DeleteChannel(scopedA, orgA, up.ID); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("second delete = %v", err)
	}
}
```

`internal/notify/store_sql.go`:

```go
package notify

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
)

// SQLStore is the org-scoped notification store.
type SQLStore struct {
	q *gen.Queries
}

// NewSQLStore builds the store on *db.DB so every statement runs in the
// request's org scope.
func NewSQLStore(d *db.DB) *SQLStore { return &SQLStore{q: gen.New(d)} }

func (st *SQLStore) CreateChannel(ctx context.Context, c Channel) (Channel, error) {
	row, err := st.q.CreateNotificationChannel(ctx, gen.CreateNotificationChannelParams{
		ID: c.ID, OrgID: c.OrgID, Kind: string(c.Kind), Name: c.Name, Config: c.Config, Enabled: c.Enabled,
		CreatedBy: db.NullUUID(c.CreatedBy),
	})
	if db.IsUniqueViolation(err) {
		return Channel{}, ErrNameTaken
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Channel{}, fmt.Errorf("create notification channel: %w", err)
	}
	return toChannel(row), nil
}

func (st *SQLStore) GetChannel(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Channel, error) {
	row, err := st.q.GetNotificationChannel(ctx, gen.GetNotificationChannelParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return Channel{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Channel{}, fmt.Errorf("get notification channel: %w", err)
	}
	return toChannel(row), nil
}

func (st *SQLStore) ListChannels(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Channel, error) {
	rows, err := st.q.ListNotificationChannels(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list notification channels: %w", err)
	}
	out := make([]Channel, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		out[i] = toChannel(r)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (st *SQLStore) UpdateChannel(ctx context.Context, c Channel, revision int64) (Channel, error) {
	row, err := st.q.UpdateNotificationChannel(ctx, gen.UpdateNotificationChannelParams{
		Name: c.Name, Config: c.Config, Enabled: c.Enabled, OrgID: c.OrgID, ID: c.ID, Revision: revision,
	})
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		if _, gerr := st.GetChannel(ctx, c.OrgID, c.ID); gerr != nil {
			return Channel{}, gerr
		}
		return Channel{}, ErrRevisionMismatch
	}
	if db.IsUniqueViolation(err) {
		return Channel{}, ErrNameTaken
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Channel{}, fmt.Errorf("update notification channel: %w", err)
	}
	return toChannel(row), nil
}

func (st *SQLStore) DeleteChannel(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	n, err := st.q.DeleteNotificationChannel(ctx, gen.DeleteNotificationChannelParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("delete notification channel: %w", err)
	}
	if n == 0 {
		return ErrNotFound
	}
	return nil
}

func toChannel(r gen.NotificationChannel) Channel {
	return Channel{
		ID: r.ID, OrgID: r.OrgID, Kind: Kind(r.Kind), Name: r.Name, Enabled: r.Enabled, Config: r.Config,
		CreatedBy: db.UUIDPtr(r.CreatedBy), CreatedAt: r.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: r.UpdatedAt, Revision: r.Revision,
	}
}
```

### Task 3: The delivery queue: senders, dispatcher and Send test

This task covers spec §5's delivery. `ChannelSender` turns an event into an e-mail or a signed webhook request, and marks failures a retry cannot fix as permanent. The dispatcher claims due deliveries across organizations with `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` and a lease. It sends them in parallel and records sent, retry (with backoff) or failed, fenced by the attempt count. "Send test" sends synchronously and records the attempt in the channel's delivery log. The manager starts the dispatcher.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/notify/send.go`, `deliveries.go`, `dispatch.go`, `dispatch_sql.go`
- Test: `internal/notify/send_test.go`, `dispatch_test.go`, `dispatch_sql_test.go`, `deliveries_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/notify/service.go` (`Deps.Deliveries`, `Deps.Sender`, `Deps.PublicURL`), `internal/notify/store_sql.go` (`InsertDelivery`, `ListDeliveries`)
- Modify: `internal/db/queries/notifications.sql` (delivery queries), regenerate `internal/db/gen`
- Modify: `internal/app/app.go` (the sender and the dispatcher loop, `App.stopDispatch`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `outbound.NewClient`, `outbound.ErrBlocked`, `outbound.ErrRedirect`, `mail.ErrNotConfigured`. Task 2's `Channel`, `Event`, `AAD`, `renderEmail`, `renderWebhookBody`, `sampleEvent`, `webhookConfig`.
- Produces (package `notify`):
  - `func Permanent(err error) error`, `func IsPermanent(err error) bool`
  - `type ChannelSender`, `func NewChannelSender(box Sealer, m Mailer, client *http.Client, now func() time.Time) *ChannelSender`, `func (s *ChannelSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch Channel, deliveryID uuid.UUID, ev Event) error`
  - `type DeliveryStatus string` (`DeliveryPending`, `DeliverySent`, `DeliveryFailed`); `type Delivery struct{ ID, OrgID, ChannelID uuid.UUID; EventKey string; EventKind EventKind; Payload json.RawMessage; Status DeliveryStatus; Attempts int; NextAttemptAt time.Time; LastError string; CreatedAt time.Time; SentAt *time.Time }`
  - `type DeliveryLog interface{ InsertDelivery(ctx, d Delivery) error; ListDeliveries(ctx, orgID, channelID uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]Delivery, error) }` (implemented by `SQLStore`)
  - `Deps` gains `Deliveries DeliveryLog`, `Sender Sender`, `PublicURL string`
  - `Service.SendTest(ctx context.Context, org OrgRef, channelID uuid.UUID) (Delivery, error)`, `Service.Deliveries(ctx, orgID, channelID uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]Delivery, error)`
  - `const MaxAttempts = 6`, `const DefaultDispatchInterval = 2 * time.Second`
  - `type Claimed struct{ Delivery Delivery; Channel Channel }`, `type DeliveryQueue interface{ Claim; Complete; Retry; Fail }`, `type Sender interface{ Send(ctx, ch Channel, deliveryID uuid.UUID, ev Event) error }`
  - `type Dispatcher`, `func NewDispatcher(queue DeliveryQueue, sender Sender, interval time.Duration, now func() time.Time, sleep func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error, log *slog.Logger) *Dispatcher`, `Run(ctx)`, `Tick(ctx) (int, error)`
  - `type DeliverySQLStore`, `func NewDeliverySQLStore(d *db.DB) *DeliverySQLStore`
  - unexported, used by Task 9: `errorText`; test helpers used later: `seedChannel`, `countingSender`, `quiet()`.

- [ ] **Step 1: The delivery queries**

Append to `internal/db/queries/notifications.sql`:

```sql
-- name: InsertNotificationDelivery :execrows
-- ON CONFLICT: the (org, event, channel) idempotency key — a retried
-- source (a finish retry, a re-decided schedule row) enqueues nothing new.
INSERT INTO notification_deliveries (id, org_id, channel_id, event_key, event_kind, payload, status, attempts,
  next_attempt_at, last_error, created_at, sent_at)
VALUES (@id, @org_id, @channel_id, @event_key, @event_kind, @payload, @status, @attempts,
  @next_attempt_at, @last_error, @created_at, sqlc.narg(sent_at))
ON CONFLICT (org_id, event_key, channel_id) DO NOTHING;

-- name: ListNotificationDeliveries :many
SELECT * FROM notification_deliveries WHERE org_id = @org_id AND channel_id = @channel_id
ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT @lim;

-- name: ClaimNotificationDeliveries :many
-- The dispatcher's claim: runs on the raw pool without pulsix.org_id
-- (bypass policy), across every organization. SKIP LOCKED lets several
-- managers dispatch without a leader; the lease (locked_until) and the
-- attempts increment are written in the same statement, so a manager that
-- dies mid-send leaves a row that becomes due again once the lease ends.
UPDATE notification_deliveries AS d
SET locked_until = @lease_until::timestamptz, attempts = d.attempts + 1
FROM notification_channels AS c
WHERE c.org_id = d.org_id AND c.id = d.channel_id
  AND d.id IN (
    SELECT p.id FROM notification_deliveries AS p
    WHERE p.status = 'pending' AND p.next_attempt_at <= @now::timestamptz
      AND (p.locked_until IS NULL OR p.locked_until <= @now::timestamptz)
    ORDER BY p.next_attempt_at
    LIMIT @lim
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
  )
RETURNING d.id, d.org_id, d.channel_id, d.event_key, d.event_kind, d.payload, d.attempts, d.created_at,
  c.kind AS channel_kind, c.name AS channel_name, c.config AS channel_config, c.enabled AS channel_enabled;

-- name: CompleteNotificationDelivery :execrows
-- attempts fences a late completion: a manager whose lease ran out and
-- whose row another manager re-claimed changes nothing.
UPDATE notification_deliveries SET status = 'sent', sent_at = @at, locked_until = NULL, last_error = ''
WHERE org_id = @org_id AND id = @id AND attempts = @attempts AND status = 'pending';

-- name: RetryNotificationDelivery :execrows
UPDATE notification_deliveries SET next_attempt_at = @next_attempt_at, locked_until = NULL, last_error = @last_error
WHERE org_id = @org_id AND id = @id AND attempts = @attempts AND status = 'pending';

-- name: FailNotificationDelivery :execrows
UPDATE notification_deliveries SET status = 'failed', locked_until = NULL, last_error = @last_error
WHERE org_id = @org_id AND id = @id AND attempts = @attempts AND status = 'pending';

-- name: TrimNotificationDeliveries :exec
-- Keeps the newest 500 finished rows of a channel; pending ones are never
-- trimmed.
DELETE FROM notification_deliveries AS d
WHERE d.org_id = @org_id AND d.channel_id = @channel_id AND d.status <> 'pending'
  AND d.id NOT IN (
    SELECT k.id FROM notification_deliveries AS k
    WHERE k.org_id = @org_id AND k.channel_id = @channel_id AND k.status <> 'pending'
    ORDER BY k.created_at DESC, k.id DESC
    LIMIT @keep
  );
```

Run: `make sqlc && go build ./...`
Expected: green; `gen.ClaimNotificationDeliveriesRow` carries the channel's kind, name, config and enabled flag.

```go
package notify_test

import (
	"crypto/hmac"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"net/netip"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/notify"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/outbound"
)

// receiver records what a webhook endpoint got and answers with status.
type receiver struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	status  int
	headers []http.Header
	bodies  [][]byte
}

func (r *receiver) ServeHTTP(w http.ResponseWriter, req *http.Request) {
	body, _ := io.ReadAll(req.Body)
	r.mu.Lock()
	r.headers = append(r.headers, req.Header.Clone())
	r.bodies = append(r.bodies, body)
	status := r.status
	r.mu.Unlock()
	if status == 0 {
		status = http.StatusNoContent
	}
	w.WriteHeader(status)
}

func loopbackClient() *http.Client {
	return outbound.NewClient(outbound.NewPolicy([]netip.Prefix{netip.MustParsePrefix("127.0.0.0/8")}), 2*time.Second, nil)
}

func webhookChannel(t *testing.T, w *world, url, template string, signed bool) notify.Channel {
	t.Helper()
	in := webhookInput("hook " + uuid.NewString()[:8])
	in.Webhook.URL, in.Webhook.BodyTemplate, in.Webhook.Signed = &url, template, signed
	if !signed {
		in.Webhook.SigningSecret = nil
	}
	c, err := w.svc.CreateChannel(ctx, w.org, w.actor, in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return c
}

func TestWebhookSendHeadersSignatureAndBody(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	rec := &receiver{}
	srv := httptest.NewServer(rec)
	defer srv.Close()
	c := webhookChannel(t, w, srv.URL+"/hook?token=abc", `{"text": {{json .Schedule.Name}}}`, true)
	now := time.Unix(1790000000, 0)
	sender := notify.NewChannelSender(fakeBox{}, w.mail, loopbackClient(), func() time.Time { return now })
	delivery := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	ev := notify.Event{Kind: notify.EventRunFinished, Schedule: &notify.NamedRef{Name: "Nightly"}}
	if err := sender.Send(ctx, c, delivery, ev); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h, body := rec.headers[0], rec.bodies[0]
	if string(body) != `{"text": "Nightly"}` || h.Get("Authorization") != "Bearer t0ken" || h.Get("Content-Type") != "application/json" ||
		h.Get("X-Pulsix-Event") != "run.finished" || h.Get("X-Pulsix-Delivery") != delivery.String() || h.Get("X-Pulsix-Timestamp") != "1790000000" {
		t.Fatalf("headers %v, body %s", h, body)
	}
	mac := hmac.New(sha256.New, []byte("0123456789abcdef0123"))
	mac.Write([]byte("1790000000." + string(body)))
	if h.Get("X-Pulsix-Signature-256") != "sha256="+hex.EncodeToString(mac.Sum(nil)) {
		t.Fatalf("signature = %q", h.Get("X-Pulsix-Signature-256"))
	}
}

func TestWebhookSendDefaultBodyIsTheEvent(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	rec := &receiver{}
	srv := httptest.NewServer(rec)
	defer srv.Close()
	c := webhookChannel(t, w, srv.URL, "", false)
	sender := notify.NewChannelSender(fakeBox{}, w.mail, loopbackClient(), nil)

		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var got notify.Event
	if err := json.Unmarshal(rec.bodies[0], &got); err != nil || got.Org.Slug != "acme" || rec.headers[0].Get("X-Pulsix-Signature-256") != "" {
		t.Fatalf("body %s, signature %q", rec.bodies[0], rec.headers[0].Get("X-Pulsix-Signature-256"))
	}
}

func TestWebhookSendClassifiesFailures(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	cases := []struct {
		status    int
		permanent bool
	}{
		{http.StatusInternalServerError, false},
		{http.StatusServiceUnavailable, false},
		{http.StatusTooManyRequests, false},
		{http.StatusRequestTimeout, false},
		{http.StatusBadRequest, true},
		{http.StatusNotFound, true},
		{http.StatusFound, true}, // redirects are never followed
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		rec := &receiver{status: c.status}
		srv := httptest.NewServer(rec)
		ch := webhookChannel(t, w, srv.URL, "", false)
		err := notify.NewChannelSender(fakeBox{}, w.mail, loopbackClient(), nil).Send(ctx, ch, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), notify.Event{Kind: notify.EventTest})
		srv.Close()
		if err == nil || notify.IsPermanent(err) != c.permanent {
			t.Errorf("status %d: err = %v, permanent = %v", c.status, err, notify.IsPermanent(err))
			continue
		}
		if strings.Contains(err.Error(), srv.URL) {
			t.Errorf("status %d: the error repeats the URL: %v", c.status, err)
		}
	}
}

func TestWebhookSendRefusesLoopbackWithoutAllowlist(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	rec := &receiver{}
	srv := httptest.NewServer(rec)
	defer srv.Close()

	client := outbound.NewClient(outbound.NewPolicy(nil), time.Second, nil)
	err := notify.NewChannelSender(fakeBox{}, w.mail, client, nil).Send(ctx, ch, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), notify.Event{Kind: notify.EventTest})
	if !notify.IsPermanent(err) || !errors.Is(err, outbound.ErrBlocked) || strings.Contains(err.Error(), "secret-path") {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v, want a permanent ErrBlocked without the URL", err)
	}
	if len(rec.bodies) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("a blocked destination was contacted")
	}
}

func TestWebhookSendTransportFailureIsRetryable(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)

	srv.Close() // nothing listens any more
	ch := webhookChannel(t, w, url, "", false)
	err := notify.NewChannelSender(fakeBox{}, w.mail, loopbackClient(), nil).Send(ctx, ch, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), notify.Event{Kind: notify.EventTest})
	if err == nil || notify.IsPermanent(err) || strings.Contains(err.Error(), url) {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v, want a retryable error without the URL", err)

func TestEmailSend(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	c, err := w.svc.CreateChannel(ctx, w.org, w.actor, notify.Input{Kind: notify.KindEmail, Name: "ops", Enabled: true, Email: &notify.EmailConfig{Recipients: []string{"ops@example.test", "dba@example.test"}}})
	if err != nil {

	ev := notify.Event{Kind: notify.EventTest, Org: notify.OrgRef{Name: "Acme"}}
	if err := sender.Send(ctx, c, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), ev); err != nil {

	if got := w.mail.sent[0]; len(got) != 4 || got[1] != "dba@example.test" || got[2] != "[Pulsix] Test notification from Acme" {
		t.Fatalf("sent = %v", got)
	}
	w.mail.err = errors.New("421 try later")
	if err := sender.Send(ctx, c, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), ev); err == nil || notify.IsPermanent(err) {
		t.Fatalf("an SMTP failure = %v, want retryable", err)
	}
	w.mail.err = mail.ErrNotConfigured
	if err := sender.Send(ctx, c, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), ev); !notify.IsPermanent(err) {
		t.Fatalf("settings removed mid-send = %v, want permanent", err)
	}
	w.mail.err, w.mail.configured = nil, false
	if err := sender.Send(ctx, c, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), ev); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrMailUnavailable) || !notify.IsPermanent(err) {
		t.Fatalf("no instance mail = %v, want permanent ErrMailUnavailable", err)
	}
}

func TestSendRefusesBrokenChannels(t *testing.T) {
	sender := notify.NewChannelSender(fakeBox{}, &fakeMail{configured: true}, loopbackClient(), nil)
	for _, ch := range []notify.Channel{
		{Kind: "sms"},
		{Kind: notify.KindEmail, Config: []byte(`{`)},
		{Kind: notify.KindWebhook, Config: []byte(`{"url_sealed":"bm9wZQ=="}`)}, // opens with the wrong AAD
	} {
		if err := sender.Send(ctx, ch, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), notify.Event{}); !notify.IsPermanent(err) {
			t.Errorf("channel %+v: err = %v, want permanent", ch, err)
		}
	}
	if notify.Permanent(nil) != nil {
		t.Fatal("Permanent(nil) must stay nil")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The senders**

`internal/notify/send.go`:

```go
package notify

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"crypto/hmac"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"net/url"
	"strconv"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/mail"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/outbound"
)

// emailTimeout bounds one e-mail send (the whole SMTP conversation).
const emailTimeout = 30 * time.Second

// maxErrorText bounds the error a delivery row keeps.
const maxErrorText = 500

// permanentError marks a failure a retry cannot fix: a refused
// destination, a redirect, a 4xx answer, an instance without mail, a body
// template that does not render. The dispatcher fails such a delivery at
// once instead of retrying it.
type permanentError struct{ err error }

func (e permanentError) Error() string { return e.err.Error() }
func (e permanentError) Unwrap() error { return e.err }

// Permanent marks err as not worth retrying.
func Permanent(err error) error {
	if err == nil {
		return nil
	}
	return permanentError{err: err}
}

// IsPermanent reports whether err was marked with Permanent.
func IsPermanent(err error) bool {
	var p permanentError
	return errors.As(err, &p)
}

// ChannelSender delivers one event through one channel.
type ChannelSender struct {
	box  Sealer
	mail Mailer
	http *http.Client
	now  func() time.Time
}

// NewChannelSender wires the sender. client must be an outbound client
// (internal/outbound): it is the one that refuses internal destinations.
func NewChannelSender(box Sealer, m Mailer, client *http.Client, now func() time.Time) *ChannelSender {
	if now == nil {
		now = time.Now
	}
	return &ChannelSender{box: box, mail: m, http: client, now: now}
}

// Send delivers ev through ch. deliveryID travels as X-Pulsix-Delivery so
// a receiver can drop the duplicate an at-least-once retry may produce.
func (s *ChannelSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch Channel, deliveryID uuid.UUID, ev Event) error {
	switch ch.Kind {
	case KindEmail:
		var cfg EmailConfig
		if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {
			return Permanent(fmt.Errorf("decode e-mail channel: %w", err))
		}
		return s.sendEmail(ctx, cfg, ev)
	case KindWebhook:
		w, err := s.openWebhook(ch)
		if err != nil {
			return Permanent(err)
		}
		return s.sendWebhook(ctx, w, deliveryID, ev)
	}
	return Permanent(fmt.Errorf("unknown channel kind %q", ch.Kind))
}

func (s *ChannelSender) sendEmail(ctx context.Context, cfg EmailConfig, ev Event) error {
	ok, err := s.mail.Configured(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("check outgoing mail: %w", err)
	}
	if !ok {
		return Permanent(ErrMailUnavailable)
	}
	subject, body := renderEmail(ev)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, emailTimeout)
	defer cancel()
	if err := s.mail.SendMany(ctx, cfg.Recipients, subject, body); err != nil {
		if errors.Is(err, mail.ErrNotConfigured) {
			return Permanent(ErrMailUnavailable)
		}
		return fmt.Errorf("send e-mail: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

// openedWebhook is a webhook channel with its secrets opened, only ever
// held for the duration of one send.
type openedWebhook struct {
	method, url, bodyTemplate string
	headers                   [][2]string
	signingSecret             []byte
}

func (s *ChannelSender) openWebhook(ch Channel) (openedWebhook, error) {
	var w webhookConfig
	if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &w); err != nil {
		return openedWebhook{}, fmt.Errorf("decode webhook channel: %w", err)
	}
	aad := AAD(ch.OrgID, ch.ID)
	u, err := s.box.OpenAAD(w.URLSealed, aad)
	if err != nil {
		return openedWebhook{}, fmt.Errorf("open webhook url: %w", err)
	}
	out := openedWebhook{method: w.Method, url: string(u), bodyTemplate: w.BodyTemplate}
	for _, h := range w.Headers {
		v, err := s.box.OpenAAD(h.ValueSealed, aad)
		if err != nil {
			return openedWebhook{}, fmt.Errorf("open header %s: %w", h.Name, err)
		}
		out.headers = append(out.headers, [2]string{h.Name, string(v)})
	}
	if len(w.SigningSealed) > 0 {
		if out.signingSecret, err = s.box.OpenAAD(w.SigningSealed, aad); err != nil {
			return openedWebhook{}, fmt.Errorf("open signing secret: %w", err)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

// sendWebhook posts the rendered body. Operator headers are set first, so
// the X-Pulsix-* headers (validation already refuses them as operator
// headers) always carry Pulsix's own values. With a signing secret the
// signature covers "<timestamp>.<body>", the scheme incoming Pulsix
// webhooks use too, so a replayed body with a fresh timestamp fails.
func (s *ChannelSender) sendWebhook(ctx context.Context, w openedWebhook, deliveryID uuid.UUID, ev Event) error {
	body, err := renderWebhookBody(w.bodyTemplate, ev)
	if err != nil {
		return Permanent(err)
	}
	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, w.method, w.url, bytes.NewReader(body))
	if err != nil {
		return Permanent(fmt.Errorf("build request: %v", redactURL(err)))
	}
	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	req.Header.Set("User-Agent", "Pulsix-Notify/1")
	for _, h := range w.headers {
		req.Header.Set(h[0], h[1])
	}
	ts := strconv.FormatInt(s.now().Unix(), 10)
	req.Header.Set("X-Pulsix-Event", string(ev.Kind))
	req.Header.Set("X-Pulsix-Delivery", deliveryID.String())
	req.Header.Set("X-Pulsix-Timestamp", ts)
	if len(w.signingSecret) > 0 {
		mac := hmac.New(sha256.New, w.signingSecret)
		mac.Write([]byte(ts + "."))
		mac.Write(body)
		req.Header.Set("X-Pulsix-Signature-256", "sha256="+hex.EncodeToString(mac.Sum(nil)))
	}
	resp, err := s.http.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		err = redactURL(err)
		if errors.Is(err, outbound.ErrBlocked) || errors.Is(err, outbound.ErrRedirect) {
			return Permanent(err)
		}
		return err
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()                                       //nolint:errcheck
	_, _ = io.Copy(io.Discard, io.LimitReader(resp.Body, 64<<10)) // reuse the connection
	switch code := resp.StatusCode; {
	case code >= 200 && code < 300:
		return nil
	case code == http.StatusRequestTimeout || code == http.StatusTooManyRequests || code >= 500:
		return fmt.Errorf("the endpoint answered %d", code)
	default:
		return Permanent(fmt.Errorf("the endpoint answered %d", code))
	}
}

// redactURL drops the request URL from a transport error: url.Error's
// text repeats the whole URL, and a webhook URL can carry a token. The
// wrapped cause (a refused address, a timeout, a TLS failure) stays.
func redactURL(err error) error {
	var ue *url.Error
	if errors.As(err, &ue) {
		if ue.Timeout() {
			return fmt.Errorf("%s request timed out", ue.Op)
		}
		return fmt.Errorf("%s request failed: %w", ue.Op, ue.Err)
	}
	return err
}

// errorText is the bounded, single-line error a delivery row keeps.
func errorText(err error) string {
	msg := []rune(err.Error())
	if len(msg) > maxErrorText {
		msg = append(msg[:maxErrorText], '…')
	}
	return string(msg)
}
```

`internal/notify/dispatch_test.go` (unit, fake queue):

```go
package notify_test

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/notify"
)

type outcome struct {
	kind    string // complete, retry, fail
	next    time.Time
	errText string
}

// fakeQueue hands out its pending deliveries once and records outcomes.
type fakeQueue struct {
	mu       sync.Mutex
	pending  []notify.Claimed
	outcomes map[uuid.UUID]outcome
	claimErr error
}

func (q *fakeQueue) Claim(_ context.Context, _ time.Time, _ time.Duration, limit int) ([]notify.Claimed, error) {
	q.mu.Lock()
	defer q.mu.Unlock()
	if q.claimErr != nil {
		return nil, q.claimErr
	}
	n := min(limit, len(q.pending))
	out := q.pending[:n]
	q.pending = q.pending[n:]
	return out, nil
}

func (q *fakeQueue) record(c notify.Claimed, o outcome) error {
	q.mu.Lock()
	defer q.mu.Unlock()
	if q.outcomes == nil {
		q.outcomes = map[uuid.UUID]outcome{}
	}
	q.outcomes[c.Delivery.ID] = o
	return nil
}

func (q *fakeQueue) Complete(_ context.Context, c notify.Claimed, _ time.Time) error {
	return q.record(c, outcome{kind: "complete"})
}

func (q *fakeQueue) Retry(_ context.Context, c notify.Claimed, next time.Time, errText string) error {
	return q.record(c, outcome{kind: "retry", next: next, errText: errText})
}

func (q *fakeQueue) Fail(_ context.Context, c notify.Claimed, errText string) error {
	return q.record(c, outcome{kind: "fail", errText: errText})
}

// scriptedSender answers per delivery id.
type scriptedSender struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	answers map[uuid.UUID]error
	panics  map[uuid.UUID]bool
	calls   int
}

func (s *scriptedSender) Send(_ context.Context, _ notify.Channel, id uuid.UUID, _ notify.Event) error {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.calls++
	if s.panics[id] {
		panic("boom")
	}
	return s.answers[id]
}

func quiet() *slog.Logger { return slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)) }

func claimed(attempts int, enabled bool, payload string) notify.Claimed {
	return notify.Claimed{
		Delivery: notify.Delivery{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Attempts: attempts, Payload: json.RawMessage(payload)},
		Channel:  notify.Channel{Enabled: enabled, Kind: notify.KindWebhook},
	}
}

func TestDispatcherOutcomes(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	ok := claimed(1, true, `{"kind":"test"}`)
	transient := claimed(2, true, `{"kind":"test"}`)
	lastTry := claimed(notify.MaxAttempts, true, `{"kind":"test"}`)
	permanent := claimed(1, true, `{"kind":"test"}`)
	disabled := claimed(1, false, `{"kind":"test"}`)
	broken := claimed(1, true, `{`)
	panicky := claimed(1, true, `{"kind":"test"}`)
	q := &fakeQueue{pending: []notify.Claimed{ok, transient, lastTry, permanent, disabled, broken, panicky}}
	s := &scriptedSender{
		answers: map[uuid.UUID]error{
			transient.Delivery.ID: errors.New("the endpoint answered 503"),
			lastTry.Delivery.ID:   errors.New("the endpoint answered 503"),
			permanent.Delivery.ID: notify.Permanent(errors.New("the endpoint answered 400")),
		},
		panics: map[uuid.UUID]bool{panicky.Delivery.ID: true},
	}
	n, err := notify.NewDispatcher(q, s, time.Second, func() time.Time { return now }, nil, quiet()).Tick(ctx)
	if err != nil || n != 7 {
		t.Fatalf("Tick = %d, %v", n, err)
	}
	want := map[uuid.UUID]string{
		ok.Delivery.ID: "complete", transient.Delivery.ID: "retry", lastTry.Delivery.ID: "fail",
		permanent.Delivery.ID: "fail", disabled.Delivery.ID: "fail", broken.Delivery.ID: "fail", panicky.Delivery.ID: "retry",
	}
	for id, kind := range want {
		if got := q.outcomes[id]; got.kind != kind {
			t.Errorf("delivery %s: %+v, want %s", id, got, kind)
		}
	}
	// Attempt 2 failed: the next try is 2 minutes later (30 s × 4).
	if got := q.outcomes[transient.Delivery.ID]; !got.next.Equal(now.Add(2*time.Minute)) || got.errText != "the endpoint answered 503" {
		t.Fatalf("retry = %+v", got)
	}
	if got := q.outcomes[disabled.Delivery.ID]; got.errText != "the channel is disabled" {
		t.Fatalf("disabled = %+v", got)
	}
	if s.calls != 5 {
		t.Fatalf("sender calls = %d; a disabled channel and an undecodable message are never sent", s.calls)
	}
}

func TestDispatcherClaimsUntilABatchIsShort(t *testing.T) {
	var pending []notify.Claimed
	for i := 0; i < 20; i++ {
		pending = append(pending, claimed(1, true, `{"kind":"test"}`))
	}
	q := &fakeQueue{pending: pending}
	n, err := notify.NewDispatcher(q, &scriptedSender{}, time.Second, nil, nil, nil).Tick(ctx)
	if err != nil || n != 20 || len(q.outcomes) != 20 {
		t.Fatalf("Tick = %d, %v; outcomes %d", n, err, len(q.outcomes))
	}
	q.claimErr = errors.New("db down")
	if _, err := notify.NewDispatcher(q, &scriptedSender{}, time.Second, nil, nil, nil).Tick(ctx); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a claim failure must surface")
	}
}

func TestDispatcherRunStopsWithTheContext(t *testing.T) {
	cctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
	ticks := 0
	sleep := func(context.Context, time.Duration) error {
		ticks++
		if ticks == 3 {
			cancel()
			return context.Canceled
		}
		return nil
	}
	notify.NewDispatcher(&fakeQueue{}, &scriptedSender{}, time.Second, nil, sleep, nil).Run(cctx)
	if ticks != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("ticks = %d", ticks)
	}
}
```

`internal/notify/dispatch_sql_test.go` (against the database; `seedChannel` and `countingSender` are reused by Tasks 9 and 10):

```go
package notify_test

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/testdb"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/notify"
)

// countingSender counts sends per delivery id.
type countingSender struct {
	mu    sync.Mutex
	sends map[uuid.UUID]int
	err   error
}

func (c *countingSender) Send(_ context.Context, _ notify.Channel, id uuid.UUID, _ notify.Event) error {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	if c.sends == nil {
		c.sends = map[uuid.UUID]int{}
	}
	c.sends[id]++
	return c.err
}

func seedChannel(t *testing.T, d *db.DB, org uuid.UUID) notify.Channel {
	t.Helper()
	cfg, _ := json.Marshal(notify.EmailConfig{Recipients: []string{"ops@example.test"}})
	c, err := notify.NewSQLStore(d).CreateChannel(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org), notify.Channel{
		ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: org, Kind: notify.KindEmail, Name: "ops " + uuid.NewString()[:6], Enabled: true, Config: cfg,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return c
}

func queueDelivery(t *testing.T, d *db.DB, c notify.Channel, key string, at time.Time) uuid.UUID {
	t.Helper()
	id := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	if err := notify.NewSQLStore(d).InsertDelivery(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, c.OrgID), notify.Delivery{
		ID: id, OrgID: c.OrgID, ChannelID: c.ID, EventKey: key, EventKind: notify.EventTest,
		Payload: json.RawMessage(`{"kind":"test"}`), Status: notify.DeliveryPending, NextAttemptAt: at, CreatedAt: at,
	}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return id
}

func deliveryRow(t *testing.T, d *db.DB, id uuid.UUID) (status string, attempts int, lastError string, next time.Time) {
	t.Helper()
	if err := d.Pool().QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT status, attempts, last_error, next_attempt_at FROM notification_deliveries WHERE id = $1`, id).
		Scan(&status, &attempts, &lastError, &next); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return
}

// TestTwoDispatchersSendEachDeliveryOnce: 30 due deliveries across two
// organizations, two dispatchers ticking concurrently over two stores —
// every delivery is sent exactly once and recorded sent.
func TestTwoDispatchersSendEachDeliveryOnce(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	var ids []uuid.UUID
	for _, slug := range []string{"dispatch-a", "dispatch-b"} {
		ch := seedChannel(t, d, seedOrg(t, d, slug))
		for i := 0; i < 15; i++ {
			ids = append(ids, queueDelivery(t, d, ch, "test:"+uuid.NewString(), now.Add(-time.Minute)))
		}
	}
	sender := &countingSender{}
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for i := 0; i < 2; i++ {
		disp := notify.NewDispatcher(notify.NewDeliverySQLStore(d), sender, time.Second, func() time.Time { return now }, nil, nil)
		wg.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer wg.Done()
			if _, err := disp.Tick(ctx); err != nil {
				t.Error(err)
			}
		}()
	}
	wg.Wait()
	for _, id := range ids {
		if sender.sends[id] != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("delivery %s sent %d times", id, sender.sends[id])
		}
		if status, attempts, _, _ := deliveryRow(t, d, id); status != "sent" || attempts != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("delivery %s = %s after %d attempts", id, status, attempts)
		}
	}
}

func TestDeliveryBackoffAndFinalFailure(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	ch := seedChannel(t, d, seedOrg(t, d, "dispatch-retry"))
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	id := queueDelivery(t, d, ch, "test:retry", now)
	sender := &countingSender{err: context.DeadlineExceeded}
	clock := now
	disp := notify.NewDispatcher(notify.NewDeliverySQLStore(d), sender, time.Second, func() time.Time { return clock }, nil, nil)
	delays := []time.Duration{30 * time.Second, 2 * time.Minute, 8 * time.Minute, 32 * time.Minute, 128 * time.Minute}
	for attempt := 1; attempt <= notify.MaxAttempts; attempt++ {
		if _, err := disp.Tick(ctx); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		status, attempts, lastErr, next := deliveryRow(t, d, id)
		if attempts != attempt || lastErr != context.DeadlineExceeded.Error() {
			t.Fatalf("attempt %d: attempts %d, last error %q", attempt, attempts, lastErr)
		}
		if attempt < notify.MaxAttempts {
			if status != "pending" || !next.Equal(clock.Add(delays[attempt-1])) {
				t.Fatalf("attempt %d: %s, next %s", attempt, status, next)
			}
			// Not due yet: a tick right now claims nothing.
			if n, _ := disp.Tick(ctx); n != 0 {
				t.Fatalf("attempt %d: a delivery was claimed before its backoff ended", attempt)
			}
			clock = next
		} else if status != "failed" {
			t.Fatalf("after %d attempts: %s, want failed", attempt, status)
		}
	}
}

// TestExpiredLeaseIsReclaimedAndALateCompletionIsFenced: a manager that
// claimed a row and vanished leaves it pending until the lease ends; the
// next claim takes it (attempts 2), and the first manager's late
// completion changes nothing.
func TestExpiredLeaseIsReclaimedAndALateCompletionIsFenced(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	ch := seedChannel(t, d, seedOrg(t, d, "dispatch-lease"))
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	id := queueDelivery(t, d, ch, "test:lease", now)
	st := notify.NewDeliverySQLStore(d)
	first, err := st.Claim(ctx, now, 2*time.Minute, 8)
	if err != nil || len(first) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("first claim = %v, %v", first, err)
	}
	if again, _ := st.Claim(ctx, now.Add(time.Minute), 2*time.Minute, 8); len(again) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("a leased row was claimed twice")
	}
	second, err := st.Claim(ctx, now.Add(3*time.Minute), 2*time.Minute, 8)
	if err != nil || len(second) != 1 || second[0].Delivery.Attempts != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("claim after the lease = %+v, %v", second, err)
	}
	if err := st.Complete(ctx, first[0], now.Add(3*time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if status, _, _, _ := deliveryRow(t, d, id); status != "pending" {
		t.Fatalf("a fenced completion changed the row to %s", status)
	}
	if err := st.Complete(ctx, second[0], now.Add(3*time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if status, _, _, _ := deliveryRow(t, d, id); status != "sent" {
		t.Fatalf("status = %s, want sent", status)
	}
}

func TestDeliveryLogKeepsTheNewest500(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	ch := seedChannel(t, d, seedOrg(t, d, "dispatch-trim"))
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	if _, err := d.Pool().Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO notification_deliveries (id, org_id, channel_id, event_key, event_kind, payload, status, attempts, next_attempt_at, created_at)
		SELECT gen_random_uuid(), $1, $2, 'old:' || g, 'test', '{}', 'sent', 1, $3::timestamptz, $3::timestamptz - g * interval '1 second' FROM generate_series(1, 505) AS g`,
		ch.OrgID, ch.ID, now.Add(-time.Hour)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	queueDelivery(t, d, ch, "test:new", now)
	if _, err := notify.NewDispatcher(notify.NewDeliverySQLStore(d), &countingSender{}, time.Second, func() time.Time { return now }, nil, nil).Tick(ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var n int
	if err := d.Pool().QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM notification_deliveries WHERE channel_id = $1`, ch.ID).Scan(&n); err != nil || n != 500 {
		t.Fatalf("rows = %d, %v; want 500", n, err)
	}
	list, err := notify.NewSQLStore(d).ListDeliveries(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, ch.OrgID), ch.OrgID, ch.ID, 1)
	if err != nil || len(list) != 1 || list[0].EventKey != "test:new" || list[0].Status != notify.DeliverySent {
		t.Fatalf("newest = %+v, %v", list, err)
	}
}

func TestInsertDeliveryIsIdempotentPerEventAndChannel(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	ch := seedChannel(t, d, seedOrg(t, d, "dispatch-idem"))
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	queueDelivery(t, d, ch, "run:same", now)
	queueDelivery(t, d, ch, "run:same", now)
	var n int
	if err := d.Pool().QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM notification_deliveries WHERE event_key = 'run:same'`).Scan(&n); err != nil || n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("rows = %d, %v; the event key must dedupe", n, err)
	}
}
```

```go
package notify_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/notify"
)

type fakeLog struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	rows []notify.Delivery
}

func (f *fakeLog) InsertDelivery(_ context.Context, d notify.Delivery) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.rows = append(f.rows, d)
	return nil
}

func (f *fakeLog) ListDeliveries(_ context.Context, _, channelID uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]notify.Delivery, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	var out []notify.Delivery
	for i := len(f.rows) - 1; i >= 0 && len(out) < limit; i-- {
		if f.rows[i].ChannelID == channelID {
			out = append(out, f.rows[i])
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func TestSendTestRecordsTheOutcome(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	log := &fakeLog{}
	sender := &countingSender{}
	svc := notify.NewService(notify.Deps{Channels: w.channels, Deliveries: log, Box: fakeBox{}, Mail: w.mail, Sender: sender, PublicURL: "https://pulsix.example.test/"})
	c, err := svc.CreateChannel(ctx, w.org, w.actor, webhookInput("ops hook"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	org := notify.OrgRef{ID: w.org, Slug: "acme", Name: "Acme"}
	d, err := svc.SendTest(ctx, org, c.ID)
	if err != nil || d.Status != notify.DeliverySent || d.SentAt == nil || !strings.HasPrefix(d.EventKey, "test:") {
		t.Fatalf("SendTest = %+v, %v", d, err)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(string(d.Payload), `"url":"https://pulsix.example.test/o/acme/settings/notifications"`) || !strings.Contains(string(d.Payload), `"kind":"test"`) {
		t.Fatalf("payload = %s", d.Payload)
	}
	sender.err = errors.New("the endpoint answered 500")
	d, err = svc.SendTest(ctx, org, c.ID)
	if err != nil || d.Status != notify.DeliveryFailed || d.LastError != "the endpoint answered 500" {
		t.Fatalf("failed test = %+v, %v", d, err)
	}
	list, err := svc.Deliveries(ctx, w.org, c.ID, 10)
	if err != nil || len(list) != 2 || list[0].Status != notify.DeliveryFailed {
		t.Fatalf("log = %+v, %v", list, err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.SendTest(ctx, org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown channel = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.Deliveries(ctx, w.org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), 10); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown channel log = %v", err)
	}
}
```
