- [ ] **Step 5: Deliveries, the dispatcher and its store**

`internal/notify/deliveries.go` (the delivery type, the log port, Send test):

```go
package notify

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// testSendTimeout bounds a synchronous "Send test".
const testSendTimeout = 35 * time.Second

// DeliveryStatus is where a message is: pending (queued or being sent),
// sent, or failed for good.
type DeliveryStatus string

const (
	DeliveryPending DeliveryStatus = "pending"
	DeliverySent    DeliveryStatus = "sent"
	DeliveryFailed  DeliveryStatus = "failed"
)

// Delivery is one message to one channel, and its log entry.
type Delivery struct {
	ID, OrgID, ChannelID uuid.UUID
	EventKey             string
	EventKind            EventKind
	Payload              json.RawMessage
	Status               DeliveryStatus
	Attempts             int
	NextAttemptAt        time.Time
	LastError            string
	CreatedAt            time.Time
	SentAt               *time.Time
}

// DeliveryLog records and lists deliveries (org-scoped).
type DeliveryLog interface {
	InsertDelivery(ctx context.Context, d Delivery) error
	ListDeliveries(ctx context.Context, orgID, channelID uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]Delivery, error)
}

// SendTest sends the sample event (as kind test) through the channel right
// now, outside the queue, so the operator sees the result at once; the
// attempt is recorded in the channel's delivery log either way. A failed
// send is not an error of this call: the returned delivery says failed and
// why.
func (s *Service) SendTest(ctx context.Context, org OrgRef, channelID uuid.UUID) (Delivery, error) {
	ch, err := s.d.Channels.GetChannel(ctx, org.ID, channelID)
	if err != nil {
		return Delivery{}, err
	}
	now := s.d.Now()
	ev := sampleEvent()
	ev.Kind, ev.At, ev.Org = EventTest, now, org
	ev.URL = strings.TrimRight(s.d.PublicURL, "/") + "/o/" + org.Slug + "/settings/notifications"
	payload, err := json.Marshal(ev)
	if err != nil {
		return Delivery{}, fmt.Errorf("encode test event: %w", err)
	}
	id := s.newID()
	sendCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, testSendTimeout)
	sendErr := s.d.Sender.Send(sendCtx, ch, id, ev)
	cancel()
	d := Delivery{
		ID: id, OrgID: org.ID, ChannelID: ch.ID, EventKey: "test:" + id.String(), EventKind: EventTest,
		Payload: payload, Status: DeliverySent, Attempts: 1, NextAttemptAt: now, CreatedAt: now,
	}
	if sendErr != nil {
		d.Status, d.LastError = DeliveryFailed, errorText(sendErr)
	} else {
		d.SentAt = &now
	}
	if err := s.d.Deliveries.InsertDelivery(ctx, d); err != nil {
		return Delivery{}, err
	}
	return d, nil
}

// Deliveries lists the newest deliveries of one channel.
func (s *Service) Deliveries(ctx context.Context, orgID, channelID uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]Delivery, error) {
	if _, err := s.d.Channels.GetChannel(ctx, orgID, channelID); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return s.d.Deliveries.ListDeliveries(ctx, orgID, channelID, limit)
}
```

`internal/notify/dispatch.go`:

```go
package notify

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"runtime/debug"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// Queue tuning (spec §5): at most MaxAttempts claims per message, then
// failed; a claim leases a row for leaseFor, far longer than one send can
// take (the webhook timeout is 10 s, an e-mail conversation 30 s), so a
// row is only claimed again when its manager died mid-send.
const (
	MaxAttempts             = 6
	DefaultDispatchInterval = 2 * time.Second
	dispatchBatch           = 8
	leaseFor                = 2 * time.Minute
	keepDeliveries          = 500
)

// retryDelay is the exponential backoff after failed attempt n (1-5):
// 30 s, 2 min, 8 min, 32 min, 2 h 8 min.
func retryDelay(attempt int) time.Duration {
	d := 30 * time.Second
	for i := 1; i < attempt; i++ {
		d *= 4
	}
	return d
}

// Claimed is one leased delivery with its channel.
type Claimed struct {
	Delivery Delivery
	Channel  Channel
}

// DeliveryQueue is the bypass-scoped queue store the dispatcher drives.
type DeliveryQueue interface {
	Claim(ctx context.Context, now time.Time, lease time.Duration, limit int) ([]Claimed, error)
	Complete(ctx context.Context, c Claimed, at time.Time) error
	Retry(ctx context.Context, c Claimed, next time.Time, errText string) error
	Fail(ctx context.Context, c Claimed, errText string) error
}

// Sender delivers one event through one channel (ChannelSender).
type Sender interface {
	Send(ctx context.Context, ch Channel, deliveryID uuid.UUID, ev Event) error
}

// Dispatcher sends queued deliveries. Several managers may run one each:
// the claim is FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED plus a lease. Delivery is
// at-least-once — a manager that dies after the receiver accepted a
// message but before recording it sent sends it again once the lease ends
// — and never more than once per (event, channel) enqueue, because the
// enqueue itself is idempotent (the unique event key).
type Dispatcher struct {
	queue    DeliveryQueue
	sender   Sender
	interval time.Duration
	now      func() time.Time
	sleep    func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error
	log      *slog.Logger
}

// NewDispatcher wires the dispatcher; zero interval, nil now, sleep and
// log take their defaults.
func NewDispatcher(queue DeliveryQueue, sender Sender, interval time.Duration, now func() time.Time, sleep func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error, log *slog.Logger) *Dispatcher {
	if interval <= 0 {
		interval = DefaultDispatchInterval
	}
	if now == nil {
		now = time.Now
	}
	if sleep == nil {
		sleep = func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error {
			select {
			case <-ctx.Done():
				return ctx.Err()
			case <-time.After(d):
				return nil
			}
		}
	}
	if log == nil {
		log = slog.Default()
	}
	return &Dispatcher{queue: queue, sender: sender, interval: interval, now: now, sleep: sleep, log: log}
}

// Run dispatches until ctx is cancelled.
func (d *Dispatcher) Run(ctx context.Context) {
	for {
		if _, err := d.Tick(ctx); err != nil && ctx.Err() == nil {
			d.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "notification dispatch failed", "err", err)
		}
		if err := d.sleep(ctx, d.interval); err != nil {
			return
		}
	}
}

// Tick sends every due delivery, one claimed batch at a time (the batch in
// parallel), until a batch comes back smaller than dispatchBatch.
func (d *Dispatcher) Tick(ctx context.Context) (int, error) {
	total := 0
	for {
		claimed, err := d.queue.Claim(ctx, d.now(), leaseFor, dispatchBatch)
		if err != nil {
			return total, err
		}
		var wg sync.WaitGroup
		for _, c := range claimed {
			wg.Add(1)
			go func() {
				defer wg.Done()
				d.deliver(ctx, c)
			}()
		}
		wg.Wait()
		total += len(claimed)
		if len(claimed) < dispatchBatch || ctx.Err() != nil {
			return total, nil
		}
	}
}

// deliver sends one claimed delivery and records the outcome. A panic in
// a sender is recovered into a retry, like a transient error.
func (d *Dispatcher) deliver(ctx context.Context, c Claimed) {
	sendErr := d.send(ctx, c)
	// The outcome is recorded even when ctx ended meanwhile (shutdown):
	// the send already happened or not; the lease would otherwise make
	// another manager repeat a completed send.
	wctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.WithoutCancel(ctx), 10*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	var err error
	switch {
	case sendErr == nil:
		err = d.queue.Complete(wctx, c, d.now())
	case IsPermanent(sendErr) || c.Delivery.Attempts >= MaxAttempts:
		err = d.queue.Fail(wctx, c, errorText(sendErr))
	default:
		err = d.queue.Retry(wctx, c, d.now().Add(retryDelay(c.Delivery.Attempts)), errorText(sendErr))
	}
	if err != nil {
		d.log.ErrorContext(wctx, "record notification delivery", "delivery", c.Delivery.ID, "org", c.Delivery.OrgID, "err", err)
	}
}

func (d *Dispatcher) send(ctx context.Context, c Claimed) (err error) {
	defer func() {
		if r := recover(); r != nil {
			d.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "notification sender panicked", "delivery", c.Delivery.ID, "panic", r, "stack", string(debug.Stack()))
			err = errors.New("the sender failed unexpectedly (see the manager log)")
		}
	}()
	if !c.Channel.Enabled {
		return Permanent(errors.New("the channel is disabled"))
	}
	var ev Event
	if err := json.Unmarshal(c.Delivery.Payload, &ev); err != nil {
		return Permanent(errors.New("the stored message does not decode"))
	}
	return d.sender.Send(ctx, c.Channel, c.Delivery.ID, ev)
}
```

`internal/notify/dispatch_sql.go`:

```go
package notify

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
)

// DeliverySQLStore is the dispatcher's store: the raw pool with no
// pulsix.org_id bound, so the bypass policy applies and the claim sees
// every organization; every other statement names its org_id.
type DeliverySQLStore struct{ q *gen.Queries }

// NewDeliverySQLStore builds the store on the raw pool.
func NewDeliverySQLStore(d *db.DB) *DeliverySQLStore {
	return &DeliverySQLStore{q: gen.New(d.Pool())}
}

func (st *DeliverySQLStore) Claim(ctx context.Context, now time.Time, lease time.Duration, limit int) ([]Claimed, error) {
	rows, err := st.q.ClaimNotificationDeliveries(ctx, gen.ClaimNotificationDeliveriesParams{
		LeaseUntil: now.Add(lease), Now: now, Lim: int32(limit), //nolint:gosec // limit is dispatchBatch
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("claim notification deliveries: %w", err)
	}
	out := make([]Claimed, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		out[i] = Claimed{
			Delivery: Delivery{
				ID: r.ID, OrgID: r.OrgID, ChannelID: r.ChannelID, EventKey: r.EventKey, EventKind: EventKind(r.EventKind),
				Payload: r.Payload, Status: DeliveryPending, Attempts: int(r.Attempts), CreatedAt: r.CreatedAt,
			},
			Channel: Channel{ID: r.ChannelID, OrgID: r.OrgID, Kind: Kind(r.ChannelKind), Name: r.ChannelName, Enabled: r.ChannelEnabled, Config: r.ChannelConfig},
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (st *DeliverySQLStore) Complete(ctx context.Context, c Claimed, at time.Time) error {
	if _, err := st.q.CompleteNotificationDelivery(ctx, gen.CompleteNotificationDeliveryParams{
		At: db.NullTime(&at), OrgID: c.Delivery.OrgID, ID: c.Delivery.ID, Attempts: int32(c.Delivery.Attempts), //nolint:gosec // attempts ≤ MaxAttempts
	}); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("complete notification delivery: %w", err)
	}
	return st.trim(ctx, c)
}

func (st *DeliverySQLStore) Retry(ctx context.Context, c Claimed, next time.Time, errText string) error {
	if _, err := st.q.RetryNotificationDelivery(ctx, gen.RetryNotificationDeliveryParams{
		NextAttemptAt: next, LastError: errText, OrgID: c.Delivery.OrgID, ID: c.Delivery.ID, Attempts: int32(c.Delivery.Attempts), //nolint:gosec // attempts ≤ MaxAttempts
	}); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("reschedule notification delivery: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

func (st *DeliverySQLStore) Fail(ctx context.Context, c Claimed, errText string) error {
	if _, err := st.q.FailNotificationDelivery(ctx, gen.FailNotificationDeliveryParams{
		LastError: errText, OrgID: c.Delivery.OrgID, ID: c.Delivery.ID, Attempts: int32(c.Delivery.Attempts), //nolint:gosec // attempts ≤ MaxAttempts
	}); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("fail notification delivery: %w", err)
	}
	return st.trim(ctx, c)
}

// trim keeps a channel's newest keepDeliveries finished rows.
func (st *DeliverySQLStore) trim(ctx context.Context, c Claimed) error {
	if err := st.q.TrimNotificationDeliveries(ctx, gen.TrimNotificationDeliveriesParams{
		OrgID: c.Delivery.OrgID, ChannelID: c.Delivery.ChannelID, Keep: keepDeliveries,
	}); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("trim notification deliveries: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}
```

```go
func (st *SQLStore) InsertDelivery(ctx context.Context, d Delivery) error {
	if _, err := st.q.InsertNotificationDelivery(ctx, gen.InsertNotificationDeliveryParams{
		ID: d.ID, OrgID: d.OrgID, ChannelID: d.ChannelID, EventKey: d.EventKey, EventKind: string(d.EventKind),
		Payload: d.Payload, Status: string(d.Status), Attempts: int32(d.Attempts), //nolint:gosec // attempts ≤ MaxAttempts
		NextAttemptAt: d.NextAttemptAt, LastError: d.LastError, CreatedAt: d.CreatedAt, SentAt: db.NullTime(d.SentAt),
	}); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("record notification delivery: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

func (st *SQLStore) ListDeliveries(ctx context.Context, orgID, channelID uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]Delivery, error) {
	rows, err := st.q.ListNotificationDeliveries(ctx, gen.ListNotificationDeliveriesParams{OrgID: orgID, ChannelID: channelID, Lim: int32(limit)}) //nolint:gosec // limit is clamped by the API (≤ 100)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list notification deliveries: %w", err)
	}
	out := make([]Delivery, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		out[i] = Delivery{
			ID: r.ID, OrgID: r.OrgID, ChannelID: r.ChannelID, EventKey: r.EventKey, EventKind: EventKind(r.EventKind),
			Payload: r.Payload, Status: DeliveryStatus(r.Status), Attempts: int(r.Attempts), NextAttemptAt: r.NextAttemptAt,
			LastError: r.LastError, CreatedAt: r.CreatedAt, SentAt: db.TimePtr(r.SentAt),
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

Expected: PASS. Two dispatchers ticking at once over 30 deliveries in two organizations send each one exactly once. A retried delivery waits 30 s, 2 min, 8 min, 32 min and 2 h 8 min, and fails after the sixth attempt. A late completion after an expired lease changes nothing. A channel keeps 500 finished rows. ### Task 4: Channels API and the Notifications settings page

This task exposes channels over the API and adds the SPA page at Settings → Notifications (spec §5: "Every channel has Send test … The delivery log is visible per channel"). Operators see channels and their log. Admins create, edit, test and delete them. An e-mail channel is refused with the reason while the instance has no outgoing mail, and the list says so (`mail_available`).

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/api/notify_dto.go`, `internal/api/notify_handlers.go`
- Test: `internal/api/notify_handlers_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/api/api.go` (`Deps.Notify`, `validate`, route registration), `internal/api/api_test.go` (hand-built Deps), `internal/api/apitest/apitest.go` (the notify service), `internal/app/app.go` (the notify service, `Deps.Notify`)
- Create: `web/src/api/hooks/useNotifications.ts`, `web/src/features/notifications/{ChannelsPage,ChannelDialog,DeliveryLog}.tsx`
- Test: `web/src/features/notifications/ChannelsPage.test.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/lib/roles.ts`, `web/src/api/types.ts`, `web/src/ui/SettingsTabs.tsx` (+ test), `web/src/router.tsx`
- Regenerate: `web/src/api/schema.d.ts`
- Docs (noix-docs): `user/notifications.md` (new), `reference/api.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 2 and 3's `notify.Service` (`CreateChannel`, `GetChannel`, `ListChannels`, `UpdateChannel`, `DeleteChannel`, `MailAvailable`, `SendTest`, `Deliveries`), `Channel.View`, `notify.OrgRef`.
- Produces (HTTP, all under `/api/v1/orgs/{org}`):

| Method | Path | Permission | Success |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/notification-channels` | channels.read | 200 `{channels, mail_available}` |
| POST | `/notification-channels` | channels.write | 201 `{channel}` + `ETag` |
| GET | `/notification-channels/{id}` | channels.read | 200 `{channel}` + `ETag` |
| PUT | `/notification-channels/{id}` | channels.write | 200 `{channel}` + `ETag`, `If-Match` required |
| DELETE | `/notification-channels/{id}` | channels.write | 200 `{}` |
| POST | `/notification-channels/{id}/test` | channels.write | 200 `{delivery}` (sent or failed) |
| GET | `/notification-channels/{id}/deliveries?limit=` | channels.read | 200 `{deliveries}`, newest first, 1–100 |

  Errors: 400 invalid input (the reason, never a secret), 404, 409 name taken or no outgoing mail, 412 stale `If-Match`, 428 missing `If-Match`. Audited as `createNotificationChannel`, `updateNotificationChannel`, `deleteNotificationChannel`, `testNotificationChannel`, object type `channel`.
- Produces (Go): `api.Deps.Notify *notify.Service`; the exported body type `api.NotifyChannelBody` (schema `NotifyChannelBody`); DTO schemas `NotifyChannelDTO`, `NotifyDeliveryDTO`, `NotifyEmailDTO`, `NotifyWebhookDTO`, `NotifyWebhookBody`; `mapNotifyErr` (reused by Tasks 9 and 10).
- Produces (SPA): `useChannels(org, enabled?)`, `useCreateChannel`, `useUpdateChannel(org, id)`, `useDeleteChannel`, `useTestChannel`, `useChannelDeliveries(org, id)`; types `NotifyChannel`, `NotifyChannelInput`, `NotifyDelivery`; permissions `channels.read`, `channels.write` in `may()`; route `/o/$org/settings/notifications`.

```go
package api_test

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"
)

type channelOut struct {
	ID      string `json:"id"`
	Kind    string `json:"kind"`
	Name    string `json:"name"`
	Enabled bool   `json:"enabled"`
	Webhook *struct {
		URL     string   `json:"url"`
		Headers []string `json:"headers"`
		Signed  bool     `json:"signed"`
	} `json:"webhook"`
	Email *struct {
		Recipients []string `json:"recipients"`
	} `json:"email"`
	Revision int64 `json:"revision"`
}

// hookReceiver is a webhook endpoint recording every request.
type hookReceiver struct {
	mu     sync.Mutex
	events []string
	bodies []string
}

func (h *hookReceiver) ServeHTTP(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	body, _ := io.ReadAll(r.Body)
	h.mu.Lock()
	h.events = append(h.events, r.Header.Get("X-Pulsix-Event"))
	h.bodies = append(h.bodies, string(body))
	h.mu.Unlock()
	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
}

func (h *hookReceiver) count() int {
	h.mu.Lock()
	defer h.mu.Unlock()
	return len(h.events)
}

func (f runFixture) channelsURL() string {
	return f.s.HTTP.URL + "/api/v1/orgs/" + f.orgSlug + "/notification-channels"
}

func TestNotificationChannelLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	f := newRunFixture(t, "notify-life")
	rec := &hookReceiver{}
	hook := httptest.NewServer(rec)
	defer hook.Close()

	body := map[string]any{
		"kind": "webhook", "name": "ops hook",
		"webhook": map[string]any{
			"url":     hook.URL + "/services/T0/B0/s3cret-path",
			"headers": []map[string]any{{"name": "Authorization", "value": "Bearer s3cret-token"}},
			"signed":  true, "signing_secret": "0123456789abcdef0123",
		},
	}
	// Operators see channels; only admins manage them.
	if resp := postJSON(t, f.operatorC, f.channelsURL(), body); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("operator create = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	resp := postJSON(t, f.adminC, f.channelsURL(), body)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusCreated || resp.Header.Get("ETag") != `"1"` {
		t.Fatalf("create = %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	raw := mustReadBody(t, resp)
	for _, secret := range []string{"s3cret-path", "s3cret-token", "0123456789abcdef0123"} {
		if strings.Contains(raw, secret) {
			t.Fatalf("the response carries %q: %s", secret, raw)
		}
	}
	var created struct {
		Channel channelOut `json:"channel"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(raw), &created); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	c := created.Channel
	if c.Webhook == nil || !strings.HasSuffix(c.Webhook.URL, "/…") || c.Webhook.Headers[0] != "Authorization" || !c.Webhook.Signed || !c.Enabled {
		t.Fatalf("created = %+v", c)
	}

	var list struct {
		Channels      []channelOut `json:"channels"`
		MailAvailable bool         `json:"mail_available"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, f.operatorC, http.MethodGet, f.channelsURL(), nil), &list)
	if len(list.Channels) != 1 || !list.MailAvailable {
		t.Fatalf("list = %+v", list)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.viewerC, http.MethodGet, f.channelsURL(), nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("viewer list = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}

	// Send test: synchronous, recorded in the log.
	resp = postJSON(t, f.adminC, f.channelsURL()+"/"+c.ID+"/test", nil)
	var tested struct {
		Delivery struct {
			Status    string `json:"status"`
			EventKind string `json:"event_kind"`
		} `json:"delivery"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &tested)
	if tested.Delivery.Status != "sent" || tested.Delivery.EventKind != "test" || rec.count() != 1 || rec.events[0] != "test" {
		t.Fatalf("test = %+v, receiver saw %v", tested, rec.events)
	}
	var log struct {
		Deliveries []struct {
			Status string `json:"status"`
		} `json:"deliveries"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, f.operatorC, http.MethodGet, f.channelsURL()+"/"+c.ID+"/deliveries?limit=10", nil), &log)
	if len(log.Deliveries) != 1 || log.Deliveries[0].Status != "sent" {
		t.Fatalf("log = %+v", log)
	}

	// Update keeps the stored URL, header value and secret when left out.
	up := map[string]any{"kind": "webhook", "name": "ops hook 2", "enabled": false, "webhook": map[string]any{
		"headers": []map[string]any{{"name": "Authorization"}}, "signed": true,
	}}
	if resp := requestWithHeaders(t, f.adminC, http.MethodPut, f.channelsURL()+"/"+c.ID, up, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"7"`}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusPreconditionFailed {
		t.Fatalf("stale update = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	resp = requestWithHeaders(t, f.adminC, http.MethodPut, f.channelsURL()+"/"+c.ID, up, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"1"`})
	var updated struct {
		Channel channelOut `json:"channel"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &updated)
	if updated.Channel.Name != "ops hook 2" || updated.Channel.Enabled || updated.Channel.Webhook.URL != c.Webhook.URL || updated.Channel.Revision != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("updated = %+v", updated.Channel)
	}

	// The change is in the activity log under the channel object type.
	var audited int
	if err := f.s.DB.Pool().QueryRow(context.Background(),
		`SELECT count(*) FROM audit_events WHERE object_type = 'channel' AND object_id = $1`, c.ID).Scan(&audited); err != nil || audited != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("channel audit events = %d, %v; want create, test and update", audited, err)
	}

	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.adminC, http.MethodDelete, f.channelsURL()+"/"+c.ID, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("delete = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.adminC, http.MethodGet, f.channelsURL()+"/"+c.ID, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("get after delete = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}

func TestEmailChannelNeedsInstanceMail(t *testing.T) {
	f := newRunFixture(t, "notify-mail")
	body := map[string]any{"kind": "email", "name": "ops mail", "email": map[string]any{"recipients": []string{"ops@example.test"}}}
	f.s.Mail.SetConfigured(false)
	resp := postJSON(t, f.adminC, f.channelsURL(), body)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusConflict || !strings.Contains(mustReadBody(t, resp), "outgoing mail") {
		t.Fatalf("e-mail channel without instance mail = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	var list struct {
		MailAvailable bool `json:"mail_available"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, f.adminC, http.MethodGet, f.channelsURL(), nil), &list)
	if list.MailAvailable {
		t.Fatal("mail_available must be false")
	}
	f.s.Mail.SetConfigured(true)
	resp = postJSON(t, f.adminC, f.channelsURL(), body)
	var created struct {
		Channel channelOut `json:"channel"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &created)
	if created.Channel.Email == nil || created.Channel.Email.Recipients[0] != "ops@example.test" {
		t.Fatalf("created = %+v", created)
	}
	resp = postJSON(t, f.adminC, f.channelsURL()+"/"+created.Channel.ID+"/test", nil)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	sent := f.s.Mail.Sent()
	if len(sent) != 1 || sent[0].To != "ops@example.test" || !strings.HasPrefix(sent[0].Subject, "[Pulsix] Test notification") {
		t.Fatalf("sent = %+v", sent)
	}
	// Invalid input names the problem.
	bad := map[string]any{"kind": "email", "name": "bad", "email": map[string]any{"recipients": []string{"Ops <ops@example.test>"}}}
	if resp := postJSON(t, f.adminC, f.channelsURL(), bad); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("display-name recipient = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The DTOs and handlers**

`internal/api/notify_dto.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"time"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/notify"
)

type notifyEmailDTO struct {
	Recipients []string `json:"recipients" maxItems:"20" doc:"Plain addresses; every recipient gets the same message"`
}

type notifyWebhookDTO struct {
	Method       string   `json:"method" enum:"POST,PUT"`
	URL          string   `json:"url" doc:"Scheme and host only: a path can carry a token and is never returned"`
	Headers      []string `json:"headers" doc:"Header names; values are never returned"`
	BodyTemplate string   `json:"body_template,omitempty"`
	Signed       bool     `json:"signed" doc:"Requests carry X-Pulsix-Signature-256"`
}

type notifyChannelDTO struct {
	ID        string            `json:"id"`
	Kind      string            `json:"kind" enum:"email,webhook"`
	Name      string            `json:"name"`
	Enabled   bool              `json:"enabled"`
	Email     *notifyEmailDTO   `json:"email,omitempty"`
	Webhook   *notifyWebhookDTO `json:"webhook,omitempty"`
	Revision  int64             `json:"revision"`
	CreatedAt time.Time         `json:"created_at"`
	UpdatedAt time.Time         `json:"updated_at"`
}

type notifyHeaderBody struct {
	Name  string  `json:"name" minLength:"1" maxLength:"100"`
	Value *string `json:"value,omitempty" maxLength:"4096" doc:"Omit on update to keep the stored value"`
}

type notifyWebhookBody struct {
	Method        string             `json:"method,omitempty" enum:"POST,PUT" doc:"Default POST"`
	URL           *string            `json:"url,omitempty" maxLength:"2048" doc:"Required on create; omit on update to keep the stored URL"`
	Headers       []notifyHeaderBody `json:"headers,omitempty" maxItems:"20"`
	BodyTemplate  string             `json:"body_template,omitempty" maxLength:"16384" doc:"Empty sends the event as JSON"`
	Signed        bool               `json:"signed,omitempty"`
	SigningSecret *string            `json:"signing_secret,omitempty" minLength:"16" maxLength:"256" doc:"Omit on update to keep the stored secret"`
}

// NotifyChannelBody is the create/update body (exported so huma names
// the schema after it).
type NotifyChannelBody struct {
	Kind    string             `json:"kind" enum:"email,webhook"`
	Name    string             `json:"name" minLength:"1" maxLength:"100"`
	Enabled *bool              `json:"enabled,omitempty" doc:"Default true"`
	Email   *notifyEmailDTO    `json:"email,omitempty"`
	Webhook *notifyWebhookBody `json:"webhook,omitempty"`
}

type notifyDeliveryDTO struct {
	ID            string     `json:"id"`
	EventKind     string     `json:"event_kind" enum:"run.finished,schedule.skipped,schedule.failed,digest.weekly,test"`
	Status        string     `json:"status" enum:"pending,sent,failed"`
	Attempts      int        `json:"attempts"`
	NextAttemptAt *time.Time `json:"next_attempt_at,omitempty" doc:"Pending only: when the next attempt is due"`
	LastError     string     `json:"last_error,omitempty"`
	CreatedAt     time.Time  `json:"created_at"`
	SentAt        *time.Time `json:"sent_at,omitempty"`
}

func (b NotifyChannelBody) input() notify.Input {
	in := notify.Input{Kind: notify.Kind(b.Kind), Name: b.Name, Enabled: b.Enabled == nil || *b.Enabled}
	if b.Email != nil {
		in.Email = &notify.EmailConfig{Recipients: b.Email.Recipients}
	}
	if w := b.Webhook; w != nil {
		in.Webhook = &notify.WebhookInput{Method: w.Method, URL: w.URL, BodyTemplate: w.BodyTemplate, Signed: w.Signed, SigningSecret: w.SigningSecret}
		for _, h := range w.Headers {
			in.Webhook.Headers = append(in.Webhook.Headers, notify.HeaderInput{Name: h.Name, Value: h.Value})
		}
	}
	return in
}

func toNotifyChannelDTO(c notify.Channel) (notifyChannelDTO, error) {
	dto := notifyChannelDTO{
		ID: c.ID.String(), Kind: string(c.Kind), Name: c.Name, Enabled: c.Enabled,
		Revision: c.Revision, CreatedAt: c.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: c.UpdatedAt,
	}
	email, hook, err := c.View()
	if err != nil {
		return notifyChannelDTO{}, err
	}
	if email != nil {
		dto.Email = &notifyEmailDTO{Recipients: email.Recipients}
	}
	if hook != nil {
		dto.Webhook = &notifyWebhookDTO{Method: hook.Method, URL: hook.URLDisplay, Headers: hook.HeaderNames, BodyTemplate: hook.BodyTemplate, Signed: hook.Signed}
		if dto.Webhook.Headers == nil {
			dto.Webhook.Headers = []string{}
		}
	}
	return dto, nil
}

func toNotifyDeliveryDTO(d notify.Delivery) notifyDeliveryDTO {
	dto := notifyDeliveryDTO{
		ID: d.ID.String(), EventKind: string(d.EventKind), Status: string(d.Status), Attempts: d.Attempts,
		LastError: d.LastError, CreatedAt: d.CreatedAt, SentAt: d.SentAt,
	}
	if d.Status == notify.DeliveryPending {
		next := d.NextAttemptAt
		dto.NextAttemptAt = &next
	}
	return dto
}
```

`internal/api/notify_handlers.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"
	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/notify"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/tenancy"
)

type notifyChannelListOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Channels      []notifyChannelDTO `json:"channels"`
		MailAvailable bool               `json:"mail_available" doc:"Whether this instance has outgoing mail settings (e-mail channels need them)"`
	}
}

type notifyChannelIDInput struct {
	Org string `path:"org"`
	ID  string `path:"id"`
}

type notifyChannelCreateInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	Body NotifyChannelBody
}

type notifyChannelUpdateInput struct {
	Org     string `path:"org"`
	ID      string `path:"id"`
	IfMatch string `header:"If-Match" doc:"The channel's ETag; required"`
	Body    NotifyChannelBody
}

type notifyChannelOutput struct {
	ETag string `header:"ETag"`
	Body struct {
		Channel notifyChannelDTO `json:"channel"`
	}
}

type notifyDeliveriesInput struct {
	Org   string `path:"org"`
	ID    string `path:"id"`
	Limit int    `query:"limit" minimum:"1" maximum:"100" default:"50"`
}

type notifyDeliveriesOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Deliveries []notifyDeliveryDTO `json:"deliveries"`
	}
}

type notifyTestOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Delivery notifyDeliveryDTO `json:"delivery"`
	}
}

// registerNotifyChannelRoutes wires spec §5's channels: channels.read
// (operator) to see them and their delivery log, channels.write (admin)
// to manage and test them.
func registerNotifyChannelRoutes(hapi huma.API, deps Deps) {
	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-channels", func(ctx context.Context, in *struct {
		Org string `path:"org"`
	}) (*notifyChannelListOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermChannelsRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		list, err := deps.Notify.ListChannels(ctx, scope.OrgID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapNotifyErr(ctx, err)
		}
		mailOK, err := deps.Notify.MailAvailable(ctx)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapNotifyErr(ctx, err)
		}
		out := &notifyChannelListOutput{}
		out.Body.MailAvailable = mailOK
		out.Body.Channels = make([]notifyChannelDTO, 0, len(list))
		for _, c := range list {
			dto, err := toNotifyChannelDTO(c)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, mapNotifyErr(ctx, err)
			}
			out.Body.Channels = append(out.Body.Channels, dto)
		}
		return out, nil
	})

	huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-channels", func(ctx context.Context, in *notifyChannelCreateInput) (*notifyChannelOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermChannelsWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		c, err := deps.Notify.CreateChannel(ctx, scope.OrgID, scope.UserID, in.Body.input())
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapNotifyErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, c.ID.String(), "created notification channel "+c.Name)
		return notifyChannelOut(ctx, c)
	}, func(o *huma.Operation) { o.DefaultStatus = http.StatusCreated }, withAudit("createNotificationChannel", "channel"))

	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-channels/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *notifyChannelIDInput) (*notifyChannelOutput, error) {
		scope, id, err := notifyChannelScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermChannelsRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		c, err := deps.Notify.GetChannel(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapNotifyErr(ctx, err)
		}
		return notifyChannelOut(ctx, c)
	})

	huma.Put(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-channels/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *notifyChannelUpdateInput) (*notifyChannelOutput, error) {
		scope, id, err := notifyChannelScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermChannelsWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		rev, err := requireIfMatch(in.IfMatch)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		c, err := deps.Notify.UpdateChannel(ctx, scope.OrgID, id, rev, in.Body.input())
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapNotifyErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, c.ID.String(), "updated notification channel "+c.Name)
		return notifyChannelOut(ctx, c)
	}, withAudit("updateNotificationChannel", "channel"))

	huma.Delete(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-channels/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *notifyChannelIDInput) (*okOutput, error) {
		scope, id, err := notifyChannelScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermChannelsWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if err := deps.Notify.DeleteChannel(ctx, scope.OrgID, id); err != nil {
			return nil, mapNotifyErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, id.String(), "deleted notification channel")
		return &okOutput{}, nil
	}, withAudit("deleteNotificationChannel", "channel"))

	huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-channels/{id}/test", func(ctx context.Context, in *notifyChannelIDInput) (*notifyTestOutput, error) {
		scope, id, err := notifyChannelScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermChannelsWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		d, err := deps.Notify.SendTest(ctx, notify.OrgRef{ID: scope.OrgID, Slug: scope.Org.Slug, Name: scope.Org.Name}, id)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapNotifyErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, id.String(), "sent a test notification ("+string(d.Status)+")")
		out := &notifyTestOutput{}
		out.Body.Delivery = toNotifyDeliveryDTO(d)
		return out, nil
	}, withAudit("testNotificationChannel", "channel"))

	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-channels/{id}/deliveries", func(ctx context.Context, in *notifyDeliveriesInput) (*notifyDeliveriesOutput, error) {
		scope, id, err := notifyChannelScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermChannelsRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		list, err := deps.Notify.Deliveries(ctx, scope.OrgID, id, clampScheduleRunsLimit(in.Limit))
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapNotifyErr(ctx, err)
		}
		out := &notifyDeliveriesOutput{}
		out.Body.Deliveries = make([]notifyDeliveryDTO, len(list))
		for i, d := range list {
			out.Body.Deliveries[i] = toNotifyDeliveryDTO(d)
		}
		return out, nil
	})
}

func notifyChannelScope(ctx context.Context, deps Deps, org, rawID string, perm tenancy.Permission) (Scope, uuid.UUID, error) {
	scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, org, perm)
	if err != nil {
		return Scope{}, uuid.Nil, err
	}
	id, err := parseUUID(rawID, "channel id")
	return scope, id, err
}

func notifyChannelOut(ctx context.Context, c notify.Channel) (*notifyChannelOutput, error) {
	dto, err := toNotifyChannelDTO(c)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, mapNotifyErr(ctx, err)
	}
	out := &notifyChannelOutput{ETag: revisionETag(c.Revision)}
	out.Body.Channel = dto
	return out, nil
}

func mapNotifyErr(ctx context.Context, err error) error {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, notify.ErrInvalidInput):
		return huma.Error400BadRequest(err.Error())
	case errors.Is(err, notify.ErrNotFound):
		return huma.Error404NotFound("not found")
	case errors.Is(err, notify.ErrNameTaken):
		return huma.Error409Conflict("a channel with this name already exists")
	case errors.Is(err, notify.ErrMailUnavailable):
		return huma.Error409Conflict(notify.ErrMailUnavailable.Error())
	case errors.Is(err, notify.ErrRevisionMismatch):
		return huma.Error412PreconditionFailed("this changed since you loaded it; reload and apply your change again")
	default:
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "unmapped notify error", "err", err)
		return huma.Error500InternalServerError("internal error")
	}
}
```

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { createMemoryHistory, createRootRoute, createRoute, createRouter, RouterProvider } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { ToastProvider } from '../../ui/toast.tsx';
import { mockApi, type MockRequest } from '../../test/mockApi.ts';
import { ChannelsPage } from './ChannelsPage.tsx';

const org = (role: string) => ({ org: { id: 'o', name: 'Acme', slug: 'acme', role, created_at: '' } });
const me = { user: { id: 'u1', email: 'a@acme.io', display_name: 'A' }, mfa_complete: true, mfa_enrolment_required: false, totp_confirmed: true, instance_admin: false };
const hook = {
  id: 'c1', kind: 'webhook', name: 'ops hook', enabled: true, revision: 2, created_at: '', updated_at: '',
  webhook: { method: 'POST', url: 'https://hooks.example.test/…', headers: ['Authorization'], signed: true },
};
const mailCh = { id: 'c2', kind: 'email', name: 'ops mail', enabled: false, revision: 1, created_at: '', updated_at: '', email: { recipients: ['ops@example.test'] } };

function renderPage() {
  const qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  const rootRoute = createRootRoute();
  const orgRoute = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => rootRoute, path: '/o/$org' });
  const page = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => orgRoute, path: '/settings/notifications', component: ChannelsPage });
  const router = createRouter({
    routeTree: rootRoute.addChildren([orgRoute.addChildren([page])]),
    history: createMemoryHistory({ initialEntries: ['/o/acme/settings/notifications'] }),
  });
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <ToastProvider>
        <RouterProvider router={router} />
      </ToastProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

describe('ChannelsPage', () => {
  it('lists channels with their target and status, and admins manage them', async () => {
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/v1/auth/me': me,
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme': org('admin'),
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/notification-channels': { channels: [hook, mailCh], mail_available: true },
    });
    renderPage();
    const row = (await screen.findByText('ops hook')).closest('[data-row]')!.parentElement as HTMLElement;
    expect(within(row).getByText('POST https://hooks.example.test/…')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(row).getByText('enabled')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('ops@example.test')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('disabled')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add channel' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Send test through ops hook' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('operators read channels and their log but cannot change them', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/v1/auth/me': me,
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme': org('operator'),
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/notification-channels': { channels: [hook], mail_available: true },
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/notification-channels/c1/deliveries': {
        deliveries: [
          { id: 'd1', event_kind: 'run.finished', status: 'pending', attempts: 2, last_error: 'the endpoint answered 503', next_attempt_at: '2026-09-22T08:02:00Z', created_at: '2026-09-22T08:00:00Z' },
          { id: 'd2', event_kind: 'test', status: 'sent', attempts: 1, created_at: '2026-09-22T07:00:00Z' },
        ],
      },
    });
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('ops hook');
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Add channel' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Send test through ops hook' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Deliveries of ops hook' }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Deliveries · ops hook' });
    expect(await within(dialog).findByText(/2 attempts · the endpoint answered 503/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialog).getAllByText(/sent|pending/)).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('viewers are told channels are for operators', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /api/v1/auth/me': me, 'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme': org('viewer') });
    renderPage();
    expect(await screen.findByText('Notification channels are visible to operators and above.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('creates a signed webhook channel with a header', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    let posted: unknown;
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/v1/auth/me': me,
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme': org('admin'),
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/notification-channels': { channels: [], mail_available: false },
      'POST /api/v1/orgs/acme/notification-channels': (req: MockRequest) => {
        posted = req.body;
        return { channel: hook };
      },
    });
    renderPage();
    expect(await screen.findByRole('note')).toHaveTextContent('outgoing mail settings');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add channel' }));
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Add channel' });
    expect(within(dialog).getByRole('option', { name: /E-mail \(needs outgoing mail settings\)/ })).toBeDisabled();
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Name'), 'ops hook');
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('URL'), 'https://hooks.example.test/T0/B0/x');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Add header' }));
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Header 1 name'), 'Authorization');
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Header 1 value'), 'Bearer t0ken');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Sign requests (X-Pulsix-Signature-256)'));
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Signing secret (16–256 characters)'), '0123456789abcdef0123');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }));
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(posted).toEqual({
        kind: 'webhook', name: 'ops hook', enabled: true,
        webhook: {
          method: 'POST', url: 'https://hooks.example.test/T0/B0/x', headers: [{ name: 'Authorization', value: 'Bearer t0ken' }],
          body_template: '', signed: true, signing_secret: '0123456789abcdef0123',
        },
      }),
    );
  });

  it('editing leaves stored secrets out of the request', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    let put: { body: unknown; ifMatch: string | null } | undefined;
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/v1/auth/me': me,
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme': org('admin'),
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/notification-channels': { channels: [hook], mail_available: true },
      'PUT /api/v1/orgs/acme/notification-channels/c1': (req: MockRequest) => {
        put = { body: req.body, ifMatch: req.headers.get('If-Match') };
        return { channel: hook };
      },
    });
    renderPage();
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Edit ops hook' }));
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Edit ops hook' });
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(put?.ifMatch).toBe('"2"'));
    expect(put?.body).toEqual({
      kind: 'webhook', name: 'ops hook', enabled: true,
      webhook: { method: 'POST', headers: [{ name: 'Authorization' }], body_template: '', signed: true },
    });
  });

  it('reports the outcome of Send test', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/v1/auth/me': me,
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme': org('admin'),
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/notification-channels': { channels: [hook], mail_available: true },
      'POST /api/v1/orgs/acme/notification-channels/c1/test': { delivery: { id: 'd', event_kind: 'test', status: 'failed', attempts: 1, last_error: 'the endpoint answered 404', created_at: '' } },
    });
    renderPage();
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Send test through ops hook' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Test through ops hook failed: the endpoint answered 404')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

`web/src/api/hooks/useNotifications.ts` (Tasks 9 and 10 append to it):

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, unwrap } from '../client.ts';
import type { NotifyChannelInput } from '../types.ts';

const ifMatch = (revision: number) => ({ 'If-Match': `"${revision}"` });

function useInvalidate(org: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return () => {
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['notification-channels', org] });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['notification-deliveries', org] });
  };
}

/** useChannels lists the org's notification channels plus whether the
 * instance can send e-mail at all (mail_available). */
export function useChannels(org: string, enabled = true) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['notification-channels', org],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-channels', { params: { path: { org } } })),
    enabled: !!org && enabled,
  });
}

export function useCreateChannel(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: NotifyChannelInput) => unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-channels', { params: { path: { org } }, body })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
    gcTime: 0,
  });
}

export function useUpdateChannel(org: string, id: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ revision, ...body }: NotifyChannelInput & { revision: number }) =>
      unwrap(api.PUT('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-channels/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id }, header: ifMatch(revision) }, body })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
    gcTime: 0,
  });
}

export function useDeleteChannel(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => unwrap(api.DELETE('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-channels/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

/** useTestChannel sends the sample message through a channel right away;
 * the response's delivery says sent or failed (and why). */
export function useTestChannel(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-channels/{id}/test', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useChannelDeliveries(org: string, id: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['notification-deliveries', org, id],
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-channels/{id}/deliveries', { params: { path: { org, id }, query: { limit: 50 } } })),
    enabled: !!org && !!id,
  });
}
```

`web/src/features/notifications/ChannelDialog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Plus, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { Dialog } from '../../ui/dialog.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '../../ui/input.tsx';
import { Label } from '../../ui/label.tsx';
import { useCreateChannel, useUpdateChannel } from '../../api/hooks/useNotifications.ts';
import type { NotifyChannel, NotifyChannelInput } from '../../api/types.ts';

const SELECT = 'h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground';
const TEXTAREA = 'min-h-20 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background p-2 font-mono text-xs';

interface HeaderRow {
  name: string;
  /** Empty while editing keeps the stored value. */
  value: string;
}

export interface ChannelDialogProps {
  org: string;
  /** The channel to edit; undefined creates one. */
  channel?: NotifyChannel;
  mailAvailable: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

/** ChannelDialog creates or edits a notification channel. Stored secrets
 * (the webhook URL, header values, the signing secret) are never shown:
 * leaving their fields empty while editing keeps them. */
export function ChannelDialog({ org, channel, mailAvailable, onClose }: ChannelDialogProps) {
  const editing = !!channel;
  const create = useCreateChannel(org);
  const update = useUpdateChannel(org, channel?.id ?? '');
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<'email' | 'webhook'>(channel?.kind ?? (mailAvailable ? 'email' : 'webhook'));
  const [name, setName] = useState(channel?.name ?? '');
  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(channel?.enabled ?? true);
  const [recipients, setRecipients] = useState((channel?.email?.recipients ?? []).join('\n'));
  const [method, setMethod] = useState<'POST' | 'PUT'>(channel?.webhook?.method ?? 'POST');
  const [url, setUrl] = useState('');
  const [headers, setHeaders] = useState<HeaderRow[]>((channel?.webhook?.headers ?? []).map((h) => ({ name: h, value: '' })));
  const [template, setTemplate] = useState(channel?.webhook?.body_template ?? '');
  const [signed, setSigned] = useState(channel?.webhook?.signed ?? false);
  const [secret, setSecret] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  function body(): NotifyChannelInput {
    if (kind === 'email') {
      return { kind, name, enabled, email: { recipients: recipients.split(/[\n,]/).map((r) => r.trim()).filter(Boolean) } };
    }
    return {
      kind,
      name,
      enabled,
      webhook: {
        method,
        ...(url.trim() !== '' ? { url: url.trim() } : {}),
        headers: headers.filter((h) => h.name.trim() !== '').map((h) => (h.value !== '' ? { name: h.name.trim(), value: h.value } : { name: h.name.trim() })),
        body_template: template,
        signed,
        ...(signed && secret !== '' ? { signing_secret: secret } : {}),
      },
    };
  }

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    try {
      if (channel) {
        await update.mutateAsync({ ...body(), revision: channel.revision });
      } else {
        await create.mutateAsync(body());
      }
      onClose();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Saving failed');
    }
  }

  const keep = editing ? 'leave empty to keep' : undefined;
  return (
    <Dialog open onClose={onClose} title={editing ? `Edit ${channel.name}` : 'Add channel'} size="wide">
      <form className="flex flex-col gap-3" onSubmit={(e) => void submit(e)}>
        <Label htmlFor="ch-kind">Kind</Label>
        <select id="ch-kind" className={SELECT} value={kind} disabled={editing} onChange={(e) => setKind(e.target.value as 'email' | 'webhook')}>
          <option value="email" disabled={!mailAvailable && !editing}>
            E-mail{!mailAvailable ? ' (needs outgoing mail settings)' : ''}
          </option>
          <option value="webhook">Webhook</option>
        </select>
        <Label htmlFor="ch-name">Name</Label>
        <Input id="ch-name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} required maxLength={100} />
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
          <input type="checkbox" checked={enabled} onChange={(e) => setEnabled(e.target.checked)} />
          Enabled
        </label>
        {kind === 'email' ? (
          <>
            <Label htmlFor="ch-recipients">Recipients (one per line)</Label>
            <textarea id="ch-recipients" className={TEXTAREA} value={recipients} onChange={(e) => setRecipients(e.target.value)} placeholder="ops@example.com" />
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-[100px_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-2">
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                <Label htmlFor="ch-method">Method</Label>
                <select id="ch-method" className={SELECT} value={method} onChange={(e) => setMethod(e.target.value as 'POST' | 'PUT')}>
                  <option value="POST">POST</option>
                  <option value="PUT">PUT</option>
                </select>
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                <Label htmlFor="ch-url">URL{editing ? ` (now ${channel.webhook?.url})` : ''}</Label>
                <Input id="ch-url" className="font-mono" value={url} onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder={keep ?? 'https://hooks.example.com/…'} required={!editing} />
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-muted-foreground">Headers</span>
            {headers.map((h, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,0.8fr)_minmax(0,1fr)_32px] gap-2">
                <Input aria-label={`Header ${i + 1} name`} className="font-mono" value={h.name} onChange={(e) => setHeaders(headers.map((x, j) => (j === i ? { ...x, name: e.target.value } : x)))} />
                <Input
                  aria-label={`Header ${i + 1} value`}
                  type="password"
                  value={h.value}
                  placeholder={keep}
                  onChange={(e) => setHeaders(headers.map((x, j) => (j === i ? { ...x, value: e.target.value } : x)))}
                />
                <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Remove header ${i + 1}`} onClick={() => setHeaders(headers.filter((_, j) => j !== i))}>
                  <Trash2 size={13} />
                </Button>
              </div>
            ))}
            <Button type="button" variant="ghost" className="self-start" onClick={() => setHeaders([...headers, { name: '', value: '' }])}>
              <Plus size={13} /> Add header
            </Button>
            <Label htmlFor="ch-template">Body template (empty sends the event as JSON)</Label>
            <textarea id="ch-template" className={TEXTAREA} value={template} onChange={(e) => setTemplate(e.target.value)} placeholder='{"text": {{json .Schedule.Name}}}' />
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={signed} onChange={(e) => setSigned(e.target.checked)} />
              Sign requests (X-Pulsix-Signature-256)
            </label>
            {signed && (
              <>
                <Label htmlFor="ch-secret">Signing secret (16–256 characters)</Label>
                <Input id="ch-secret" type="password" value={secret} onChange={(e) => setSecret(e.target.value)} placeholder={channel?.webhook?.signed ? keep : undefined} />
              </>
            )}
          </>
        )}
        {error && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-[var(--px-crit)]">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={create.isPending || update.isPending}>
            Save
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/notifications/DeliveryLog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Dialog } from '../../ui/dialog.tsx';
import { Pill, type Tone } from '../../ui/badge.tsx';
import { QueryLoading } from '../../ui/QueryState.tsx';
import { useChannelDeliveries } from '../../api/hooks/useNotifications.ts';
import type { NotifyChannel } from '../../api/types.ts';

const STATUS_TONE: Record<string, Tone> = { sent: 'ok', pending: 'warn', failed: 'crit' };

/** DeliveryLog shows a channel's newest deliveries: what was sent, how
 * often it was tried, and the last error of a retried or failed one. */
export function DeliveryLog({ org, channel, onClose }: { org: string; channel: NotifyChannel; onClose: () => void }) {
  const log = useChannelDeliveries(org, channel.id);
  const rows = log.data?.deliveries ?? [];
  return (
    <Dialog open onClose={onClose} title={`Deliveries · ${channel.name}`} size="wide">
      {!log.data ? (
        <QueryLoading />
      ) : rows.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Nothing sent through this channel yet.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="flex flex-col divide-y divide-border">
          {rows.map((d) => (
            <li key={d.id} className="grid grid-cols-[140px_130px_70px_minmax(0,1fr)] items-center gap-3 py-2 text-xs" data-delivery>
              <span className="text-muted-foreground">{new Date(d.created_at).toLocaleString()}</span>
              <span className="font-mono">{d.event_kind}</span>
              <span>
                <Pill tone={STATUS_TONE[d.status] ?? 'unknown'}>{d.status}</Pill>
              </span>
              <span className="truncate text-muted-foreground" title={d.last_error}>
                {d.attempts > 1 ? `${d.attempts} attempts` : ''}
                {d.last_error ? ` · ${d.last_error}` : ''}
                {d.status === 'pending' && d.next_attempt_at ? ` · next try ${new Date(d.next_attempt_at).toLocaleTimeString()}` : ''}
              </span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/notifications/ChannelsPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useParams } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { Bell } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card, GridHeader, GridRow } from '../../ui/table.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Pill } from '../../ui/badge.tsx';
import { QueryLoading } from '../../ui/QueryState.tsx';
import { SettingsTabs } from '../../ui/SettingsTabs.tsx';
import { useQueryGuard } from '../../ui/useQueryGuard.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { useOrg } from '../../api/hooks/useOrgs.ts';
import { useChannels, useDeleteChannel, useTestChannel } from '../../api/hooks/useNotifications.ts';
import { may } from '../../lib/roles.ts';
import type { NotifyChannel } from '../../api/types.ts';
import { ChannelDialog } from './ChannelDialog.tsx';
import { DeliveryLog } from './DeliveryLog.tsx';

const COLUMNS = 'minmax(0, 0.8fr) 90px minmax(0, 1.2fr) 90px 260px';

function target(c: NotifyChannel): string {
  if (c.email) return (c.email.recipients ?? []).join(', ');
  return c.webhook ? `${c.webhook.method} ${c.webhook.url}` : '';
}

/** ChannelsPage is Settings → Notifications (spec 2026-09-18 §5): the
 * organization's e-mail and webhook channels with Send test and each
 * channel's delivery log. Operators see them; admins manage them. */
export function ChannelsPage() {
  const { org } = useParams({ strict: false }) as { org: string };
  const toast = useToast();
  const orgInfo = useOrg(org);
  const role = orgInfo.data?.org.role;
  const canRead = may(role, 'channels.read');
  const canWrite = may(role, 'channels.write');
  const channels = useChannels(org, !!orgInfo.data && canRead);
  const test = useTestChannel(org);
  const remove = useDeleteChannel(org);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<NotifyChannel | 'new' | null>(null);
  const [logOf, setLogOf] = useState<NotifyChannel | null>(null);

  const guard = useQueryGuard(orgInfo);
  if (guard) return guard;
  if (!orgInfo.data) return <QueryLoading />;
  const header = (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Notifications</h1>
      <SettingsTabs org={org} />
    </div>
  );
  if (!canRead) {
    return (
      <>
        {header}
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Notification channels are visible to operators and above.</p>
      </>
    );
  }
  if (!channels.data) return <QueryLoading />;
  const list = channels.data.channels ?? [];
  const mailAvailable = channels.data.mail_available;

  async function onTest(c: NotifyChannel) {
    try {
      const res = await test.mutateAsync(c.id);
      if (res.delivery.status === 'sent') toast.show(`Test message sent through ${c.name}`);
      else toast.show(`Test through ${c.name} failed: ${res.delivery.last_error ?? 'unknown error'}`, 'destructive');
    } catch (err) {
      toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Sending the test failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  async function onDelete(c: NotifyChannel) {
    if (!confirm(`Delete channel ${c.name}? Schedules stop notifying it and its delivery log is removed.`)) return;
    try {
      await remove.mutateAsync(c.id);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Deleting failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      {header}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">Where schedule results and the weekly digest are sent</span>
        {canWrite && (
          <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setEditing('new')}>
            <Bell size={15} /> Add channel
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
      {!mailAvailable && (
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground" role="note">
          E-mail channels need the instance's outgoing mail settings, which an instance administrator sets under Settings → Outgoing mail.
        </p>
      )}
      <Card>
        <GridHeader columns={COLUMNS}>
          <span>Channel</span>
          <span>Kind</span>
          <span>Sends to</span>
          <span>Status</span>
          <span />
        </GridHeader>
        {list.map((c) => (
          <GridRow key={c.id} columns={COLUMNS}>
            <span className="truncate text-[13px] font-medium" data-row>
              {c.name}
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs">{c.kind === 'email' ? 'E-mail' : 'Webhook'}</span>
            <span className="truncate font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">{target(c)}</span>
            <span>{c.enabled ? <Pill tone="ok">enabled</Pill> : <Pill tone="unknown">disabled</Pill>}</span>
            <span className="flex justify-end gap-1">
              <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Deliveries of ${c.name}`} onClick={() => setLogOf(c)}>
                Log
              </Button>
              {canWrite && (
                <>
                  <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Send test through ${c.name}`} onClick={() => void onTest(c)} disabled={test.isPending}>
                    Send test
                  </Button>
                  <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Edit ${c.name}`} onClick={() => setEditing(c)}>
                    Edit
                  </Button>
                  <Button type="button" variant="destructive" aria-label={`Delete ${c.name}`} onClick={() => void onDelete(c)}>
                    Delete
                  </Button>
                </>
              )}
            </span>
          </GridRow>
        ))}
        {list.length === 0 && <p className="px-4 py-6 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">No channels yet.</p>}
      </Card>
      {editing && <ChannelDialog org={org} channel={editing === 'new' ? undefined : editing} mailAvailable={mailAvailable} onClose={() => setEditing(null)} />}
      {logOf && <DeliveryLog org={org} channel={logOf} onClose={() => setLogOf(null)} />}
    </>
  );
}
```

Replace `web/src/ui/SettingsTabs.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { useMe } from '../api/hooks/useMe.ts';
import { useOrg } from '../api/hooks/useOrgs.ts';
import { may } from '../lib/roles.ts';

const ACTIVE = 'border-b-2 border-primary py-2.5 text-[13px] font-semibold text-foreground';
const INACTIVE = 'border-b-2 border-transparent py-2.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-muted-foreground';

/**
 * SettingsTabs is the tab strip Members.dc.html/Activity.dc.html show at
 * the top of the Settings section (design's `tab()` generator: an
 * underline on the active label). Real router links, not a client-only
 * tab switch, so the URL, breadcrumbs and browser back button all stay in
 * sync with which tab is open — finding "Important 6": Members and
 * Activity moved under one Settings section (task-15a-report.md fix
 * round 1), and this is how a page in that section gets back to its
 * siblings.
 *
 * "Outgoing mail" is instance-wide and shown only to instance
 * administrators — the only callers GET /admin/settings/smtp admits.
 * "Notifications" is shown to the roles that may read channels
 * (channels.read, operator and above).
 */
export function SettingsTabs({ org }: { org: string }) {
  const me = useMe({ enabled: true });
  const orgInfo = useOrg(org);
  return (
    <div role="tablist" className="flex gap-6 border-b border-border">
      <Link
        to="/o/$org/settings/members"
        params={{ org }}
        role="tab"
        activeProps={{ className: ACTIVE, 'aria-selected': true }}
        inactiveProps={{ className: INACTIVE, 'aria-selected': false }}
      >
        Members
      </Link>
      <Link
        to="/o/$org/settings/activity"
        params={{ org }}
        role="tab"
        activeProps={{ className: ACTIVE, 'aria-selected': true }}
        inactiveProps={{ className: INACTIVE, 'aria-selected': false }}
      >
        Activity
      </Link>
      <Link
        to="/o/$org/settings/tokens"
        params={{ org }}
        role="tab"
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Docs**

Create `~/_dev/noix-docs/src/content/docs/pulsix/user/notifications.md` (Tasks 9 and 10 add their sections):

````markdown
---
title: Notifications
description: E-mail and webhook channels, what Pulsix sends through them, and the delivery log
sidebar:
  order: 4.7
---

A *channel* is where Pulsix sends a message: an e-mail list or any HTTP endpoint
that accepts a webhook. Channels are shared by the whole organization and managed
under *Settings → Notifications*. Schedules decide what goes to which channel (see
[Schedule notifications](#schedule-notifications)).

## Channels

| Kind | What it sends |
|---|---|
| **E-mail** | One plain-text message to every recipient of the channel (up to 20 addresses), through the instance's outgoing mail settings. |
| **Webhook** | An HTTP `POST` (or `PUT`) to a URL of your choice, with your own headers and an optional signature. |

E-mail channels need the instance's outgoing mail settings. Without them the e-mail
kind cannot be chosen, and the page says so. An instance administrator sets them up
under *Settings → Outgoing mail*.

A webhook's URL, its header values and its signing secret are stored encrypted and
are never shown again. The channel list shows only the URL's scheme and host. When
you edit a channel, leave these fields empty to keep what is stored.

### Webhook body

Without a body template, the body is the message as JSON:

```json
{
  "kind": "run.finished",
  "at": "2026-09-22T01:04:12Z",
  "org": { "id": "…", "slug": "acme", "name": "Acme" },
  "categories": ["failure", "change"],
  "schedule": { "id": "…", "name": "Nightly OS patching" },
  "template": { "id": "…", "name": "patch-all" },
  "run": {
    "id": "…", "result": "failed", "hosts": 12, "hosts_ok": 11,
    "hosts_changed": 9, "hosts_failed": 1,
    "started_at": "…", "finished_at": "…", "duration_seconds": 252
  },
  "url": "https://pulsix.example.com/o/acme/runs/…"
}
```

`kind` is `run.finished`, `schedule.skipped`, `schedule.failed`, `digest.weekly` or
`test`. Only the sections that belong to the kind are present: `run` for a finished
run, `decision` (`outcome`, `reason`, `due_at`) for a skipped or failed occurrence,
and `digest` for the weekly digest.

A **body template** shapes the body for a receiver that expects its own format, such
as a chat tool. Placeholders use the field names in their capitalized form:
`{{ .Kind }}`, `{{ .Schedule.Name }}`, `{{ .Run.Result }}`, `{{ .Run.HostsFailed }}`,
`{{ .Decision.Reason }}`, `{{ .URL }}`. Wrap a value in `json` so quotes and line
breaks stay valid JSON. A channel can receive every kind of message, and a message
fails when the template reads a section it does not carry, so guard sections with
`with`:

```text
{"text": {{ json (printf "Pulsix %s" .Kind) }}{{ with .Schedule }}, "schedule": {{ json .Name }}{{ end }}{{ with .Run }}, "result": {{ json .Result }}{{ end }}, "link": {{ json .URL }}}
```

Pulsix checks a template when you save the channel. A misspelled field is refused
right away, not with the first real message.

### Headers and signature

Every request carries:

| Header | Content |
|---|---|
| `X-Pulsix-Event` | the message kind, for example `run.finished` |
| `X-Pulsix-Delivery` | an id that stays the same when the message is sent again |
| `X-Pulsix-Timestamp` | when the request was made (Unix seconds) |
| `X-Pulsix-Signature-256` | with signing enabled: `sha256=` and the hex HMAC-SHA256 of `<timestamp>.<body>` with the channel's signing secret |

To verify a request, compute the HMAC over the timestamp header, a dot and the raw
body, compare it in constant time, and refuse timestamps older than a few minutes.
Your own headers (for example `Authorization`) are added to every request; the
`X-Pulsix-*` names and transport headers such as `Host` cannot be set.

## Send test

*Send test* sends an example message through the channel right away and shows the
receiver's answer. The attempt appears in the channel's delivery log like any other
message.

## Delivery and the delivery log

Messages are queued and sent within seconds. If a receiver is unreachable or answers
with a server error, `408` or `429`, Pulsix tries again after 30 seconds, 2 minutes,
8 minutes, 32 minutes and about 2 hours, and then gives up: six attempts in total.
Messages that cannot succeed are not retried: other `4xx` answers, redirects, an
address Pulsix refuses to contact, a body template that does not render, and a
disabled channel.

A message can arrive twice in rare cases, for example when a manager restarts while
a receiver was accepting it. Receivers that must not act twice should ignore a
repeated `X-Pulsix-Delivery` id.

*Log* on a channel shows its newest deliveries: what was sent, whether it was sent,
how often it was tried, and the last error. Each channel keeps its newest 500
finished deliveries.

For which addresses Pulsix contacts at all, see [Security → Outgoing notifications](/pulsix/admin/security/#outgoing-notifications).

## Who may do what

**Operators** see the channels and their delivery logs, and pick channels for their
schedules. **Admins** add, edit, test and delete channels.
````

In `reference/api.md`, add after the "Schedules" section:

```markdown
## Notification channels

Channels live under `/api/v1/orgs/{slug}/notification-channels`. Operators can read
them and their delivery log; admins create, edit, test and delete them.

| Method | Path | Success |
|---|---|---|
| GET | `/notification-channels` | 200 `{channels: [...], mail_available}` |
| POST | `/notification-channels` | 201 `{channel}` + `ETag` |
| GET | `/notification-channels/{id}` | 200 `{channel}` + `ETag` |
| PUT | `/notification-channels/{id}` | 200 + `ETag` — requires `If-Match` |
| DELETE | `/notification-channels/{id}` | 200 `{}` |
| POST | `/notification-channels/{id}/test` | 200 `{delivery}` — `status` is `sent` or `failed` with `last_error` |
| GET | `/notification-channels/{id}/deliveries?limit=` | 200 `{deliveries: [...]}` — newest first, `limit` 1–100 |

A webhook's `url`, header `value`s and `signing_secret` are write-only: responses show
the URL as scheme and host and header names only; leaving them out of a `PUT` keeps
the stored values. Creating an e-mail channel on an instance without outgoing mail
answers `409`.
```

### Task 5: Run groups in the job queue: completion sink, host exclusion, run targets, summaries, trigger filters

The job queue gains everything the later tasks ask of it. It reports a run group once, when its last job becomes terminal (the notification source, spec §7). A run request can leave hosts out (the `exclude_hosts` guard, §6). It answers which hosts a run would target, and it summarises run groups for the digest and the calendar (§7, §8). The runs list filters by trigger kind and trigger reference (§8). No notification code yet: the sink is a port.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/jobs/run_group.go`, `internal/jobs/group_summary.go`
- Test: `internal/jobs/run_group_test.go` (internal, against the database), `internal/jobs/run_exclude_test.go`
