- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing run-group tests**

`internal/jobs/run_group_test.go` (package `jobs`, reusing `testZone` from `terminal_paths_test.go`):

```go
package jobs

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"errors"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/testdb"
)

// recordingGroupSink records every finished run group; err is returned
// from each call.
type recordingGroupSink struct {
	mu    sync.Mutex
	calls []RunGroupResult
	err   error
}

func (s *recordingGroupSink) RunGroupFinished(_ context.Context, r RunGroupResult) error {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.calls = append(s.calls, r)
	return s.err
}

func (s *recordingGroupSink) snapshot() []RunGroupResult {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]RunGroupResult(nil), s.calls...)
}

type groupWorld struct {
	d    *db.DB
	org  uuid.UUID
	q    *SQLQueue
	sink *recordingGroupSink
}

func newGroupWorld(t *testing.T, slug string) *groupWorld {
	t.Helper()
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	org := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	if _, err := gen.New(d.Pool()).CreateOrganization(context.Background(), gen.CreateOrganizationParams{ID: org, Slug: slug, Name: slug}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	q := NewSQLQueue(d, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, time.Now, rand.Reader)
	sink := &recordingGroupSink{}
	q.SetRunGroupSink(sink)
	return &groupWorld{d: d, org: org, q: q, sink: sink}
}

// runGroup inserts one scheduled run group with one job per zone, each
// targeting one host and already running.
func (w *groupWorld) runGroup(t *testing.T, zones int) (uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID, []Job) {
	t.Helper()
	group, schedule, tmpl := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	var out []Job
	for i := 0; i < zones; i++ {
		j := Job{
			ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: w.org, ZoneID: testZone(t, w.d, w.org), Kind: KindRun, TemplateID: &tmpl,
			HostIDs: []uuid.UUID{uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())}, Status: StatusQueued, RunGroupID: group,
			Trigger: TriggerSchedule, TriggerRef: &schedule, CreatedAt: time.Now().Add(time.Duration(i) * time.Millisecond),
		}
		if err := NewSQLStore(w.d).CreateJob(db.WithOrgScope(context.Background(), w.org), j, nil); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		out = append(out, j)
	}
	for _, j := range out {
		if _, err := w.d.Pool().Exec(context.Background(), `UPDATE jobs SET status = 'running', started_at = now() WHERE id = $1`, j.ID); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	return group, schedule, out
}

func TestRunGroupReportedOnceWhenItsLastJobFinishes(t *testing.T) {
	w := newGroupWorld(t, "rungroup-once")
	group, schedule, js := w.runGroup(t, 2)
	// The worker writes JSON null for an empty host list (a nil slice).
	if err := w.q.Finish(context.Background(), js[0].ID, StatusSucceeded,
		map[string]any{"changed_hosts": []string{js[0].HostIDs[0].String()}, "failed_hosts": nil}, ""); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := w.sink.snapshot(); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("reported before the group finished: %+v", got)
	}
	if err := w.q.Finish(context.Background(), js[1].ID, StatusFailed,
		map[string]any{"changed_hosts": nil, "unreachable_hosts": []string{js[1].HostIDs[0].String()}}, "exited with code 4"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got := w.sink.snapshot()
	if len(got) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("reports = %d, want 1", len(got))
	}
	r := got[0]
	if r.GroupID != group || r.OrgID != w.org || r.FirstJobID != js[0].ID || r.Status != StatusFailed || r.Jobs != 2 ||
		r.Hosts != 2 || r.HostsChanged != 1 || r.HostsFailed != 1 || r.HostsOK() != 1 ||
		r.Trigger != TriggerSchedule || r.TriggerRef == nil || *r.TriggerRef != schedule || r.StartedAt == nil || r.FinishedAt == nil {
		t.Fatalf("result = %+v", r)
	}
	// A repeated finish is the idempotent no-op and reports nothing more.
	if err := w.q.Finish(context.Background(), js[1].ID, StatusFailed, nil, ""); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if n := len(w.sink.snapshot()); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("reports after a repeated finish = %d", n)
	}
}

func TestConcurrentFinishesReportTheGroupOnce(t *testing.T) {
	w := newGroupWorld(t, "rungroup-race")
	for round := 0; round < 5; round++ {
		_, _, js := w.runGroup(t, 3)
		var wg sync.WaitGroup
		for _, j := range js {
			wg.Add(1)
			go func() {
				defer wg.Done()
				if err := w.q.Finish(context.Background(), j.ID, StatusSucceeded, nil, ""); err != nil {
					t.Error(err)
				}
			}()
		}
		wg.Wait()
		if n := len(w.sink.snapshot()); n != round+1 {
			t.Fatalf("round %d: reports = %d, want %d", round, n, round+1)
		}
	}
}

func TestCancellingTheLastQueuedJobCompletesTheGroup(t *testing.T) {
	w := newGroupWorld(t, "rungroup-cancel")
	_, _, js := w.runGroup(t, 2)
	if _, err := w.d.Pool().Exec(context.Background(), `UPDATE jobs SET status = 'queued', started_at = NULL WHERE id = $1`, js[1].ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := w.q.Finish(context.Background(), js[0].ID, StatusSucceeded, nil, ""); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	scoped := db.WithOrgScope(context.Background(), w.org)
	if status, err := NewSQLStore(w.d).CancelJob(scoped, w.org, js[1].ID); err != nil || status != StatusCancelled {
		t.Fatalf("cancel = %s, %v", status, err)
	}
	w.q.ClearTerminalPayload(scoped, js[1].ID)
	got := w.sink.snapshot()
	if len(got) != 1 || got[0].Status != StatusCancelled {
		t.Fatalf("reports = %+v, want one cancelled group", got)
	}
}

func TestRunGroupSinkErrorDoesNotFailTheFinish(t *testing.T) {
	w := newGroupWorld(t, "rungroup-sinkerr")
	w.sink.err = errors.New("outbox down")
	_, _, js := w.runGroup(t, 1)
	if err := w.q.Finish(context.Background(), js[0].ID, StatusSucceeded, nil, ""); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Finish = %v, want nil although the sink failed", err)
	}
	var status string
	if err := w.d.Pool().QueryRow(context.Background(), `SELECT status FROM jobs WHERE id = $1`, js[0].ID).Scan(&status); err != nil || status != "succeeded" {
		t.Fatalf("status = %s, %v", status, err)
	}
}

func TestNonRunJobsAreNotReported(t *testing.T) {
	w := newGroupWorld(t, "rungroup-kinds")
	j := Job{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: w.org, ZoneID: testZone(t, w.d, w.org), Kind: KindAdhoc,
		HostIDs: []uuid.UUID{uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())}, Status: StatusQueued, RunGroupID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Trigger: TriggerManual, CreatedAt: time.Now()}
	if err := NewSQLStore(w.d).CreateJob(db.WithOrgScope(context.Background(), w.org), j, nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := w.d.Pool().Exec(context.Background(), `UPDATE jobs SET status = 'running' WHERE id = $1`, j.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := w.q.Finish(context.Background(), j.ID, StatusSucceeded, nil, ""); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if n := len(w.sink.snapshot()); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("an ad-hoc job was reported as a run group (%d)", n)
	}
}

func TestGroupSummariesAggregateStatusAndTiming(t *testing.T) {
	w := newGroupWorld(t, "rungroup-summary")
	failedGroup, _, fj := w.runGroup(t, 2)
	runningGroup, _, rj := w.runGroup(t, 2)
	queuedGroup, _, qj := w.runGroup(t, 1)
	if _, err := w.d.Pool().Exec(context.Background(), `UPDATE jobs SET status = 'queued', started_at = NULL WHERE id = $1`, qj[0].ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := w.q.Finish(context.Background(), fj[0].ID, StatusSucceeded, nil, ""); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := w.q.Finish(context.Background(), fj[1].ID, StatusError, nil, "boom"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := w.q.Finish(context.Background(), rj[0].ID, StatusSucceeded, nil, ""); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got, err := NewSQLStore(w.d).GroupSummaries(db.WithOrgScope(context.Background(), w.org), w.org, []uuid.UUID{failedGroup, runningGroup, queuedGroup, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(got) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("summaries = %d, want 3", len(got))
	}
	if g := got[failedGroup]; g.Status != StatusFailed || g.FirstJobID != fj[0].ID || g.FinishedAt == nil || g.Duration() <= 0 {
		t.Fatalf("failed group = %+v", g)
	}
	if g := got[runningGroup]; g.Status != StatusRunning || g.Duration() != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("running group = %+v", g)
	}
	if g := got[queuedGroup]; g.Status != StatusQueued || g.StartedAt != nil {
		t.Fatalf("queued group = %+v", g)
	}
}

func TestCancelledRunGroupCheckIgnoresOtherJobs(t *testing.T) {
	w := newGroupWorld(t, "rungroup-cancel-kinds")
	scoped := db.WithOrgScope(context.Background(), w.org)
	// An unknown job is logged and ignored.
	w.q.recordCancelledRunGroup(scoped, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()))
	// A cancelled ad-hoc job is no run group.
	j := Job{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: w.org, ZoneID: testZone(t, w.d, w.org), Kind: KindAdhoc,
		HostIDs: []uuid.UUID{uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())}, Status: StatusQueued, RunGroupID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Trigger: TriggerManual, CreatedAt: time.Now()}
	if err := NewSQLStore(w.d).CreateJob(scoped, j, nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := NewSQLStore(w.d).CancelJob(scoped, w.org, j.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	w.q.ClearTerminalPayload(scoped, j.ID)
	// Without a sink nothing is checked at all.
	w.q.SetRunGroupSink(nil)
	_, _, js := w.runGroup(t, 1)
	if _, err := w.d.Pool().Exec(context.Background(), `UPDATE jobs SET status = 'queued', started_at = NULL WHERE id = $1`, js[0].ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := NewSQLStore(w.d).CancelJob(scoped, w.org, js[0].ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	w.q.ClearTerminalPayload(scoped, js[0].ID)
	if n := len(w.sink.snapshot()); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("reports = %d, want none", n)
	}
}
```

`internal/jobs/run_exclude_test.go` (package `jobs_test`, reusing `runFixture`):

```go
package jobs_test

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
)

func TestRunTargetsListsEveryZonesHosts(t *testing.T) {

	}); !errors.Is(err, jobs.ErrAllHostsExcluded) {

}

func TestGroupSummaryDurationAndHostsOK(t *testing.T) {
	start := time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 1, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	end := start.Add(4 * time.Minute)
	if d := (jobs.GroupSummary{Status: jobs.StatusSucceeded, StartedAt: &start, FinishedAt: &end}).Duration(); d != 4*time.Minute {
		t.Fatalf("finished duration = %s", d)
	}
	if d := (jobs.GroupSummary{Status: jobs.StatusRunning, StartedAt: &start, FinishedAt: &end}).Duration(); d != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("running duration = %s, want 0", d)
	}
	if n := (jobs.RunGroupResult{Hosts: 1, HostsFailed: 3}).HostsOK(); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("HostsOK = %d, want 0 when a host counts in several failure lists", n)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The run-group sink**

`internal/jobs/run_group.go`:

```go
// Run groups: one run of a job template is split into one job per zone,
// all sharing run_group_id. Notifications (spec 2026-09-18 §7) are about
// the run, not a zone's job, so the queue reports a run group once — when
// its last job becomes terminal — through the optional RunGroupSink.
package jobs

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// RunGroupResult summarises a run group whose jobs are all terminal.
// Host counts come from the per-host results the worker stored in each
// job's stats (changed_hosts, failed_hosts, unreachable_hosts).
type RunGroupResult struct {
	OrgID, GroupID uuid.UUID
	// FirstJobID is the group's oldest job: the run view link.
	FirstJobID uuid.UUID
	TemplateID *uuid.UUID
	Trigger    string
	TriggerRef *uuid.UUID
	// Status aggregates the jobs: failed when any failed or errored,
	// otherwise cancelled when any was cancelled, otherwise succeeded.
	Status                                 Status
	Jobs, Hosts, HostsChanged, HostsFailed int
	StartedAt, FinishedAt                  *time.Time
}

// HostsOK is the number of targeted hosts that did not fail.
func (r RunGroupResult) HostsOK() int { return max(r.Hosts-r.HostsFailed, 0) }

// RunGroupSink receives each finished run group exactly once per terminal
// transition that completes it; ctx carries the finishing transaction, so
// what the sink writes commits with the job's terminal status. A sink
// error never fails the finish: it is logged and the notification lost —
// a notification must not wedge a job in running.
type RunGroupSink interface {
	RunGroupFinished(ctx context.Context, r RunGroupResult) error
}

// SetRunGroupSink injects the finished-run-group port (the notification
// outbox), the same post-construction wiring the other sinks use. Nil
// reports nothing and takes no group lock for run jobs.
func (q *SQLQueue) SetRunGroupSink(sink RunGroupSink) { q.runGroups = sink }

// runGroupQuery reads a run group inside the finishing transaction.
// jsonb_typeof guards the stats lists: a worker that found no host in a
// state writes JSON null there (a nil Go slice), not an empty array.
const runGroupQuery = `
WITH g AS (
  SELECT id, status, host_ids, started_at, finished_at, created_at, template_id, trigger, trigger_ref, stats
  FROM jobs WHERE org_id = $1 AND run_group_id = $2 AND kind = 'run'
),
lists AS (
  SELECT
    CASE WHEN jsonb_typeof(stats->'changed_hosts') = 'array' THEN stats->'changed_hosts' ELSE '[]'::jsonb END AS changed,
    (CASE WHEN jsonb_typeof(stats->'failed_hosts') = 'array' THEN stats->'failed_hosts' ELSE '[]'::jsonb END) ||
    (CASE WHEN jsonb_typeof(stats->'unreachable_hosts') = 'array' THEN stats->'unreachable_hosts' ELSE '[]'::jsonb END) AS failed
  FROM g
)
SELECT
  (SELECT count(*) FROM g),
  (SELECT count(*) FROM g WHERE status IN ('succeeded', 'failed', 'cancelled', 'error')),
  (SELECT count(*) FROM g WHERE status IN ('failed', 'error')),
  (SELECT count(*) FROM g WHERE status = 'cancelled'),
  (SELECT COALESCE(sum(cardinality(host_ids)), 0) FROM g),
  (SELECT count(DISTINCT h) FROM lists, jsonb_array_elements_text(lists.changed) AS h),
  (SELECT count(DISTINCT h) FROM lists, jsonb_array_elements_text(lists.failed) AS h),
  (SELECT min(started_at) FROM g),
  (SELECT max(finished_at) FROM g),
  (SELECT id FROM g ORDER BY created_at, id LIMIT 1),
  (SELECT template_id FROM g LIMIT 1),
  (SELECT trigger FROM g LIMIT 1),
  (SELECT trigger_ref FROM g LIMIT 1)`

// recordRunGroup hands a completed run group to the sink, inside the
// finishing transaction, which already holds the group's advisory lock
// (terminate): with the lock, the job that completes the group always
// sees every other member terminal, and two members finishing together
// cannot both miss the completion.
func (q *SQLQueue) recordRunGroup(ctx context.Context, jobID uuid.UUID) error {
	if q.runGroups == nil {
		return nil
	}
	var orgID, groupID uuid.UUID
	var kind string
	if err := q.db.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT org_id, kind, run_group_id FROM jobs WHERE id = $1`, jobID).Scan(&orgID, &kind, &groupID); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("record run group: look up job: %w", err)
	}
	if Kind(kind) != KindRun {
		return nil
	}
	r := RunGroupResult{OrgID: orgID, GroupID: groupID}
	var total, terminal, failed, cancelled int
	var first *uuid.UUID
	if err := q.db.QueryRow(ctx, runGroupQuery, orgID, groupID).Scan(
		&total, &terminal, &failed, &cancelled, &r.Hosts, &r.HostsChanged, &r.HostsFailed,
		&r.StartedAt, &r.FinishedAt, &first, &r.TemplateID, &r.Trigger, &r.TriggerRef,
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("record run group: read group: %w", err)
	}
	if total == 0 || terminal < total || first == nil {
		return nil // the group is not done yet
	}
	r.Jobs, r.FirstJobID = total, *first
	switch {
	case failed > 0:
		r.Status = StatusFailed
	case cancelled > 0:
		r.Status = StatusCancelled
	default:
		r.Status = StatusSucceeded
	}
	if err := q.runGroups.RunGroupFinished(ctx, r); err != nil {
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "finished run group: the notification sink failed; its notifications are lost", "group", groupID, "org", orgID, "err", err)
	}
	return nil
}

// recordCancelledRunGroup is recordRunGroup for a job cancelled before it
// was claimed: that cancel is a single UPDATE (CancelJob), not a terminal
// transition, so the group check runs in its own transaction under the
// same group lock terminate takes.
func (q *SQLQueue) recordCancelledRunGroup(ctx context.Context, jobID uuid.UUID) {
	if q.runGroups == nil {
		return
	}
	var orgID, groupID uuid.UUID
	var kind string
	if err := q.db.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT org_id, kind, run_group_id FROM jobs WHERE id = $1`, jobID).Scan(&orgID, &kind, &groupID); err != nil {
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "cancelled run group: look up job", "job", jobID, "err", err)
		return
	}
	if Kind(kind) != KindRun {
		return
	}
	if err := q.db.WithWriteTx(ctx, orgID, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		if _, err := q.db.Exec(ctx, `SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))`, finishGroupLockKeyPrefix+groupID.String()); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("lock run group: %w", err)
		}
		return q.recordRunGroup(ctx, jobID)
	}); err != nil {
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "cancelled run group: record", "job", jobID, "err", err)
	}
}
```

```go
// RunTargets resolves the template's scope into the managed host ids a
// run started now would target (every zone together), before the
// changed-host-key exclusion. The maintenance guard asks it which hosts a
// scheduled run would touch.
func (s *Service) RunTargets(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID) ([]uuid.UUID, error) {
	tmpl, err := s.templates.TemplateLookup(ctx, orgID, templateID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	scope, err := parseScope(tmpl.Scope)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	targets, err := s.resolver.Resolve(ctx, orgID, scope)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var out []uuid.UUID
	for _, t := range targets {
		out = append(out, t.HostIDs...)
	}
	return out, nil
}

// excludeHosts drops the given hosts from per-zone targets; a zone left
// without hosts is dropped too.
func excludeHosts(targets []ZoneTargets, exclude []uuid.UUID) []ZoneTargets {
	if len(exclude) == 0 {
		return targets
	}
	drop := make(map[uuid.UUID]bool, len(exclude))
	for _, id := range exclude {
		drop[id] = true
	}
	out := make([]ZoneTargets, 0, len(targets))
	for _, t := range targets {
		kept := t.HostIDs[:0:0]
		for _, id := range t.HostIDs {
			if !drop[id] {
				kept = append(kept, id)
			}
		}
		if len(kept) > 0 {
			out = append(out, ZoneTargets{ZoneID: t.ZoneID, HostIDs: kept})
		}
	}
	return out
}
```

`internal/jobs/group_summary.go`:

```go
package jobs

import (
	"context"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// GroupSummary is one run group at a glance, for the calendar and the
// weekly digest: its first job (the run view link), an aggregate status
// and its time span.
type GroupSummary struct {
	GroupID, FirstJobID uuid.UUID
	// Status is queued while every job waits, running while any job is
	// not terminal, and otherwise the RunGroupResult aggregate (failed,
	// cancelled, succeeded).
	Status                Status
	StartedAt, FinishedAt *time.Time
}

// Duration is FinishedAt - StartedAt for a finished group, zero otherwise.
func (g GroupSummary) Duration() time.Duration {
	if g.StartedAt == nil || g.FinishedAt == nil || !g.Status.Terminal() {
		return 0
	}
	return g.FinishedAt.Sub(*g.StartedAt)
}

// GroupSummaries summarises the given run groups of the organization.
func (s *Service) GroupSummaries(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, groupIDs []uuid.UUID) (map[uuid.UUID]GroupSummary, error) {
	if len(groupIDs) == 0 {
		return map[uuid.UUID]GroupSummary{}, nil
	}
	return s.store.GroupSummaries(ctx, orgID, groupIDs)
}
```

```go

		TriggerRef: uuidToPg(f.TriggerRef),
```

and add before the pgtype helpers:

```go
// groupSummaryQuery aggregates run groups; raw SQL because the
// aggregates' NULLs (a group that never started) scan into pointers.
const groupSummaryQuery = `
SELECT run_group_id,
  (array_agg(id ORDER BY created_at, id))[1],
  count(*),
  count(*) FILTER (WHERE status IN ('succeeded', 'failed', 'cancelled', 'error')),
  count(*) FILTER (WHERE status IN ('queued', 'claimed')),
  count(*) FILTER (WHERE status IN ('failed', 'error')),
  count(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'cancelled'),
  min(started_at), max(finished_at)
FROM jobs
WHERE org_id = $1 AND kind = 'run' AND run_group_id = ANY($2::uuid[])
GROUP BY run_group_id`

func (s *SQLStore) GroupSummaries(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, groupIDs []uuid.UUID) (map[uuid.UUID]GroupSummary, error) {
	rows, err := s.d.Query(ctx, groupSummaryQuery, orgID, groupIDs)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("summarise run groups: %w", err)
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	out := map[uuid.UUID]GroupSummary{}
	for rows.Next() {
		var g GroupSummary
		var total, terminal, waiting, failed, cancelled int
		if err := rows.Scan(&g.GroupID, &g.FirstJobID, &total, &terminal, &waiting, &failed, &cancelled, &g.StartedAt, &g.FinishedAt); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("summarise run groups: %w", err)
		}
		switch {
		case waiting == total:
			g.Status = StatusQueued
		case terminal < total:
			g.Status = StatusRunning
		case failed > 0:
			g.Status = StatusFailed
		case cancelled > 0:
			g.Status = StatusCancelled
		default:
			g.Status = StatusSucceeded
		}
		out[g.GroupID] = g
	}
	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("summarise run groups: %w", err)
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

```go
// parseTriggerFilter validates the runs list's trigger filters (spec
// 2026-09-18 §8): a known trigger kind and a well-formed trigger id.
func parseTriggerFilter(kind, ref string) (*string, *uuid.UUID, error) {
	var k *string
	switch kind {
	case "":
	case jobs.TriggerManual, jobs.TriggerSchedule, jobs.TriggerWebhook, jobs.TriggerSystem:
		k = &kind
	default:
		return nil, nil, huma.Error400BadRequest("invalid trigger: must be one of manual, schedule, webhook, system")
	}
	if ref == "" {
		return k, nil, nil
	}
	id, err := uuid.Parse(ref)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, huma.Error400BadRequest("invalid trigger_ref")
	}
	return k, &id, nil
}
```

### Task 6: Maintenance windows (migration 00030, occurrences, service, store)

This task covers spec §6's `maintenance_windows`. A window covers a host, an inventory group (and its subgroups) or the whole organization. It is either one-off or recurring: a cron expression in a time zone, each occurrence lasting a duration, evaluated by `internal/cronexpr` like schedules. The service answers the two questions the rest of v0.2d asks: which of these hosts are in maintenance at this instant (the guard), and which stretches fall into this range (the calendar). The migration also widens `schedule_runs_outcome_check` for `skipped_maintenance`.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/db/migrations/00030_maintenance_windows.sql`, `internal/db/queries/maintenance.sql`
- Create: `internal/maintenance/window.go`, `occur.go`, `validate.go`, `service.go`, `store_sql.go`
- Test: `internal/maintenance/occur_test.go`, `validate_test.go`, `service_test.go`, `store_sql_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/tenancy/roles.go`, `internal/tenancy/roles_test.go` (`maintenance.read`, `maintenance.write`)
- Modify: `scripts/coverage-gate.sh` (add `internal/maintenance` to the 90 % list)
- Regenerate: `internal/db/gen`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (package `maintenance`):
  - sentinels `ErrNotFound`, `ErrInvalidInput`, `ErrNameTaken`, `ErrRevisionMismatch`
  - `type TargetKind string` (`TargetHost` `host`, `TargetGroup` `inventory_group`, `TargetOrg` `org`)
  - `type Window struct{ ID, OrgID uuid.UUID; Name string; TargetKind TargetKind; TargetID *uuid.UUID; TargetName string; StartsAt, EndsAt *time.Time; Cron string; Duration time.Duration; Timezone, Note string; CreatedBy *uuid.UUID; CreatedAt, UpdatedAt time.Time; Revision int64 }`, `func (w Window) Recurring() bool`, `func (w Window) DurationText() string`
  - `type Input struct{ Name string; TargetKind TargetKind; TargetID *uuid.UUID; StartsAt, EndsAt *time.Time; Cron, Duration, Timezone, Note string }` (`Duration` is a Go-style duration string such as `"90m"`)
  - `type Occurrence struct{ WindowID uuid.UUID; Name string; TargetKind TargetKind; TargetID *uuid.UUID; TargetName string; Start, End time.Time }`
  - `func ActiveAt(w Window, at time.Time) (bool, error)`, `func Occurrences(w Window, from, to time.Time, max int) ([]Occurrence, error)`
  - `type Store interface{ Create; Get; List; Update; Delete }`, `type GroupHosts interface{ HostsInGroup(ctx context.Context, orgID, groupID uuid.UUID) ([]uuid.UUID, error) }`
  - `func NewService(store Store, groups GroupHosts, now func() time.Time, entropy io.Reader, log *slog.Logger) *Service`
  - `Service.Create(ctx, orgID, actor uuid.UUID, in Input) (Window, error)`, `Get(ctx, orgID, id)`, `List(ctx, orgID) ([]Window, map[uuid.UUID]bool /*active now*/, error)`, `Update(ctx, orgID, id uuid.UUID, revision int64, in Input)`, `Delete(ctx, orgID, id)`
  - `Service.HostsInMaintenance(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, hostIDs []uuid.UUID, at time.Time) (map[uuid.UUID]bool, error)` (Task 8's `schedules.MaintenanceChecker`)
  - `Service.OccurrencesBetween(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, from, to time.Time) ([]Occurrence, error)` (Task 11's `calendar.Windows`)
  - `type SQLStore`, `func NewSQLStore(d *db.DB) *SQLStore`
- Produces (package `tenancy`): `PermMaintenanceRead` (`maintenance.read`, viewer), `PermMaintenanceWrite` (`maintenance.write`, operator).

- [ ] **Step 1: The migration and the queries**

`internal/db/migrations/00030_maintenance_windows.sql`:

```sql
-- +goose Up
-- Maintenance windows (spec 2026-09-18 §6). The target is a host, an
-- inventory group or the whole organization; host_id and group_id carry
-- the composite foreign keys a polymorphic target_id could not, and
-- deleting the target deletes its windows. A window is one-off
-- (starts_at/ends_at) or recurring (cron + duration_seconds + timezone,
-- evaluated like schedules). v0.4 adds monitoring-group targets and alert
-- silencing, v0.5 public announcements.
CREATE TABLE maintenance_windows (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  org_id           uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  name             text NOT NULL,
  target_kind      text NOT NULL CHECK (target_kind IN ('host', 'inventory_group', 'org')),
  host_id          uuid,
  group_id         uuid,
  starts_at        timestamptz,
  ends_at          timestamptz,
  cron             text,
  duration_seconds integer,
  timezone         text,
  note             text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  created_by       uuid,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  revision         bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  UNIQUE (org_id, name),
  UNIQUE (org_id, id),
  CHECK ((target_kind = 'host') = (host_id IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK ((target_kind = 'inventory_group') = (group_id IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK ((starts_at IS NULL) = (ends_at IS NULL)),
  CHECK ((cron IS NULL) = (starts_at IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK ((cron IS NULL) = (duration_seconds IS NULL) AND (cron IS NULL) = (timezone IS NULL)),
  CHECK (ends_at IS NULL OR ends_at > starts_at),
  CHECK (duration_seconds IS NULL OR duration_seconds BETWEEN 60 AND 2678400),
  FOREIGN KEY (org_id, host_id) REFERENCES hosts (org_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  FOREIGN KEY (org_id, group_id) REFERENCES inventory_groups (org_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);
CREATE INDEX maintenance_windows_org_idx ON maintenance_windows (org_id);

ALTER TABLE maintenance_windows ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE maintenance_windows FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY org_isolation ON maintenance_windows
  USING (org_id = NULLIF(current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true), '')::uuid);
CREATE POLICY org_isolation_bypass ON maintenance_windows
  USING (current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) IS NULL OR current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) = '');

-- The maintenance guard's outcome (spec §6). Dropping and re-adding the
-- named CHECK only relaxes it; no row changes.
ALTER TABLE schedule_runs DROP CONSTRAINT schedule_runs_outcome_check;
ALTER TABLE schedule_runs ADD CONSTRAINT schedule_runs_outcome_check
  CHECK (outcome IN ('triggered', 'skipped_overlap', 'skipped_maintenance', 'missed', 'failed'));

-- +goose Down
-- NOT VALID: rows recorded as skipped_maintenance stay as they are; the
-- narrower CHECK applies to new rows only, so the rollback never fails and
-- deletes nothing.
ALTER TABLE schedule_runs DROP CONSTRAINT schedule_runs_outcome_check;
ALTER TABLE schedule_runs ADD CONSTRAINT schedule_runs_outcome_check
  CHECK (outcome IN ('triggered', 'skipped_overlap', 'missed', 'failed')) NOT VALID;
DROP TABLE maintenance_windows;
```

`internal/db/queries/maintenance.sql`:

```sql
-- name: CreateMaintenanceWindow :one
INSERT INTO maintenance_windows (id, org_id, name, target_kind, host_id, group_id, starts_at, ends_at, cron,
  duration_seconds, timezone, note, created_by)
VALUES (@id, @org_id, @name, @target_kind, sqlc.narg(host_id), sqlc.narg(group_id), sqlc.narg(starts_at),
  sqlc.narg(ends_at), sqlc.narg(cron), sqlc.narg(duration_seconds), sqlc.narg(timezone), @note, sqlc.narg(created_by))
RETURNING id;

-- name: GetMaintenanceWindow :one
SELECT w.*, COALESCE(h.name, g.name, '')::text AS target_name
FROM maintenance_windows AS w
LEFT JOIN hosts AS h ON h.org_id = w.org_id AND h.id = w.host_id
LEFT JOIN inventory_groups AS g ON g.org_id = w.org_id AND g.id = w.group_id
WHERE w.org_id = @org_id AND w.id = @id;

-- name: ListMaintenanceWindows :many
SELECT w.*, COALESCE(h.name, g.name, '')::text AS target_name
FROM maintenance_windows AS w
LEFT JOIN hosts AS h ON h.org_id = w.org_id AND h.id = w.host_id
LEFT JOIN inventory_groups AS g ON g.org_id = w.org_id AND g.id = w.group_id
WHERE w.org_id = @org_id
ORDER BY w.name;

-- name: UpdateMaintenanceWindow :execrows
UPDATE maintenance_windows SET name = @name, target_kind = @target_kind, host_id = sqlc.narg(host_id),
  group_id = sqlc.narg(group_id), starts_at = sqlc.narg(starts_at), ends_at = sqlc.narg(ends_at),
  cron = sqlc.narg(cron), duration_seconds = sqlc.narg(duration_seconds), timezone = sqlc.narg(timezone),
  note = @note, revision = revision + 1, updated_at = now()
WHERE org_id = @org_id AND id = @id AND revision = @revision;

-- name: DeleteMaintenanceWindow :execrows
DELETE FROM maintenance_windows WHERE org_id = @org_id AND id = @id;
```

Run: `make sqlc && go build ./... && go test -race -timeout 900s -run 'TestEveryOrgOwnedTableForcesRowLevelSecurity|TestExecutionMigrationsRollBackAndReapply' ./internal/db/...`
Expected: PASS. `gen.GetMaintenanceWindowRow` and `gen.ListMaintenanceWindowsRow` have identical fields (the store converts one into the other), and `target_name` is a non-null `string`.

`internal/maintenance/occur_test.go`:

```go
package maintenance_test

import (
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/maintenance"
)

func at(s string) time.Time {
	t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, s)
	if err != nil {
		panic(err)
	}
	return t
}

func ptr(t time.Time) *time.Time { return &t }

// nightly is 02:00-04:00 Europe/Berlin every day.
func nightly() maintenance.Window {
	return maintenance.Window{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Name: "nightly", TargetKind: maintenance.TargetOrg,
		Cron: "0 2 * * *", Duration: 2 * time.Hour, Timezone: "Europe/Berlin"}
}

func TestActiveAtOneOff(t *testing.T) {
	w := maintenance.Window{StartsAt: ptr(at("2026-09-22T10:00:00Z")), EndsAt: ptr(at("2026-09-22T12:00:00Z"))}
	cases := map[string]bool{
		"2026-09-22T09:59:59Z": false,
		"2026-09-22T10:00:00Z": true, // start is inside
		"2026-09-22T11:59:59Z": true,
		"2026-09-22T12:00:00Z": false, // end is outside
	}
	for instant, want := range cases {
		if got, err := maintenance.ActiveAt(w, at(instant)); err != nil || got != want {
			t.Errorf("ActiveAt(%s) = %v, %v; want %v", instant, got, err, want)
		}
	}
	if _, err := maintenance.ActiveAt(maintenance.Window{}, at("2026-09-22T10:00:00Z")); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a one-off window without bounds evaluated")
	}
}

func TestActiveAtRecurringAcrossDST(t *testing.T) {
	w := nightly()
	cases := map[string]bool{
		// Summer time: 02:00 CEST is 00:00 UTC.
		"2026-09-22T00:00:00Z": true,
		"2026-09-22T01:59:59Z": true,
		"2026-09-22T02:00:00Z": false,
		"2026-09-21T23:59:59Z": false,
		// Winter time: 02:00 CET is 01:00 UTC.
		"2026-11-10T00:30:00Z": false,
		"2026-11-10T01:00:00Z": true,
		"2026-11-10T02:59:00Z": true,
	}
	for instant, want := range cases {
		if got, err := maintenance.ActiveAt(w, at(instant)); err != nil || got != want {
			t.Errorf("ActiveAt(%s) = %v, %v; want %v", instant, got, err, want)
		}
	}
	w.Cron = "not cron"
	if _, err := maintenance.ActiveAt(w, at("2026-09-22T00:00:00Z")); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a broken expression evaluated")
	}
	w.Cron, w.Timezone = "0 2 * * *", "Mars/Olympus"
	if _, err := maintenance.ActiveAt(w, at("2026-09-22T00:00:00Z")); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("an unknown zone evaluated")
	}
}

func TestOccurrences(t *testing.T) {
	w := nightly()
	// A range that starts inside an occurrence includes that occurrence.
	got, err := maintenance.Occurrences(w, at("2026-09-22T01:00:00Z"), at("2026-09-25T00:00:00Z"), 100)
	if err != nil || len(got) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("occurrences = %+v, %v", got, err)
	}
	if !got[0].Start.Equal(at("2026-09-22T00:00:00Z")) || !got[0].End.Equal(at("2026-09-22T02:00:00Z")) || got[0].Name != "nightly" {
		t.Fatalf("first = %+v", got[0])
	}
	if capped, _ := maintenance.Occurrences(w, at("2026-09-01T00:00:00Z"), at("2026-10-01T00:00:00Z"), 5); len(capped) != 5 {
		t.Fatalf("capped = %d", len(capped))
	}
	oneOff := maintenance.Window{Name: "db upgrade", StartsAt: ptr(at("2026-09-23T20:00:00Z")), EndsAt: ptr(at("2026-09-24T02:00:00Z"))}
	if in, _ := maintenance.Occurrences(oneOff, at("2026-09-24T00:00:00Z"), at("2026-09-25T00:00:00Z"), 5); len(in) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("overlapping one-off = %+v", in)
	}
	if out, _ := maintenance.Occurrences(oneOff, at("2026-09-24T02:00:00Z"), at("2026-09-25T00:00:00Z"), 5); len(out) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("one-off ending at the range start = %+v", out)
	}
	if _, err := maintenance.Occurrences(maintenance.Window{}, at("2026-09-24T00:00:00Z"), at("2026-09-25T00:00:00Z"), 5); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a one-off window without bounds listed")
	}
	w.Cron = "nope"
	if _, err := maintenance.Occurrences(w, at("2026-09-24T00:00:00Z"), at("2026-09-25T00:00:00Z"), 5); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a broken expression listed")
	}
}
```

`internal/maintenance/validate_test.go`:

```go
package maintenance

import (
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

func TestNormalize(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	host := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	start, end := now.Add(time.Hour), now.Add(3*time.Hour)
	oneOff := Input{Name: " db upgrade ", TargetKind: TargetHost, TargetID: &host, StartsAt: &start, EndsAt: &end}
	w, err := normalize(oneOff, now)
	if err != nil || w.Name != "db upgrade" || w.Recurring() || !w.StartsAt.Equal(start) {
		t.Fatalf("one-off = %+v, %v", w, err)
	}
	recurring := Input{Name: "nightly", TargetKind: TargetOrg, Cron: "@daily", Duration: "90m", Timezone: "Europe/Berlin"}
	w, err = normalize(recurring, now)
	if err != nil || w.Cron != "@daily" || w.Duration != 90*time.Minute || w.TargetID != nil {
		t.Fatalf("recurring = %+v, %v", w, err)
	}
	past := now.Add(-time.Hour)
	bad := map[string]Input{
		"no name":             {TargetKind: TargetOrg, Cron: "@daily", Duration: "1h", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"long name":           {Name: strings.Repeat("x", 101), TargetKind: TargetOrg, Cron: "@daily", Duration: "1h", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"control name":        {Name: "a\nb", TargetKind: TargetOrg, Cron: "@daily", Duration: "1h", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"long note":           {Name: "n", Note: strings.Repeat("x", 1001), TargetKind: TargetOrg, Cron: "@daily", Duration: "1h", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"host without id":     {Name: "n", TargetKind: TargetHost, Cron: "@daily", Duration: "1h", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"org with id":         {Name: "n", TargetKind: TargetOrg, TargetID: &host, Cron: "@daily", Duration: "1h", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"unknown kind":        {Name: "n", TargetKind: "zone", Cron: "@daily", Duration: "1h", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"neither mode":        {Name: "n", TargetKind: TargetOrg},
		"both modes":          {Name: "n", TargetKind: TargetOrg, StartsAt: &start, EndsAt: &end, Cron: "@daily", Duration: "1h", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"end before start":    {Name: "n", TargetKind: TargetOrg, StartsAt: &start, EndsAt: &past},
		"too long one-off":    {Name: "n", TargetKind: TargetOrg, StartsAt: &start, EndsAt: ptrTime(start.Add(367 * 24 * time.Hour))},
		"duration on one-off": {Name: "n", TargetKind: TargetOrg, StartsAt: &start, EndsAt: &end, Duration: "1h"},
		"bad cron":            {Name: "n", TargetKind: TargetOrg, Cron: "61 * * * *", Duration: "1h", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"short duration":      {Name: "n", TargetKind: TargetOrg, Cron: "@daily", Duration: "30s", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"long duration":       {Name: "n", TargetKind: TargetOrg, Cron: "@daily", Duration: "745h", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"odd duration":        {Name: "n", TargetKind: TargetOrg, Cron: "@daily", Duration: "90m30s", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"no timezone":         {Name: "n", TargetKind: TargetOrg, Cron: "@daily", Duration: "1h"},
		"local timezone":      {Name: "n", TargetKind: TargetOrg, Cron: "@daily", Duration: "1h", Timezone: "Local"},
		"unknown timezone":    {Name: "n", TargetKind: TargetOrg, Cron: "@daily", Duration: "1h", Timezone: "Mars/Olympus"},
		"never fires":         {Name: "n", TargetKind: TargetOrg, Cron: "0 0 31 2 *", Duration: "1h", Timezone: "UTC"},
	}
	for name, in := range bad {
		if _, err := normalize(in, now); !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidInput) {
			t.Errorf("%s: err = %v, want ErrInvalidInput", name, err)
		}
	}
}

func ptrTime(t time.Time) *time.Time { return &t }
```

`internal/maintenance/service_test.go`:

```go
package maintenance_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/maintenance"
)

var ctx = context.Background()

type fakeStore struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	byID map[uuid.UUID]maintenance.Window
	err  error
}

func newFakeStore() *fakeStore { return &fakeStore{byID: map[uuid.UUID]maintenance.Window{}} }

func (f *fakeStore) Create(_ context.Context, w maintenance.Window) (maintenance.Window, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	w.Revision = 1
	f.byID[w.ID] = w
	return w, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) Get(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (maintenance.Window, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	w, ok := f.byID[id]
	if !ok || w.OrgID != orgID {
		return maintenance.Window{}, maintenance.ErrNotFound
	}
	return w, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) List(_ context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]maintenance.Window, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	if f.err != nil {
		return nil, f.err
	}
	var out []maintenance.Window
	for _, w := range f.byID {
		if w.OrgID == orgID {
			out = append(out, w)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) Update(_ context.Context, w maintenance.Window, revision int64) (maintenance.Window, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	cur, ok := f.byID[w.ID]
	if !ok {
		return maintenance.Window{}, maintenance.ErrNotFound
	}
	if cur.Revision != revision {
		return maintenance.Window{}, maintenance.ErrRevisionMismatch
	}
	w.Revision = revision + 1
	f.byID[w.ID] = w
	return w, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) Delete(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	if w, ok := f.byID[id]; !ok || w.OrgID != orgID {
		return maintenance.ErrNotFound
	}
	delete(f.byID, id)
	return nil
}

type fakeGroups struct {
	members map[uuid.UUID][]uuid.UUID
	err     error
}

func (f fakeGroups) HostsInGroup(_ context.Context, _, groupID uuid.UUID) ([]uuid.UUID, error) {
	return f.members[groupID], f.err
}

var quiet = slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil))

func TestHostsInMaintenance(t *testing.T) {
	now := at("2026-09-22T10:30:00Z")
	store := newFakeStore()
	org := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	hA, hB, hC, group := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	svc := maintenance.NewService(store, fakeGroups{members: map[uuid.UUID][]uuid.UUID{group: {hB}}}, func() time.Time { return now }, nil, quiet)
	active := maintenance.Input{Name: "a", TargetKind: maintenance.TargetHost, TargetID: &hA, StartsAt: ptr(now.Add(-time.Hour)), EndsAt: ptr(now.Add(time.Hour))}
	if _, err := svc.Create(ctx, org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), active); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	later := maintenance.Input{Name: "b", TargetKind: maintenance.TargetHost, TargetID: &hC, StartsAt: ptr(now.Add(time.Hour)), EndsAt: ptr(now.Add(2 * time.Hour))}
	if _, err := svc.Create(ctx, org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), later); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	groupWin := maintenance.Input{Name: "g", TargetKind: maintenance.TargetGroup, TargetID: &group, Cron: "30 12 * * *", Duration: "1h", Timezone: "Europe/Berlin"}
	if _, err := svc.Create(ctx, org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), groupWin); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// A stored window that no longer evaluates is ignored, not fatal.
	broken := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	store.byID[broken] = maintenance.Window{ID: broken, OrgID: org, TargetKind: maintenance.TargetOrg, Cron: "broken", Duration: time.Hour, Timezone: "UTC"}

	got, err := svc.HostsInMaintenance(ctx, org, []uuid.UUID{hA, hB, hC}, now)
	if err != nil || len(got) != 2 || !got[hA] || !got[hB] || got[hC] {
		t.Fatalf("in maintenance = %v, %v; want hA (host window) and hB (group window)", got, err)
	}
	orgWin := maintenance.Input{Name: "all", TargetKind: maintenance.TargetOrg, StartsAt: ptr(now.Add(-time.Minute)), EndsAt: ptr(now.Add(time.Minute))}
	if _, err := svc.Create(ctx, org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), orgWin); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got, _ := svc.HostsInMaintenance(ctx, org, []uuid.UUID{hA, hB, hC}, now); len(got) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("an organization window covers every host, got %v", got)
	}
	failing := maintenance.NewService(store, fakeGroups{err: errors.New("inventory down")}, nil, nil, quiet)
	if _, err := failing.HostsInMaintenance(ctx, org, []uuid.UUID{hB}, now); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a group resolution failure must surface")
	}
	store.err = errors.New("db down")
	if _, err := svc.HostsInMaintenance(ctx, org, []uuid.UUID{hA}, now); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a store failure must surface")
	}
}

func TestListMarksActiveWindowsAndDurationText(t *testing.T) {
	now := at("2026-09-22T10:30:00Z")
	store := newFakeStore()
	org := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	svc := maintenance.NewService(store, fakeGroups{}, func() time.Time { return now }, nil, quiet)
	on, err := svc.Create(ctx, org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), maintenance.Input{Name: "on", TargetKind: maintenance.TargetOrg, Cron: "0 10 * * *", Duration: "90m", Timezone: "UTC"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	off, err := svc.Create(ctx, org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), maintenance.Input{Name: "off", TargetKind: maintenance.TargetOrg, Cron: "0 20 * * *", Duration: "2h", Timezone: "UTC"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, active, err := svc.List(ctx, org)
	if err != nil || !active[on.ID] || active[off.ID] {
		t.Fatalf("active = %v, %v", active, err)
	}
	if on.DurationText() != "1h30m" || off.DurationText() != "2h" || (maintenance.Window{}).DurationText() != "" {
		t.Fatalf("duration texts = %q, %q", on.DurationText(), off.DurationText())
	}
	store.err = errors.New("db down")
	if _, _, err := svc.List(ctx, org); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a store failure must surface")
	}
}

func TestOccurrencesBetweenIsSorted(t *testing.T) {
	store := newFakeStore()
	org := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	svc := maintenance.NewService(store, fakeGroups{}, func() time.Time { return at("2026-09-22T00:00:00Z") }, nil, quiet)
	for _, in := range []maintenance.Input{
		{Name: "late", TargetKind: maintenance.TargetOrg, StartsAt: ptr(at("2026-09-24T10:00:00Z")), EndsAt: ptr(at("2026-09-24T11:00:00Z"))},
		{Name: "daily", TargetKind: maintenance.TargetOrg, Cron: "0 6 * * *", Duration: "30m", Timezone: "UTC"},
	} {
		if _, err := svc.Create(ctx, org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), in); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	broken := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	store.byID[broken] = maintenance.Window{ID: broken, OrgID: org, TargetKind: maintenance.TargetOrg}
	got, err := svc.OccurrencesBetween(ctx, org, at("2026-09-23T00:00:00Z"), at("2026-09-25T00:00:00Z"))
	if err != nil || len(got) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("occurrences = %+v, %v", got, err)
	}
	// 23 Sep 06:00 daily, 24 Sep 06:00 daily, 24 Sep 10:00 late.
	if got[0].Name != "daily" || got[1].Name != "daily" || got[2].Name != "late" || !got[1].Start.Before(got[2].Start) {
		t.Fatalf("order = %s, %s, %s", got[0].Name, got[1].Name, got[2].Name)
	}
}

func TestCreateUpdateDelete(t *testing.T) {
	store := newFakeStore()
	org, actor := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	svc := maintenance.NewService(store, fakeGroups{}, nil, nil, quiet)
	in := maintenance.Input{Name: "nightly", TargetKind: maintenance.TargetOrg, Cron: "@daily", Duration: "1h", Timezone: "UTC"}
	w, err := svc.Create(ctx, org, actor, in)
	if err != nil || *w.CreatedBy != actor || w.OrgID != org {
		t.Fatalf("create = %+v, %v", w, err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.Create(ctx, org, actor, maintenance.Input{}); !errors.Is(err, maintenance.ErrInvalidInput) {
		t.Fatalf("invalid create = %v", err)
	}
	in.Name = "nightly patching"
	up, err := svc.Update(ctx, org, w.ID, w.Revision, in)
	if err != nil || up.Name != "nightly patching" || up.Revision != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("update = %+v, %v", up, err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.Update(ctx, org, w.ID, 1, in); !errors.Is(err, maintenance.ErrRevisionMismatch) {
		t.Fatalf("stale update = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.Update(ctx, org, w.ID, 2, maintenance.Input{}); !errors.Is(err, maintenance.ErrInvalidInput) {
		t.Fatalf("invalid update = %v", err)
	}
	if list, active, err := svc.List(ctx, org); err != nil || len(list) != 1 || len(active) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("list = %v, %v, %v", list, active, err)
	}
	if got, err := svc.Get(ctx, org, w.ID); err != nil || got.ID != w.ID {
		t.Fatalf("get = %v", err)
	}
	if err := svc.Delete(ctx, org, w.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.Get(ctx, org, w.ID); !errors.Is(err, maintenance.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("after delete = %v", err)
	}
}
```

`internal/maintenance/window.go`:

```go
// Package maintenance holds maintenance windows (spec 2026-09-18 §6): a
// host, an inventory group or the whole organization is in maintenance
// either once (starts_at to ends_at) or on a recurring timetable (a cron
// expression in a time zone, each occurrence lasting a duration). v0.2d
// uses them for the schedules' maintenance guard and the calendar; v0.4
// adds monitoring-group targets and alert silencing.
package maintenance

import (
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// Sentinels the API maps to HTTP.
var (
	ErrNotFound         = errors.New("not found")
	ErrInvalidInput     = errors.New("invalid input")
	ErrNameTaken        = errors.New("name already in use")
	ErrRevisionMismatch = errors.New("revision mismatch")
)

// TargetKind is what a window covers.
type TargetKind string

const (
	TargetHost  TargetKind = "host"
	TargetGroup TargetKind = "inventory_group"
	TargetOrg   TargetKind = "org"
)

// Window is one stored maintenance window. A one-off window has StartsAt
// and EndsAt; a recurring one has Cron, Duration and Timezone.
type Window struct {
	ID, OrgID  uuid.UUID
	Name       string
	TargetKind TargetKind
	// TargetID is the host or inventory group; nil for the organization.
	TargetID *uuid.UUID
	// TargetName is the host's or group's name, read with the window.
	TargetName           string
	StartsAt, EndsAt     *time.Time
	Cron                 string
	Duration             time.Duration
	Timezone             string
	Note                 string
	CreatedBy            *uuid.UUID
	CreatedAt, UpdatedAt time.Time
	Revision             int64
}

// Recurring reports whether the window repeats on a timetable.
func (w Window) Recurring() bool { return w.Cron != "" }

// DurationText is Duration without trailing zero units ("1h30m", "2h").
func (w Window) DurationText() string {
	if w.Duration == 0 {
		return ""
	}
	s := w.Duration.String()
	s = strings.TrimSuffix(s, "0s")
	if strings.HasSuffix(s, "h0m") {
		s = strings.TrimSuffix(s, "0m")
	}
	return s
}

// Input is what an operator submits for create and update. Duration is a
// Go-style duration such as "2h" or "90m".
type Input struct {
	Name             string
	TargetKind       TargetKind
	TargetID         *uuid.UUID
	StartsAt, EndsAt *time.Time
	Cron             string
	Duration         string
	Timezone         string
	Note             string
}

// Occurrence is one concrete stretch of a window, for the calendar.
type Occurrence struct {
	WindowID   uuid.UUID
	Name       string
	TargetKind TargetKind
	TargetID   *uuid.UUID
	TargetName string
	Start, End time.Time
}
```

`internal/maintenance/occur.go`:

```go
package maintenance

import (
	"fmt"
	"slices"
	"time"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/cronexpr"
)

// timetable parses a recurring window's expression and zone.
func (w Window) timetable() (cronexpr.Expr, *time.Location, error) {
	expr, err := cronexpr.Parse(w.Cron)
	if err != nil {
		return cronexpr.Expr{}, nil, err
	}
	loc, err := time.LoadLocation(w.Timezone)
	if err != nil {
		return cronexpr.Expr{}, nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown timezone %q: %w", w.Timezone, err)
	}
	return expr, loc, nil
}

// ActiveAt reports whether w covers the instant at. A recurring window is
// active when an occurrence started in (at - Duration, at]: the first
// occurrence strictly after at - Duration, if it is not after at.
func ActiveAt(w Window, at time.Time) (bool, error) {
	if !w.Recurring() {
		if w.StartsAt == nil || w.EndsAt == nil {
			return false, fmt.Errorf("one-off window %s has no start or end", w.ID)
		}
		return !at.Before(*w.StartsAt) && at.Before(*w.EndsAt), nil
	}
	expr, loc, err := w.timetable()
	if err != nil {
		return false, err
	}
	s := expr.Next(at.Add(-w.Duration), loc)
	return !s.IsZero() && !s.After(at), nil
}

// Occurrences lists the stretches of w that overlap [from, to), at most
// max of them, in order.
func Occurrences(w Window, from, to time.Time, max int) ([]Occurrence, error) {
	occ := func(start, end time.Time) Occurrence {
		return Occurrence{WindowID: w.ID, Name: w.Name, TargetKind: w.TargetKind, TargetID: w.TargetID, TargetName: w.TargetName, Start: start, End: end}
	}
	if !w.Recurring() {
		if w.StartsAt == nil || w.EndsAt == nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("one-off window %s has no start or end", w.ID)
		}
		if w.StartsAt.Before(to) && w.EndsAt.After(from) {
			return []Occurrence{occ(*w.StartsAt, *w.EndsAt)}, nil
		}
		return nil, nil
	}
	expr, loc, err := w.timetable()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var out []Occurrence
	for s := expr.Next(from.Add(-w.Duration), loc); !s.IsZero() && s.Before(to) && len(out) < max; s = expr.Next(s, loc) {
		out = append(out, occ(s, s.Add(w.Duration)))
	}
	return out, nil
}

// sortOccurrences orders occurrences by start, then window id.
func sortOccurrences(occ []Occurrence) {
	slices.SortFunc(occ, func(a, b Occurrence) int {
		if c := a.Start.Compare(b.Start); c != 0 {
			return c
		}
		return slices.Compare(a.WindowID[:], b.WindowID[:])
	})
}
```

`internal/maintenance/validate.go`:

```go
package maintenance

import (
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"time"
	"unicode"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/cronexpr"
)

const (
	maxNameRunes = 100
	maxNoteRunes = 1000
	minDuration  = time.Minute
	maxDuration  = 31 * 24 * time.Hour
	maxOneOff    = 366 * 24 * time.Hour
)

// normalize validates an input into the window's fields; id, org and the
// bookkeeping fields are the caller's.
func normalize(in Input, now time.Time) (Window, error) {
	var w Window
	name := strings.TrimSpace(in.Name)
	if name == "" || utf8.RuneCountInString(name) > maxNameRunes {
		return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: name must be 1-%d characters", ErrInvalidInput, maxNameRunes)
	}
	for _, r := range name {
		if unicode.IsControl(r) {
			return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: name must not contain control characters", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
	}
	w.Name = name
	if utf8.RuneCountInString(in.Note) > maxNoteRunes {
		return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: note must be at most %d characters", ErrInvalidInput, maxNoteRunes)
	}
	w.Note = in.Note
	switch in.TargetKind {
	case TargetHost, TargetGroup:
		if in.TargetID == nil {
			return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: a %s window needs target_id", ErrInvalidInput, in.TargetKind)
		}
	case TargetOrg:
		if in.TargetID != nil {
			return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: an organization window takes no target_id", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
	default:
		return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: target_kind must be host, inventory_group or org", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	w.TargetKind, w.TargetID = in.TargetKind, in.TargetID

	cron := strings.TrimSpace(in.Cron)
	if cron == "" {
		if in.Duration != "" || in.Timezone != "" {
			return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: duration and timezone belong to a recurring window (cron)", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
		if in.StartsAt == nil || in.EndsAt == nil {
			return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: a one-off window needs starts_at and ends_at; a recurring one needs cron", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
		if !in.EndsAt.After(*in.StartsAt) || in.EndsAt.Sub(*in.StartsAt) > maxOneOff {
			return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: ends_at must be after starts_at, at most 366 days later", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
		start, end := in.StartsAt.UTC(), in.EndsAt.UTC()
		w.StartsAt, w.EndsAt = &start, &end
		return w, nil
	}
	if in.StartsAt != nil || in.EndsAt != nil {
		return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: a recurring window has no starts_at or ends_at", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	expr, err := cronexpr.Parse(cron)
	if err != nil {
		return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrInvalidInput, err)
	}
	d, err := time.ParseDuration(in.Duration)
	if err != nil || d < minDuration || d > maxDuration {
		return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: duration must be between 1m and 744h (31 days)", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	if d%time.Minute != 0 {
		return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: duration must be whole minutes", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	if in.Timezone == "" || in.Timezone == "Local" {
		return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: timezone must be an IANA name such as Europe/Berlin or UTC", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	loc, err := time.LoadLocation(in.Timezone)
	if err != nil {
		return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: unknown timezone %q", ErrInvalidInput, in.Timezone)
	}
	if expr.Next(now, loc).IsZero() {
		return w, fmt.Errorf("%w: the expression never fires", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	w.Cron, w.Duration, w.Timezone = expr.String(), d, in.Timezone
	return w, nil
}
```

`internal/maintenance/service.go`:

```go
package maintenance

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// maxOccurrencesPerWindow bounds one window's occurrences in a range (an
// every-minute recurring window over six weeks would be 60 000).
const maxOccurrencesPerWindow = 500

// Store persists windows (org-scoped).
type Store interface {
	Create(ctx context.Context, w Window) (Window, error)
	Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Window, error)
	List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Window, error)
	Update(ctx context.Context, w Window, revision int64) (Window, error)
	Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error
}

// GroupHosts resolves an inventory group (and its subgroups) into the
// managed hosts in it; the app wires the job scope resolver behind it.
type GroupHosts interface {
	HostsInGroup(ctx context.Context, orgID, groupID uuid.UUID) ([]uuid.UUID, error)
}

// Service manages windows and answers which hosts are in maintenance.
type Service struct {
	store  Store
	groups GroupHosts
	now    func() time.Time
	rand   io.Reader
	log    *slog.Logger
}

// NewService wires the service; nil now, rand and log take defaults.
func NewService(store Store, groups GroupHosts, now func() time.Time, entropy io.Reader, log *slog.Logger) *Service {
	if now == nil {
		now = time.Now
	}
	if entropy == nil {
		entropy = rand.Reader
	}
	if log == nil {
		log = slog.Default()
	}
	return &Service{store: store, groups: groups, now: now, rand: entropy, log: log}
}

func (s *Service) newID() uuid.UUID {
	id, err := uuid.NewV7FromReader(s.rand)
	if err != nil {
		panic(err) //nolint:forbidigo // entropy failure is unrecoverable, like uuid.Must
	}
	return id
}

// Create validates and stores a window.
func (s *Service) Create(ctx context.Context, orgID, actor uuid.UUID, in Input) (Window, error) {
	w, err := normalize(in, s.now())
	if err != nil {
		return Window{}, err
	}
	w.ID, w.OrgID, w.CreatedBy = s.newID(), orgID, &actor
	return s.store.Create(ctx, w)
}

// Update replaces a window when revision is still current.
func (s *Service) Update(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, revision int64, in Input) (Window, error) {
	w, err := normalize(in, s.now())
	if err != nil {
		return Window{}, err
	}
	w.ID, w.OrgID = id, orgID
	return s.store.Update(ctx, w, revision)
}

func (s *Service) Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Window, error) {
	return s.store.Get(ctx, orgID, id)
}

// List returns the organization's windows and which of them are active
// right now.
func (s *Service) List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Window, map[uuid.UUID]bool, error) {
	windows, err := s.store.List(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	now := s.now()
	active := map[uuid.UUID]bool{}
	for _, w := range windows {
		if ok, err := ActiveAt(w, now); err == nil && ok {
			active[w.ID] = true
		}
	}
	return windows, active, nil
}

func (s *Service) Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	return s.store.Delete(ctx, orgID, id)
}

// HostsInMaintenance returns the subset of hostIDs that an active window
// covers at the instant at: an organization window covers every host, a
// group window the managed hosts of the group and its subgroups. A stored
// window that no longer evaluates is logged and ignored.
func (s *Service) HostsInMaintenance(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, hostIDs []uuid.UUID, at time.Time) (map[uuid.UUID]bool, error) {
	windows, err := s.store.List(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	covered := map[uuid.UUID]bool{}
	all := false
	for _, w := range windows {
		active, err := ActiveAt(w, at)
		if err != nil {
			s.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "maintenance window does not evaluate; ignoring it", "window", w.ID, "org", orgID, "err", err)
			continue
		}
		if !active {
			continue
		}
		switch w.TargetKind {
		case TargetOrg:
			all = true
		case TargetHost:
			covered[*w.TargetID] = true
		case TargetGroup:
			members, err := s.groups.HostsInGroup(ctx, orgID, *w.TargetID)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, fmt.Errorf("resolve maintenance group %s: %w", w.TargetID, err)
			}
			for _, h := range members {
				covered[h] = true
			}
		}
	}
	out := map[uuid.UUID]bool{}
	for _, h := range hostIDs {
		if all || covered[h] {
			out[h] = true
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

// OccurrencesBetween lists every window's stretches overlapping
// [from, to), ordered by start.
func (s *Service) OccurrencesBetween(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, from, to time.Time) ([]Occurrence, error) {
	windows, err := s.store.List(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var out []Occurrence
	for _, w := range windows {
		occ, err := Occurrences(w, from, to, maxOccurrencesPerWindow)
		if err != nil {
			s.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "maintenance window does not evaluate; leaving it out of the calendar", "window", w.ID, "org", orgID, "err", err)
			continue
		}
		out = append(out, occ...)
	}
	sortOccurrences(out)
	return out, nil
}
```

Run: `go test -race -timeout 900s -run 'ActiveAt|Occurrences|Normalize|HostsInMaintenance|ListMarks|CreateUpdateDelete' ./internal/maintenance/...`
Expected: PASS. 02:00–04:00 Europe/Berlin is 00:00–02:00 UTC in summer and 01:00–03:00 UTC in winter. A range starting inside an occurrence includes it. Group windows resolve through `GroupHosts`, an organization window covers every host, and a window that no longer evaluates is ignored.

`internal/maintenance/store_sql_test.go`:

```go
package maintenance_test

import (
	"errors"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/testdb"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/maintenance"
)

// seedHost creates an organization with one zone and one host.
func seedHost(t *testing.T, d *db.DB, slug string) (org, host uuid.UUID) {
	t.Helper()
	q := gen.New(d.Pool())
	o, err := q.CreateOrganization(ctx, gen.CreateOrganizationParams{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Slug: slug, Name: slug})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	zone, host := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	if _, err := d.Pool().Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO zones (id, org_id, slug, name) VALUES ($1, $2, 'z', 'z')`, zone, o.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := d.Pool().Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO hosts (id, org_id, zone_id, name, managed) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 'web-1', true)`, host, o.ID, zone); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return o.ID, host
}

func TestWindowStoreRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	org, host := seedHost(t, d, "maint-store")
	otherOrg, _ := seedHost(t, d, "maint-other")
	st := maintenance.NewSQLStore(d)
	scoped := db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org)
	start, end := at("2026-09-22T10:00:00Z"), at("2026-09-22T12:00:00Z")
	oneOff, err := st.Create(scoped, maintenance.Window{
		ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: org, Name: "db upgrade", TargetKind: maintenance.TargetHost, TargetID: &host,
		StartsAt: &start, EndsAt: &end, Note: "kernel 6.12",
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if oneOff.TargetName != "web-1" || *oneOff.TargetID != host || !oneOff.EndsAt.Equal(end) || oneOff.Revision != 1 || oneOff.Recurring() {
		t.Fatalf("one-off = %+v", oneOff)
	}
	recurring, err := st.Create(scoped, maintenance.Window{
		ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: org, Name: "nightly", TargetKind: maintenance.TargetOrg,
		Cron: "0 2 * * *", Duration: 90 * time.Minute, Timezone: "Europe/Berlin",
	})
	if err != nil || recurring.Duration != 90*time.Minute || recurring.Timezone != "Europe/Berlin" || recurring.TargetID != nil || recurring.StartsAt != nil {
		t.Fatalf("recurring = %+v, %v", recurring, err)
	}
	if _, err := st.Create(scoped, maintenance.Window{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: org, Name: "nightly", TargetKind: maintenance.TargetOrg,
		Cron: "@daily", Duration: time.Hour, Timezone: "UTC"}); !errors.Is(err, maintenance.ErrNameTaken) {
		t.Fatalf("duplicate name = %v", err)
	}
	ghost := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	if _, err := st.Create(scoped, maintenance.Window{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: org, Name: "ghost", TargetKind: maintenance.TargetGroup, TargetID: &ghost,
		StartsAt: &start, EndsAt: &end}); !errors.Is(err, maintenance.ErrInvalidInput) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown group = %v", err)
	}
	// Another organization's scope sees nothing.
	if list, err := st.List(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, otherOrg), org); err != nil || len(list) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("cross-org list = %v, %v", list, err)
	}
	recurring.Name = "nightly patching"
	up, err := st.Update(scoped, recurring, 1)
	if err != nil || up.Name != "nightly patching" || up.Revision != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("update = %+v, %v", up, err)
	}
	if _, err := st.Update(scoped, recurring, 1); !errors.Is(err, maintenance.ErrRevisionMismatch) {
		t.Fatalf("stale update = %v", err)
	}
	recurring.ID = uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	if _, err := st.Update(scoped, recurring, 1); !errors.Is(err, maintenance.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown update = %v", err)
	}
	// Deleting the host deletes its windows.
	if _, err := d.Pool().Exec(ctx, `DELETE FROM hosts WHERE id = $1`, host); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := st.Get(scoped, org, oneOff.ID); !errors.Is(err, maintenance.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("window of a deleted host = %v", err)
	}
	if err := st.Delete(scoped, org, up.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := st.Delete(scoped, org, up.ID); !errors.Is(err, maintenance.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("second delete = %v", err)
	}
}
```

`internal/maintenance/store_sql.go`:

```go
package maintenance

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgtype"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
)

// SQLStore is the org-scoped window store.
type SQLStore struct{ q *gen.Queries }

// NewSQLStore builds the store on *db.DB so every statement runs in the
// request's org scope.
func NewSQLStore(d *db.DB) *SQLStore { return &SQLStore{q: gen.New(d)} }

// columns maps a window onto its nullable columns.
type columns struct {
	hostID, groupID  pgtype.UUID
	startsAt, endsAt pgtype.Timestamptz
	cron, timezone   *string
	duration         *int32
}

func toColumns(w Window) columns {
	var c columns
	switch w.TargetKind {
	case TargetHost:
		c.hostID = db.NullUUID(w.TargetID)
	case TargetGroup:
		c.groupID = db.NullUUID(w.TargetID)
	}
	if w.Recurring() {
		cron, tz := w.Cron, w.Timezone
		secs := int32(w.Duration / time.Second) //nolint:gosec // ≤ 31 days, validated
		c.cron, c.timezone, c.duration = &cron, &tz, &secs
	} else {
		c.startsAt, c.endsAt = db.NullTime(w.StartsAt), db.NullTime(w.EndsAt)
	}
	return c
}

func (st *SQLStore) Create(ctx context.Context, w Window) (Window, error) {
	c := toColumns(w)
	id, err := st.q.CreateMaintenanceWindow(ctx, gen.CreateMaintenanceWindowParams{
		ID: w.ID, OrgID: w.OrgID, Name: w.Name, TargetKind: string(w.TargetKind), HostID: c.hostID, GroupID: c.groupID,
		StartsAt: c.startsAt, EndsAt: c.endsAt, Cron: c.cron, DurationSeconds: c.duration, Timezone: c.timezone,
		Note: w.Note, CreatedBy: db.NullUUID(w.CreatedBy),
	})
	if err := mapWriteErr(err); err != nil {
		return Window{}, fmt.Errorf("create maintenance window: %w", err)
	}
	return st.Get(ctx, w.OrgID, id)
}

func (st *SQLStore) Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Window, error) {
	row, err := st.q.GetMaintenanceWindow(ctx, gen.GetMaintenanceWindowParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return Window{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Window{}, fmt.Errorf("get maintenance window: %w", err)
	}
	return toWindow(gen.ListMaintenanceWindowsRow(row)), nil
}

func (st *SQLStore) List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Window, error) {
	rows, err := st.q.ListMaintenanceWindows(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list maintenance windows: %w", err)
	}
	out := make([]Window, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		out[i] = toWindow(r)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (st *SQLStore) Update(ctx context.Context, w Window, revision int64) (Window, error) {
	c := toColumns(w)
	n, err := st.q.UpdateMaintenanceWindow(ctx, gen.UpdateMaintenanceWindowParams{
		Name: w.Name, TargetKind: string(w.TargetKind), HostID: c.hostID, GroupID: c.groupID,
		StartsAt: c.startsAt, EndsAt: c.endsAt, Cron: c.cron, DurationSeconds: c.duration, Timezone: c.timezone,
		Note: w.Note, OrgID: w.OrgID, ID: w.ID, Revision: revision,
	})
	if err := mapWriteErr(err); err != nil {
		return Window{}, fmt.Errorf("update maintenance window: %w", err)
	}
	if n == 0 {
		if _, gerr := st.Get(ctx, w.OrgID, w.ID); gerr != nil {
			return Window{}, gerr
		}
		return Window{}, ErrRevisionMismatch
	}
	return st.Get(ctx, w.OrgID, w.ID)
}

func (st *SQLStore) Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	n, err := st.q.DeleteMaintenanceWindow(ctx, gen.DeleteMaintenanceWindowParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("delete maintenance window: %w", err)
	}
	if n == 0 {
		return ErrNotFound
	}
	return nil
}

// mapWriteErr turns constraint violations into sentinels: a taken name,
// and a target that is not a host or group of the organization.
func mapWriteErr(err error) error {
	switch {
	case err == nil:
		return nil
	case db.IsUniqueViolation(err):
		return ErrNameTaken
	case db.IsForeignKeyViolation(err):
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: the target does not exist", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	return err
}

func toWindow(r gen.ListMaintenanceWindowsRow) Window {
	w := Window{
		ID: r.ID, OrgID: r.OrgID, Name: r.Name, TargetKind: TargetKind(r.TargetKind), TargetName: r.TargetName,
		StartsAt: db.TimePtr(r.StartsAt), EndsAt: db.TimePtr(r.EndsAt), Note: r.Note,
		CreatedBy: db.UUIDPtr(r.CreatedBy), CreatedAt: r.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: r.UpdatedAt, Revision: r.Revision,
	}
	switch w.TargetKind {
	case TargetHost:
		w.TargetID = db.UUIDPtr(r.HostID)
	case TargetGroup:
		w.TargetID = db.UUIDPtr(r.GroupID)
	}
	if r.Cron != nil {
		w.Cron = *r.Cron
	}
	if r.Timezone != nil {
		w.Timezone = *r.Timezone
	}
	if r.DurationSeconds != nil {
		w.Duration = time.Duration(*r.DurationSeconds) * time.Second
	}
	return w
}
```

### Task 7: Maintenance API, page and navigation

This task exposes maintenance windows over the API and adds the SPA page at the sidebar entry **Maintenance** (after Schedules). The manager wires the service, with group windows resolved by the same scope resolver runs use.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/api/maintenance_handlers.go`; Test: `internal/api/maintenance_handlers_test.go`
- Create: `internal/app/maintenance_ports.go`; Test: `internal/app/maintenance_ports_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/api/api.go` (`Deps.Maintenance`, `validate`, routes), `internal/api/api_test.go`, `internal/api/apitest/apitest.go`, `internal/app/app.go`
- Create: `web/src/api/hooks/useMaintenance.ts`, `web/src/features/maintenance/{MaintenancePage,WindowDialog}.tsx`, `web/src/features/maintenance/time.ts`
- Test: `web/src/features/maintenance/MaintenancePage.test.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/lib/roles.ts`, `web/src/api/types.ts`, `web/src/ui/shell.tsx`, `web/src/router.tsx`
- Regenerate: `web/src/api/schema.d.ts`
- Docs (noix-docs): `user/maintenance-windows.md` (new), `reference/api.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6's `maintenance.Service` and `maintenance.GroupHosts`, `jobs.ScopeResolver` (existing: `Resolve(ctx, orgID, render.Scope) ([]ZoneTargets, error)`).
- Produces (package `app`): `func NewGroupHosts(r jobs.ScopeResolver) maintenance.GroupHosts` (also used by `apitest`).
- Produces (HTTP, under `/api/v1/orgs/{org}`): `GET /maintenance-windows` (maintenance.read, `{windows}` with `active_now`), `POST /maintenance-windows` (maintenance.write, 201 `{window}` + `ETag`), `GET/PUT/DELETE /maintenance-windows/{id}` (`PUT` needs `If-Match`). Audited as `createMaintenanceWindow`, `updateMaintenanceWindow`, `deleteMaintenanceWindow`, object type `maintenance`. Schemas `MaintenanceWindowDTO`, `MaintenanceWindowBody`.
- Produces (Go): `api.Deps.Maintenance *maintenance.Service`.
- Produces (SPA): `useMaintenanceWindows`, `useCreateMaintenanceWindow`, `useUpdateMaintenanceWindow(org, id)`, `useDeleteMaintenanceWindow`; types `MaintenanceWindow`, `MaintenanceWindowInput`; permission `maintenance.write`; route `/o/$org/maintenance`.

```go
package api_test

import (
	"net/http"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

type maintenanceWindowOut struct {
	ID          string `json:"id"`
	Name        string `json:"name"`
	TargetKind  string `json:"target_kind"`
	TargetID    string `json:"target_id"`
	TargetName  string `json:"target_name"`
	Cron        string `json:"cron"`
	Description string `json:"description"`
	Duration    string `json:"duration"`
	ActiveNow   bool   `json:"active_now"`
	Revision    int64  `json:"revision"`
}

func (f runFixture) maintenanceURL() string {
	return f.s.HTTP.URL + "/api/v1/orgs/" + f.orgSlug + "/maintenance-windows"
}

func TestMaintenanceWindowLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	f := newRunFixture(t, "maint-life")
	now := time.Now().UTC()
	oneOff := map[string]any{
		"name": "db upgrade", "target_kind": "host", "target_id": f.hostA.String(),
		"starts_at": now.Add(-time.Hour).Format(time.RFC3339), "ends_at": now.Add(time.Hour).Format(time.RFC3339),
	}
	// Viewers see windows; operators plan them.
	if resp := postJSON(t, f.viewerC, f.maintenanceURL(), oneOff); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("viewer create = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	resp := postJSON(t, f.operatorC, f.maintenanceURL(), oneOff)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusCreated || resp.Header.Get("ETag") != `"1"` {
		t.Fatalf("create = %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	var created struct {
		Window maintenanceWindowOut `json:"window"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &created)
	if created.Window.TargetName != "web-1" || created.Window.TargetID != f.hostA.String() {
		t.Fatalf("created = %+v", created.Window)
	}
	recurring := map[string]any{"name": "nightly", "target_kind": "org", "cron": "0 3 * * *", "duration": "90m", "timezone": "Europe/Berlin"}
	resp = postJSON(t, f.operatorC, f.maintenanceURL(), recurring)
	var rec struct {
		Window maintenanceWindowOut `json:"window"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &rec)
	if rec.Window.Duration != "1h30m" || rec.Window.Description == "" || rec.Window.TargetKind != "org" {
		t.Fatalf("recurring = %+v", rec.Window)
	}

	var list struct {
		Windows []maintenanceWindowOut `json:"windows"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, f.viewerC, http.MethodGet, f.maintenanceURL(), nil), &list)
	if len(list.Windows) != 2 || list.Windows[0].Name != "db upgrade" || !list.Windows[0].ActiveNow {
		t.Fatalf("list = %+v", list.Windows)
	}

	recurring["duration"] = "2h"
	if resp := requestWithHeaders(t, f.operatorC, http.MethodPut, f.maintenanceURL()+"/"+rec.Window.ID, recurring, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"9"`}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusPreconditionFailed {
		t.Fatalf("stale update = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	resp = requestWithHeaders(t, f.operatorC, http.MethodPut, f.maintenanceURL()+"/"+rec.Window.ID, recurring, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"1"`})
	decodeInto(t, resp, &rec)
	if rec.Window.Duration != "2h" || rec.Window.Revision != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("updated = %+v", rec.Window)
	}

	for name, body := range map[string]map[string]any{
		"both modes":     {"name": "x", "target_kind": "org", "cron": "@daily", "duration": "1h", "timezone": "UTC", "starts_at": now.Format(time.RFC3339), "ends_at": now.Add(time.Hour).Format(time.RFC3339)},
		"unknown target": {"name": "y", "target_kind": "host", "target_id": "0197c0a2-0000-7000-8000-000000000001", "starts_at": now.Format(time.RFC3339), "ends_at": now.Add(time.Hour).Format(time.RFC3339)},
		"bad target id":  {"name": "z", "target_kind": "host", "target_id": "nope", "starts_at": now.Format(time.RFC3339), "ends_at": now.Add(time.Hour).Format(time.RFC3339)},
	} {
		if resp := postJSON(t, f.operatorC, f.maintenanceURL(), body); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusBadRequest {
			t.Errorf("%s = %d, want 400", name, resp.StatusCode)
		}
	}
	if resp := postJSON(t, f.operatorC, f.maintenanceURL(), oneOff); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusConflict {
		t.Fatalf("duplicate name = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.operatorC, http.MethodDelete, f.maintenanceURL()+"/"+created.Window.ID, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("delete = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.viewerC, http.MethodGet, f.maintenanceURL()+"/"+created.Window.ID, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("get after delete = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}
```

```go
package app

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/render"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
)

type fakeScopeResolver struct {
	targets []jobs.ZoneTargets
	err     error
	scope   render.Scope
}

func (f *fakeScopeResolver) Resolve(_ context.Context, _ uuid.UUID, scope render.Scope) ([]jobs.ZoneTargets, error) {
	f.scope = scope
	return f.targets, f.err
}

func TestGroupHosts(t *testing.T) {
	a, b, group := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	r := &fakeScopeResolver{targets: []jobs.ZoneTargets{{HostIDs: []uuid.UUID{a}}, {HostIDs: []uuid.UUID{b}}}}
	got, err := NewGroupHosts(r).HostsInGroup(context.Background(), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), group)
	if err != nil || len(got) != 2 || r.scope.GroupID == nil || *r.scope.GroupID != group {
		t.Fatalf("HostsInGroup = %v, %v (scope %+v)", got, err, r.scope)
	}
	r.err = jobs.ErrNotFound
	if got, err := NewGroupHosts(r).HostsInGroup(context.Background(), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), group); err != nil || got != nil {
		t.Fatalf("deleted group = %v, %v; want nothing and no error", got, err)
	}
	r.err = errors.New("db down")
	if _, err := NewGroupHosts(r).HostsInGroup(context.Background(), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), group); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a resolver failure must surface")
	}
}
```
