`internal/api/maintenance_handlers.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"
	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/cronexpr"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/maintenance"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/tenancy"
)

type maintenanceWindowDTO struct {
	ID          string     `json:"id"`
	Name        string     `json:"name"`
	TargetKind  string     `json:"target_kind" enum:"host,inventory_group,org"`
	TargetID    string     `json:"target_id,omitempty"`
	TargetName  string     `json:"target_name,omitempty"`
	StartsAt    *time.Time `json:"starts_at,omitempty"`
	EndsAt      *time.Time `json:"ends_at,omitempty"`
	Cron        string     `json:"cron,omitempty"`
	Description string     `json:"description,omitempty" doc:"Human text for simple cron shapes"`
	Duration    string     `json:"duration,omitempty" doc:"Length of each occurrence, e.g. 2h or 90m"`
	Timezone    string     `json:"timezone,omitempty"`
	Note        string     `json:"note,omitempty"`
	ActiveNow   bool       `json:"active_now"`
	Revision    int64      `json:"revision"`
	CreatedAt   time.Time  `json:"created_at"`
	UpdatedAt   time.Time  `json:"updated_at"`
}

// MaintenanceWindowBody is the create/update body: starts_at and ends_at
// for a one-off window, or cron, duration and timezone for a recurring one.
type MaintenanceWindowBody struct {
	Name       string     `json:"name" minLength:"1" maxLength:"100"`
	TargetKind string     `json:"target_kind" enum:"host,inventory_group,org"`
	TargetID   string     `json:"target_id,omitempty" doc:"The host or inventory group; omit for the organization"`
	StartsAt   *time.Time `json:"starts_at,omitempty"`
	EndsAt     *time.Time `json:"ends_at,omitempty"`
	Cron       string     `json:"cron,omitempty" maxLength:"200"`
	Duration   string     `json:"duration,omitempty" maxLength:"20" doc:"1m to 744h, whole minutes"`
	Timezone   string     `json:"timezone,omitempty" maxLength:"64"`
	Note       string     `json:"note,omitempty" maxLength:"1000"`
}

type maintenanceListOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Windows []maintenanceWindowDTO `json:"windows"`
	}
}

type maintenanceIDInput struct {
	Org string `path:"org"`
	ID  string `path:"id"`
}

type maintenanceCreateInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	Body MaintenanceWindowBody
}

type maintenanceUpdateInput struct {
	Org     string `path:"org"`
	ID      string `path:"id"`
	IfMatch string `header:"If-Match" doc:"The window's ETag; required"`
	Body    MaintenanceWindowBody
}

type maintenanceOutput struct {
	ETag string `header:"ETag"`
	Body struct {
		Window maintenanceWindowDTO `json:"window"`
	}
}

// registerMaintenanceRoutes wires spec §6's windows: maintenance.read
// (viewer) to see them, maintenance.write (operator) to plan them.
func registerMaintenanceRoutes(hapi huma.API, deps Deps) {
	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/maintenance-windows", func(ctx context.Context, in *struct {
		Org string `path:"org"`
	}) (*maintenanceListOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermMaintenanceRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		list, active, err := deps.Maintenance.List(ctx, scope.OrgID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapMaintenanceErr(ctx, err)
		}
		out := &maintenanceListOutput{}
		out.Body.Windows = make([]maintenanceWindowDTO, len(list))
		for i, w := range list {
			out.Body.Windows[i] = toMaintenanceWindowDTO(w, active[w.ID])
		}
		return out, nil
	})

	huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/maintenance-windows", func(ctx context.Context, in *maintenanceCreateInput) (*maintenanceOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermMaintenanceWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		input, err := in.Body.input()
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		w, err := deps.Maintenance.Create(ctx, scope.OrgID, scope.UserID, input)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapMaintenanceErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, w.ID.String(), "created maintenance window "+w.Name)
		return maintenanceOut(w), nil
	}, func(o *huma.Operation) { o.DefaultStatus = http.StatusCreated }, withAudit("createMaintenanceWindow", "maintenance"))

	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/maintenance-windows/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *maintenanceIDInput) (*maintenanceOutput, error) {
		scope, id, err := maintenanceScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermMaintenanceRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		w, err := deps.Maintenance.Get(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapMaintenanceErr(ctx, err)
		}
		return maintenanceOut(w), nil
	})

	huma.Put(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/maintenance-windows/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *maintenanceUpdateInput) (*maintenanceOutput, error) {
		scope, id, err := maintenanceScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermMaintenanceWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		rev, err := requireIfMatch(in.IfMatch)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		input, err := in.Body.input()
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		w, err := deps.Maintenance.Update(ctx, scope.OrgID, id, rev, input)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapMaintenanceErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, w.ID.String(), "updated maintenance window "+w.Name)
		return maintenanceOut(w), nil
	}, withAudit("updateMaintenanceWindow", "maintenance"))

	huma.Delete(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/maintenance-windows/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *maintenanceIDInput) (*okOutput, error) {
		scope, id, err := maintenanceScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermMaintenanceWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if err := deps.Maintenance.Delete(ctx, scope.OrgID, id); err != nil {
			return nil, mapMaintenanceErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, id.String(), "deleted maintenance window")
		return &okOutput{}, nil
	}, withAudit("deleteMaintenanceWindow", "maintenance"))
}

func (b MaintenanceWindowBody) input() (maintenance.Input, error) {
	target, err := optionalUUID(b.TargetID, "target id")
	if err != nil {
		return maintenance.Input{}, err
	}
	return maintenance.Input{
		Name: b.Name, TargetKind: maintenance.TargetKind(b.TargetKind), TargetID: target,
		StartsAt: b.StartsAt, EndsAt: b.EndsAt, Cron: b.Cron, Duration: b.Duration, Timezone: b.Timezone, Note: b.Note,
	}, nil
}

func toMaintenanceWindowDTO(w maintenance.Window, active bool) maintenanceWindowDTO {
	dto := maintenanceWindowDTO{
		ID: w.ID.String(), Name: w.Name, TargetKind: string(w.TargetKind), TargetName: w.TargetName,
		StartsAt: w.StartsAt, EndsAt: w.EndsAt, Cron: w.Cron, Duration: w.DurationText(), Timezone: w.Timezone,
		Note: w.Note, ActiveNow: active, Revision: w.Revision, CreatedAt: w.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: w.UpdatedAt,
	}
	if w.TargetID != nil {
		dto.TargetID = w.TargetID.String()
	}
	if w.Cron != "" {
		dto.Description = cronexpr.Describe(w.Cron)
	}
	return dto
}

func maintenanceScope(ctx context.Context, deps Deps, org, rawID string, perm tenancy.Permission) (Scope, uuid.UUID, error) {
	scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, org, perm)
	if err != nil {
		return Scope{}, uuid.Nil, err
	}
	id, err := parseUUID(rawID, "window id")
	return scope, id, err
}

// maintenanceOut shapes a single window; active_now is not computed for a
// single read (the list carries it).
func maintenanceOut(w maintenance.Window) *maintenanceOutput {
	out := &maintenanceOutput{ETag: revisionETag(w.Revision)}
	out.Body.Window = toMaintenanceWindowDTO(w, false)
	return out
}

func mapMaintenanceErr(ctx context.Context, err error) error {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, maintenance.ErrInvalidInput):
		return huma.Error400BadRequest(err.Error())
	case errors.Is(err, maintenance.ErrNotFound):
		return huma.Error404NotFound("not found")
	case errors.Is(err, maintenance.ErrNameTaken):
		return huma.Error409Conflict("a maintenance window with this name already exists")
	case errors.Is(err, maintenance.ErrRevisionMismatch):
		return huma.Error412PreconditionFailed("this changed since you loaded it; reload and apply your change again")
	default:
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "unmapped maintenance error", "err", err)
		return huma.Error500InternalServerError("internal error")
	}
}
```

`internal/app/maintenance_ports.go`:

```go
package app

import (
	"context"
	"errors"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/render"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/maintenance"
)

// groupHosts adapts the job scope resolver to maintenance.GroupHosts: a
// group window covers the managed hosts a run scoped to that group (and
// its subgroups) would target — the same selection, so the maintenance
// guard and the run agree on which hosts a group means.
type groupHosts struct{ r jobs.ScopeResolver }

// NewGroupHosts wires the adapter (also used by internal/api/apitest).
func NewGroupHosts(r jobs.ScopeResolver) maintenance.GroupHosts { return groupHosts{r: r} }

// HostsInGroup resolves the group; a group deleted meanwhile covers
// nothing (its windows go with it through the foreign key).
func (g groupHosts) HostsInGroup(ctx context.Context, orgID, groupID uuid.UUID) ([]uuid.UUID, error) {
	targets, err := g.r.Resolve(ctx, orgID, render.Scope{GroupID: &groupID})
	if errors.Is(err, jobs.ErrNotFound) {
		return nil, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var out []uuid.UUID
	for _, t := range targets {
		out = append(out, t.HostIDs...)
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

```tsx
const org = (role: string) => ({ org: { id: 'o', name: 'Acme', slug: 'acme', role, created_at: '' } });
const nightly: MaintenanceWindow = {
  id: 'w1', name: 'nightly', target_kind: 'org', cron: '0 2 * * 6', description: 'Sat at 02:00', duration: '2h',
  timezone: 'Europe/Berlin', active_now: true, revision: 1, created_at: '', updated_at: '',
};
const dbUpgrade: MaintenanceWindow = {
  id: 'w2', name: 'db upgrade', target_kind: 'host', target_id: 'h1', target_name: 'db-1', starts_at: '2026-09-22T10:00:00Z',
  ends_at: '2026-09-22T12:00:00Z', note: 'kernel', active_now: false, revision: 3, created_at: '', updated_at: '',
};
const noHosts = { hosts: [], next_cursor: '' };

function renderPage() {

describe('maintenance time helpers', () => {

    expect(whenLabel({ ...nightly, description: '' })).toBe('0 2 * * 6 · for 2h · Europe/Berlin');
    expect(whenLabel(dbUpgrade)).toContain('–');

});
```

`web/src/api/hooks/useMaintenance.ts`:

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, unwrap } from '../client.ts';
import type { MaintenanceWindowInput } from '../types.ts';

const ifMatch = (revision: number) => ({ 'If-Match': `"${revision}"` });

function useInvalidate(org: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return () => {
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['maintenance-windows', org] });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['calendar', org] });
  };
}

export function useMaintenanceWindows(org: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['maintenance-windows', org],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/maintenance-windows', { params: { path: { org } } })),
    enabled: !!org,
  });
}

export function useCreateMaintenanceWindow(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: MaintenanceWindowInput) => unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/maintenance-windows', { params: { path: { org } }, body })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useUpdateMaintenanceWindow(org: string, id: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ revision, ...body }: MaintenanceWindowInput & { revision: number }) =>
      unwrap(api.PUT('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/maintenance-windows/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id }, header: ifMatch(revision) }, body })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useDeleteMaintenanceWindow(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => unwrap(api.DELETE('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/maintenance-windows/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}
```

`web/src/features/maintenance/time.ts`:

```ts
import type { MaintenanceWindow } from '../../api/types.ts';

const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');

/** toLocalInput renders an instant for a datetime-local input (the
 * browser's own time zone), the inverse of fromLocalInput. */
export function toLocalInput(iso?: string | null): string {
  if (!iso) return '';
  const d = new Date(iso);
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}T${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`;
}

/** fromLocalInput reads a datetime-local value as the browser's local
 * time and returns the instant as ISO 8601 (UTC). */
export function fromLocalInput(value: string): string {
  return new Date(value).toISOString();
}

/** whenLabel says when a window applies, in words. */
export function whenLabel(w: MaintenanceWindow): string {
  if (w.cron) {
    return `${w.description || w.cron} · for ${w.duration ?? ''} · ${w.timezone ?? ''}`;
  }
  const fmt = (iso?: string | null) => (iso ? new Date(iso).toLocaleString(undefined, { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' }) : '');
  return `${fmt(w.starts_at)} – ${fmt(w.ends_at)}`;
}

/** targetLabel names what a window covers. */
export function targetLabel(w: MaintenanceWindow): string {
  if (w.target_kind === 'org') return 'Whole organization';
  return `${w.target_kind === 'host' ? 'Host' : 'Group'} ${w.target_name ?? ''}`.trim();
}
```

`web/src/features/maintenance/WindowDialog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Dialog } from '../../ui/dialog.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '../../ui/input.tsx';
import { Label } from '../../ui/label.tsx';
import { useHosts } from '../../api/hooks/useHosts.ts';
import { useGroups } from '../../api/hooks/useInventory.ts';
import { useCreateMaintenanceWindow, useUpdateMaintenanceWindow } from '../../api/hooks/useMaintenance.ts';
import type { MaintenanceWindow, MaintenanceWindowInput } from '../../api/types.ts';
import { fromLocalInput, toLocalInput } from './time.ts';

const SELECT = 'h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground';

type TargetKind = 'org' | 'host' | 'inventory_group';

/** WindowDialog creates or edits a maintenance window: its target (the
 * organization, a host or an inventory group) and either a one-off
 * stretch or a recurring timetable. */
export function WindowDialog({ org, window: w, onClose }: { org: string; window?: MaintenanceWindow; onClose: () => void }) {
  const create = useCreateMaintenanceWindow(org);
  const update = useUpdateMaintenanceWindow(org, w?.id ?? '');
  const [name, setName] = useState(w?.name ?? '');
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<TargetKind>((w?.target_kind as TargetKind) ?? 'org');
  const [targetId, setTargetId] = useState(w?.target_id ?? '');
  const [hostQuery, setHostQuery] = useState('');
  const [recurring, setRecurring] = useState(!!w?.cron);
  const [startsAt, setStartsAt] = useState(toLocalInput(w?.starts_at));
  const [endsAt, setEndsAt] = useState(toLocalInput(w?.ends_at));
  const [cron, setCron] = useState(w?.cron ?? '0 2 * * 6');
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState(w?.duration ?? '2h');
  const [timezone, setTimezone] = useState(w?.timezone ?? Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone);
  const [note, setNote] = useState(w?.note ?? '');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const hosts = useHosts(org, { managed: true, q: hostQuery || undefined });
  const groups = useGroups(org);
  const hostList = hosts.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.hosts ?? []) ?? [];
  const groupList = groups.data?.groups ?? [];

  function body(): MaintenanceWindowInput {
    const base = { name, target_kind: kind, ...(kind !== 'org' && targetId ? { target_id: targetId } : {}), note };
    return recurring
      ? { ...base, cron, duration, timezone }
      : { ...base, starts_at: startsAt ? fromLocalInput(startsAt) : undefined, ends_at: endsAt ? fromLocalInput(endsAt) : undefined };
  }

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    try {
      if (w) await update.mutateAsync({ ...body(), revision: w.revision });
      else await create.mutateAsync(body());
      onClose();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Saving failed');
    }
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open onClose={onClose} title={w ? `Edit ${w.name}` : 'New maintenance window'} size="wide">
      <form className="flex flex-col gap-3" onSubmit={(e) => void submit(e)}>
        <Label htmlFor="mw-name">Name</Label>
        <Input id="mw-name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} required maxLength={100} />
        <Label htmlFor="mw-kind">Applies to</Label>
        <select id="mw-kind" className={SELECT} value={kind} onChange={(e) => { setKind(e.target.value as TargetKind); setTargetId(''); }}>
          <option value="org">The whole organization</option>
          <option value="host">One host</option>
          <option value="inventory_group">An inventory group (and its subgroups)</option>
        </select>
        {kind === 'host' && (
          <>
            <Input aria-label="Find host" placeholder="Filter hosts by name" value={hostQuery} onChange={(e) => setHostQuery(e.target.value)} />
            <select aria-label="Host" className={SELECT} value={targetId} onChange={(e) => setTargetId(e.target.value)} required>
              <option value="">Choose a host…</option>
              {hostList.map((h) => (
                <option key={h.id} value={h.id}>
                  {h.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </>
        )}
        {kind === 'inventory_group' && (
          <select aria-label="Group" className={SELECT} value={targetId} onChange={(e) => setTargetId(e.target.value)} required>
            <option value="">Choose a group…</option>
            {groupList.map((g) => (
              <option key={g.id} value={g.id}>
                {g.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        )}
        <div role="radiogroup" aria-label="When" className="flex gap-1">
          {([false, true] as const).map((r) => (
            <button
              key={String(r)}
              type="button"
              role="radio"
              aria-checked={recurring === r}
              className={`rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1 text-xs ${recurring === r ? 'border-primary text-primary' : 'border-border text-muted-foreground'}`}
              onClick={() => setRecurring(r)}
            >
              {r ? 'Recurring' : 'Once'}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        {recurring ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <Label htmlFor="mw-cron">Starts (cron)</Label>
              <Input id="mw-cron" className="font-mono" value={cron} onChange={(e) => setCron(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <Label htmlFor="mw-duration">Lasts</Label>
              <Input id="mw-duration" className="font-mono" value={duration} onChange={(e) => setDuration(e.target.value)} placeholder="2h" />
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <Label htmlFor="mw-tz">Timezone</Label>
              <Input id="mw-tz" className="font-mono" value={timezone} onChange={(e) => setTimezone(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <Label htmlFor="mw-start">From</Label>
              <Input id="mw-start" type="datetime-local" value={startsAt} onChange={(e) => setStartsAt(e.target.value)} required />
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <Label htmlFor="mw-end">Until</Label>
              <Input id="mw-end" type="datetime-local" value={endsAt} onChange={(e) => setEndsAt(e.target.value)} required />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
        <Label htmlFor="mw-note">Note</Label>
        <textarea id="mw-note" className="min-h-16 rounded-md border border-border bg-background p-2 text-xs" value={note} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} maxLength={1000} />
        {error && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-[var(--px-crit)]">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={create.isPending || update.isPending}>
            Save
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/maintenance/MaintenancePage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useParams } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { Wrench } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card, GridHeader, GridRow } from '../../ui/table.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Pill } from '../../ui/badge.tsx';
import { QueryLoading } from '../../ui/QueryState.tsx';
import { useQueryGuard } from '../../ui/useQueryGuard.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { useOrg } from '../../api/hooks/useOrgs.ts';
import { useDeleteMaintenanceWindow, useMaintenanceWindows } from '../../api/hooks/useMaintenance.ts';
import { may } from '../../lib/roles.ts';
import type { MaintenanceWindow } from '../../api/types.ts';
import { WindowDialog } from './WindowDialog.tsx';
import { targetLabel, whenLabel } from './time.ts';

const COLUMNS = 'minmax(0, 0.8fr) minmax(0, 0.8fr) minmax(0, 1.4fr) 110px 150px';

/** MaintenancePage lists the organization's maintenance windows (spec
 * 2026-09-18 §6): what each covers, when, and whether it is active now.
 * Schedules can skip runs, or leave out hosts, during a window. */
export function MaintenancePage() {
  const { org } = useParams({ strict: false }) as { org: string };
  const toast = useToast();
  const orgInfo = useOrg(org);
  const canWrite = may(orgInfo.data?.org.role, 'maintenance.write');
  const windows = useMaintenanceWindows(org);
  const remove = useDeleteMaintenanceWindow(org);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<MaintenanceWindow | 'new' | null>(null);

  const guard = useQueryGuard(windows);
  if (guard) return guard;
  if (!windows.data) return <QueryLoading />;
  const list = windows.data.windows ?? [];

  async function onDelete(w: MaintenanceWindow) {
    if (!confirm(`Delete maintenance window ${w.name}?`)) return;
    try {
      await remove.mutateAsync(w.id);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Deleting failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Maintenance</h1>
          <span className="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">
            {list.filter((w) => w.active_now).length} active now · schedules can skip runs or leave out hosts during a window
          </span>
        </div>
        {canWrite && (
          <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setEditing('new')}>
            <Wrench size={15} /> New window
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
      <Card>
        <GridHeader columns={COLUMNS}>
          <span>Window</span>
          <span>Applies to</span>
          <span>When</span>
          <span>Status</span>
          <span />
        </GridHeader>
        {list.map((w) => (
          <GridRow key={w.id} columns={COLUMNS}>
            <span className="flex min-w-0 flex-col" data-row>
              <span className="truncate text-[13px] font-medium">{w.name}</span>
              {w.note && <span className="truncate text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{w.note}</span>}
            </span>
            <span className="truncate text-xs">{targetLabel(w)}</span>
            <span className="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">{whenLabel(w)}</span>
            <span>{w.active_now ? <Pill tone="warn">active now</Pill> : <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">—</span>}</span>
            <span className="flex justify-end gap-1">
              {canWrite && (
                <>
                  <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Edit ${w.name}`} onClick={() => setEditing(w)}>
                    Edit
                  </Button>
                  <Button type="button" variant="destructive" aria-label={`Delete ${w.name}`} onClick={() => void onDelete(w)}>
                    Delete
                  </Button>
                </>
              )}
            </span>
          </GridRow>
        ))}
        {list.length === 0 && <p className="px-4 py-6 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">No maintenance windows.</p>}
      </Card>
      {editing && <WindowDialog org={org} window={editing === 'new' ? undefined : editing} onClose={() => setEditing(null)} />}
    </>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Docs**

Create `~/_dev/noix-docs/src/content/docs/pulsix/user/maintenance-windows.md`:

```markdown
---
title: Maintenance windows
description: Plan maintenance on a host, an inventory group or the whole organization, once or on a recurring timetable
sidebar:
  order: 4.8
---

A *maintenance window* marks a stretch of time in which hosts are being worked on.
Schedules can react to it: skip their run, or leave the hosts in maintenance out
(see [Schedules → Maintenance windows](/pulsix/user/schedules/#maintenance-windows)). The
calendar on the *Schedules* page draws every window as a violet bar.

Windows are listed under *Maintenance* in the sidebar, with whether each one is
active right now.

## What a window covers

| Applies to | Covers |
|---|---|
| **One host** | that host |
| **An inventory group** | every managed host in the group and in its subgroups — the same hosts a job template scoped to that group would run on |
| **The whole organization** | every host |

Deleting a host or a group deletes its maintenance windows.

## When

- **Once**: from a start to an end time (at most a year apart), entered in your
  browser's time zone.
- **Recurring**: a cron expression for when each occurrence starts, how long it
  lasts (`90m`, `2h`, up to 31 days, whole minutes) and the time zone the expression
  is read in — for example `0 2 * * 6`, `4h`, `Europe/Berlin` for every Saturday from
  02:00 to 06:00 local time. Changes between summer and winter time are handled the
  same way as for schedules.

A note on the window is shown in the list, for example the ticket it belongs to.

## Who may do what

Everyone in the organization sees maintenance windows; **operators** (and above)
create, edit and delete them.

:::note[Coming later]
Silencing alerts during a window and announcing windows on status pages arrive with
alerting and status pages.
:::
```

In `reference/api.md`, add after "Notification channels":

```markdown
## Maintenance windows

Windows live under `/api/v1/orgs/{slug}/maintenance-windows`. Everyone can read
them; operators (and above) change them.

| Method | Path | Success |
|---|---|---|
| GET | `/maintenance-windows` | 200 `{windows: [...]}` — each with `active_now` |
| POST | `/maintenance-windows` | 201 `{window}` + `ETag` |
| GET | `/maintenance-windows/{id}` | 200 `{window}` + `ETag` |
| PUT | `/maintenance-windows/{id}` | 200 + `ETag` — requires `If-Match` |
| DELETE | `/maintenance-windows/{id}` | 200 `{}` |

A body names `target_kind` (`host`, `inventory_group`, `org`) and, except for `org`, a
`target_id`, then either `starts_at` and `ends_at`, or `cron`, `duration` (for example
`"90m"`) and `timezone`.
```

### Task 8: The schedules' maintenance guard

This task covers spec §6's per-schedule guard: `ignore` (the default), `skip` or `exclude_hosts`. The guard runs in `Decide` after the late/catch-up rule and before the overlap guard. It records the new `skipped_maintenance` outcome (the CHECK was widened in Task 6), or it hands the hosts in a window to the enqueue as `ExcludeHosts`. The schedule panel gets a select for it.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/schedules/maintenance_guard.go`; Test: `internal/schedules/maintenance_guard_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/schedules/schedule.go` (`Maintenance`, `Guards.Maintenance`, `OutcomeSkippedMaintenance`), `validate.go` (`normalizeGuards`), `service.go` (`RunEnqueuer.RunTargets`, the `maint` field, the `enqueue` exclude parameter), `decide.go` (the guard, `ErrAllHostsExcluded`)
- Modify: the `RunEnqueuer` fakes in `internal/schedules/fakes_test.go`, `decide_test.go` (`panicOnceRuns`), `due_sql_test.go` (`blockingRuns`)
- Modify: `internal/api/schedule_dto.go` (`guards.maintenance`, the outcome enum); Test: `internal/api/schedule_maintenance_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/app/app.go`, `internal/api/apitest/apitest.go` (`schedulesSvc.SetMaintenance`)
- Modify: `web/src/features/schedules/ScheduleEditor.tsx`, `outcome.ts`, `SchedulesPage.test.tsx`
- Regenerate: `web/src/api/schema.d.ts`
- Docs (noix-docs): `user/schedules.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5's `jobs.Service.RunTargets`, `jobs.RunRequest.ExcludeHosts`, `jobs.ErrAllHostsExcluded`. Task 6's `maintenance.Service.HostsInMaintenance`.
- Produces (package `schedules`):
  - `type Maintenance string` (`MaintenanceIgnore` `ignore`, `MaintenanceSkip` `skip`, `MaintenanceExcludeHosts` `exclude_hosts`), `Guards.Maintenance Maintenance` (json `maintenance,omitempty`)
  - `OutcomeSkippedMaintenance Outcome = "skipped_maintenance"`
  - `type MaintenanceChecker interface{ HostsInMaintenance(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, hostIDs []uuid.UUID, at time.Time) (map[uuid.UUID]bool, error) }`, `func (s *Service) SetMaintenance(m MaintenanceChecker)`
  - `RunEnqueuer` gains `RunTargets(ctx, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID) ([]uuid.UUID, error)` (Task 10 adds `GroupSummaries`)
- Produces (HTTP): `guards.maintenance` (`ignore`, `skip`, `exclude_hosts`) on the schedule body and DTO; `skipped_maintenance` in the run outcome enum.

```go
package schedules_test

import (
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/schedules"
)

func TestMaintenanceGuard(t *testing.T) {
	base := time.Date(2026, 9, 18, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	hA, hB := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	guards := func(m schedules.Maintenance) schedules.Guards {
		return schedules.Guards{Overlap: schedules.OverlapSkip, CatchUp: schedules.CatchUp{Window: "1h"}, Maintenance: m}
	}
	cases := []struct {
		name     string
		mode     schedules.Maintenance
		inWindow []uuid.UUID
		checkErr error
		outcome  schedules.Outcome
		reason   string
		exclude  []uuid.UUID
		enqueued bool
	}{
		{name: "ignore runs inside a window", mode: schedules.MaintenanceIgnore, inWindow: []uuid.UUID{hA, hB},
			outcome: schedules.OutcomeTriggered, enqueued: true},
		{name: "skip without a window runs", mode: schedules.MaintenanceSkip,
			outcome: schedules.OutcomeTriggered, enqueued: true},
		{name: "skip with one host in a window", mode: schedules.MaintenanceSkip, inWindow: []uuid.UUID{hA},
			outcome: schedules.OutcomeSkippedMaintenance, reason: "1 targeted host(s) in a maintenance window"},
		{name: "exclude_hosts leaves the host out", mode: schedules.MaintenanceExcludeHosts, inWindow: []uuid.UUID{hA},
			outcome: schedules.OutcomeTriggered, reason: "1 host(s) left out for maintenance", exclude: []uuid.UUID{hA}, enqueued: true},
		{name: "exclude_hosts with every host in a window skips", mode: schedules.MaintenanceExcludeHosts, inWindow: []uuid.UUID{hA, hB},
			outcome: schedules.OutcomeSkippedMaintenance, reason: "every targeted host"},
		{name: "a failing check fails closed", mode: schedules.MaintenanceSkip, checkErr: errors.New("db down"),
			outcome: schedules.OutcomeFailed, reason: "could not check maintenance windows"},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			w := newWorld(t)
			m := &fakeMaintenance{inWindow: map[uuid.UUID]bool{}, err: c.checkErr}
			for _, h := range c.inWindow {
				m.inWindow[h] = true
			}
			w.svc.SetMaintenance(m)
			w.runs.targets = []uuid.UUID{hA, hB}
			s := due(t, w, "0 * * * *", base, guards(c.mode))
			d := w.svc.Decide(ctx, s, base.Add(time.Second), grace)
			if d.Run.Outcome != c.outcome || !strings.Contains(d.Run.Reason, c.reason) {
				t.Fatalf("run = %+v, want %s containing %q", d.Run, c.outcome, c.reason)
			}
			if got := len(w.runs.requests) == 1; got != c.enqueued {
				t.Fatalf("enqueued = %v, want %v", got, c.enqueued)
			}
			if c.enqueued && len(c.exclude) > 0 {
				if ex := w.runs.requests[0].ExcludeHosts; len(ex) != 1 || ex[0] != c.exclude[0] {
					t.Fatalf("exclude = %v, want %v", ex, c.exclude)
				}
			}
			if d.NextRunAt == nil {
				t.Fatal("a guarded decision must still advance the schedule")
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestMaintenanceGuardWithoutACheckerFailsClosed(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	s := due(t, w, "0 * * * *", w.now, schedules.Guards{Overlap: schedules.OverlapSkip, CatchUp: schedules.CatchUp{Window: "1h"}, Maintenance: schedules.MaintenanceSkip})
	if d := w.svc.Decide(ctx, s, w.now, grace); d.Run.Outcome != schedules.OutcomeFailed || len(w.runs.requests) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("decision = %+v, want failed and no run", d.Run)
	}
}

func TestMaintenanceGuardRaceWithTheEnqueueSkips(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	w.svc.SetMaintenance(&fakeMaintenance{inWindow: map[uuid.UUID]bool{}})
	w.runs.err = jobs.ErrAllHostsExcluded
	s := due(t, w, "0 * * * *", w.now, schedules.Guards{Overlap: schedules.OverlapSkip, CatchUp: schedules.CatchUp{Window: "1h"}, Maintenance: schedules.MaintenanceExcludeHosts})
	if d := w.svc.Decide(ctx, s, w.now, grace); d.Run.Outcome != schedules.OutcomeSkippedMaintenance {
		t.Fatalf("decision = %+v, want skipped_maintenance", d.Run)
	}
}

func TestMaintenanceGuardTargetsFailureFailsTheRun(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	w.svc.SetMaintenance(&fakeMaintenance{})
	w.runs.targetsErr = jobs.ErrNotFound
	s := due(t, w, "0 * * * *", w.now, schedules.Guards{Overlap: schedules.OverlapSkip, CatchUp: schedules.CatchUp{Window: "1h"}, Maintenance: schedules.MaintenanceSkip})
	if d := w.svc.Decide(ctx, s, w.now, grace); d.Run.Outcome != schedules.OutcomeFailed {
		t.Fatalf("decision = %+v, want failed", d.Run)
	}
}

func TestMaintenanceGuardValidation(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	in := validInput(w.templateID)
	s, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, in)
	if err != nil || s.Guards.Maintenance != schedules.MaintenanceIgnore {
		t.Fatalf("default guard = %q, %v", s.Guards.Maintenance, err)
	}
	in.Name, in.Guards.Maintenance = "other", "sometimes"
	if _, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, in); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrInvalidInput) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown guard = %v", err)
	}
}
```

```go
func (f *fakeRuns) RunTargets(context.Context, uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID) ([]uuid.UUID, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	return f.targets, f.targetsErr
}

// fakeMaintenance reports the hosts of inWindow as in maintenance.
type fakeMaintenance struct {
	inWindow map[uuid.UUID]bool
	err      error
}

func (f *fakeMaintenance) HostsInMaintenance(_ context.Context, _ uuid.UUID, hostIDs []uuid.UUID, _ time.Time) (map[uuid.UUID]bool, error) {
	if f.err != nil {
		return nil, f.err
	}
	out := map[uuid.UUID]bool{}
	for _, h := range hostIDs {
		if f.inWindow[h] {
			out[h] = true
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

In `internal/schedules/schedule.go`, replace the `Guards` doc comment and struct with:

```go
// Maintenance is the guard for a due run while targeted hosts are in a
// maintenance window (spec §6): ignore (the default — patch runs often
// belong inside a window), skip the run when any targeted host is in a
// window, or exclude those hosts (skip when none remains).
type Maintenance string

const (
	MaintenanceIgnore       Maintenance = "ignore"
	MaintenanceSkip         Maintenance = "skip"
	MaintenanceExcludeHosts Maintenance = "exclude_hosts"
)

// Guards is the schedules.guards jsonb. Unknown keys never reach the
// database because the API decodes into this struct; rows stored before
// v0.2d have no maintenance key and read as ignore.
type Guards struct {
	Overlap     Overlap     `json:"overlap"`
	CatchUp     CatchUp     `json:"catch_up"`
	Maintenance Maintenance `json:"maintenance,omitempty"`
}
```

and add `OutcomeSkippedMaintenance Outcome = "skipped_maintenance"` after `OutcomeSkippedOverlap`.

`internal/schedules/maintenance_guard.go`:

```go
package schedules

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
)

// MaintenanceChecker answers which hosts are in a maintenance window at an
// instant (maintenance.Service).
type MaintenanceChecker interface {
	HostsInMaintenance(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, hostIDs []uuid.UUID, at time.Time) (map[uuid.UUID]bool, error)
}

// SetMaintenance injects the maintenance-window checker the maintenance
// guard asks — post-construction wiring like the job queue's sinks, so the
// schedules service keeps one constructor for its many callers.
func (s *Service) SetMaintenance(m MaintenanceChecker) { s.maint = m }

// errNoMaintenanceChecker is a wiring gap: a schedule asks for a
// maintenance guard but nothing answers it.
var errNoMaintenanceChecker = errors.New("no maintenance checker is wired")

// hostsInMaintenance returns the run's targeted hosts that are in a
// maintenance window at now, and whether that is every targeted host.
func (s *Service) hostsInMaintenance(ctx context.Context, sch Schedule, now time.Time) ([]uuid.UUID, bool, error) {
	if s.maint == nil {
		return nil, false, errNoMaintenanceChecker
	}
	scoped := db.WithOrgScope(ctx, sch.OrgID)
	targets, err := s.runs.RunTargets(scoped, sch.OrgID, sch.TemplateID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, false, fmt.Errorf("resolve run targets: %w", err)
	}
	covered, err := s.maint.HostsInMaintenance(scoped, sch.OrgID, targets, now)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, false, fmt.Errorf("check maintenance windows: %w", err)
	}
	var in []uuid.UUID
	for _, h := range targets {
		if covered[h] {
			in = append(in, h)
		}
	}
	return in, len(targets) > 0 && len(in) == len(targets), nil
}

// joinReason appends b to a reason that may already say something.
func joinReason(a, b string) string {
	if a == "" {
		return b
	}
	return a + "; " + b
}
```

```go
			reason = joinReason(reason, fmt.Sprintf("%d host(s) left out for maintenance", len(inWindow)))
		}
	}
```

`internal/api/schedule_maintenance_test.go`:

```go
package api_test

import (
	"net/http"
	"testing"
)

// TestScheduleMaintenanceGuardRoundTrips: the guard defaults to ignore,
// takes skip and exclude_hosts, and refuses anything else.
func TestScheduleMaintenanceGuardRoundTrips(t *testing.T) {
	f := newRunFixture(t, "sched-maint")
	body := f.scheduleBody("Nightly")
	resp := postJSON(t, f.operatorC, f.schedulesURL(), body)
	var created struct {
		Schedule struct {
			ID     string `json:"id"`
			Guards struct {
				Maintenance string `json:"maintenance"`
			} `json:"guards"`
		} `json:"schedule"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &created)
	if created.Schedule.Guards.Maintenance != "ignore" {
		t.Fatalf("default maintenance guard = %q", created.Schedule.Guards.Maintenance)
	}
	body["name"], body["guards"] = "Guarded", map[string]any{"maintenance": "exclude_hosts"}
	resp = postJSON(t, f.operatorC, f.schedulesURL(), body)
	decodeInto(t, resp, &created)
	if created.Schedule.Guards.Maintenance != "exclude_hosts" {
		t.Fatalf("maintenance guard = %q", created.Schedule.Guards.Maintenance)
	}
	body["name"], body["guards"] = "Bad", map[string]any{"maintenance": "sometimes"}
	if resp := postJSON(t, f.operatorC, f.schedulesURL(), body); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusUnprocessableEntity {
		t.Fatalf("unknown guard = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Docs**

In `~/_dev/noix-docs/src/content/docs/pulsix/user/schedules.md`, insert before "## Overrides":

```markdown
## Maintenance windows

A schedule decides what happens when a host it would run on is in a
[maintenance window](/pulsix/user/maintenance-windows/) at the time the run is due:

- **Run anyway** (the default): patch runs often belong inside a window.
- **Skip the run**: when any targeted host is in a window, the run does not start and
  is recorded as *skipped (maintenance)*.
- **Leave those hosts out**: the run starts on the other hosts only, and the history
  says how many were left out. When every targeted host is in a window, the run is
  skipped.

If Pulsix cannot check the windows at that moment, the run does not start and is
recorded as failed — a guard that cannot be evaluated never lets a run through.
```

and correct the Run now bullet (v0.2c said guards apply, but they never did) to:

```markdown
- **Run now** fires a schedule immediately, outside its timetable, with the
  schedule's overrides, and takes you straight to the run. Guards — overlap and
  maintenance windows — do not apply to a run you start yourself.
```

In the paragraph after it, change "fired, skipped, missed or failed" to "fired, skipped (overlap or maintenance), missed or failed".

### Task 9: Schedule notification rules and the outbox

This task covers spec §7's `notify_rules`. The rules are stored merged per channel, so a run yields at most one message per channel. They are read and replaced as a sub-resource of the schedule in the spec's `{on, channels}` shape. The transactional outbox connects both sources. A finished scheduled run group reports from its terminal transaction through Task 5's `RunGroupSink`. A skipped, missed or failed decision reports from the ticker's row savepoint. The schedule panel gets the channel × category matrix.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/notify/rules.go`, `internal/notify/outbox.go`; Test: `internal/notify/rules_test.go`, `internal/notify/outbox_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/notify/service.go` (`Deps.Rules`), `internal/notify/store_sql.go` (the `d` field, `ScheduleRules`, `ReplaceScheduleRules`), `internal/db/queries/notifications.sql` (rule and outbox queries), regenerate `internal/db/gen`
- Modify: `internal/schedules/due_sql.go` (`DecisionOutbox`, `SetOutbox`, the hook in `processRow`); Test: `internal/schedules/decision_outbox_test.go`
- Create: `internal/api/notify_rules_handlers.go`; Test: `internal/api/notify_rules_handlers_test.go`; Modify: `internal/api/api.go` (route), `internal/api/apitest/apitest.go`, `internal/app/app.go`
- Create: `web/src/features/schedules/ScheduleNotifications.tsx`; Test: `web/src/features/schedules/ScheduleNotifications.test.tsx`; Modify: `web/src/api/hooks/useNotifications.ts`, `web/src/features/schedules/ScheduleEditor.tsx`
- Regenerate: `web/src/api/schema.d.ts`
- Docs (noix-docs): `user/notifications.md`, `user/schedules.md`, `reference/api.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5's `jobs.RunGroupSink`, `jobs.RunGroupResult`, `jobs.SQLQueue.SetRunGroupSink`. Tasks 2–3's `Event`, `runCategories`, `decisionCategory`, `DeliveryPending`.
- Produces (package `notify`):
  - `type Rule struct{ On Category; Channels []uuid.UUID }`, `type ChannelRule struct{ ChannelID uuid.UUID; ChannelName string; ChannelKind Kind; Events []Category }`, `type RuleView struct{ On Category; Channels []ChannelRef }`, `type ChannelRef struct{ ID uuid.UUID; Name string; Kind Kind }`
  - `type RuleStore interface{ ScheduleRules(ctx, orgID, scheduleID uuid.UUID) ([]ChannelRule, error); ReplaceScheduleRules(ctx, orgID, scheduleID uuid.UUID, rules []ChannelRule) error }`, `Deps.Rules RuleStore`
  - `Service.ScheduleRules(ctx, orgID, scheduleID uuid.UUID) ([]RuleView, error)`, `Service.SetScheduleRules(ctx, orgID, scheduleID uuid.UUID, rules []Rule) ([]RuleView, error)`
  - `type Outbox`, `func NewOutbox(d *db.DB, publicURL string, now func() time.Time, rand io.Reader, log *slog.Logger) *Outbox`
  - `func (o *Outbox) RunGroupFinished(ctx context.Context, r jobs.RunGroupResult) error` (implements `jobs.RunGroupSink`)
  - `type Decision struct{ OrgID, ScheduleID, RunID uuid.UUID; Outcome, Reason string; DueAt, DecidedAt time.Time }`, `func (o *Outbox) EnqueueDecision(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, d Decision) error`
  - unexported, used by Task 10: `(*Outbox).inSavepoint`, `(*Outbox).insert`
- Produces (package `schedules`): `type DecisionOutbox interface{ EnqueueDecision(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, d notify.Decision) error }`, `func (st *DueSQLStore) SetOutbox(o DecisionOutbox)`.
- Produces (HTTP): `GET /api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}/notifications` (templates.read) and `PUT` (templates.write, audited `setScheduleNotifications`, object `schedule`), body `NotifyRulesBody{rules: [{on, channels}]}`, response `{rules: [{on, channels: [{id, name, kind}]}]}`; 400 for an unknown channel, 404 for an unknown schedule.
- Produces (SPA): `useScheduleNotifications(org, scheduleId)`, `useSetScheduleNotifications(org, scheduleId)`, `type NotifyRuleInput`.

- [ ] **Step 1: The rule and outbox queries**

Append to `internal/db/queries/notifications.sql`:

```sql
-- name: ListScheduleNotifications :many
SELECT n.channel_id, n.events, c.name AS channel_name, c.kind AS channel_kind
FROM schedule_notifications AS n
JOIN notification_channels AS c ON c.org_id = n.org_id AND c.id = n.channel_id
WHERE n.org_id = @org_id AND n.schedule_id = @schedule_id
ORDER BY c.name;

-- name: DeleteScheduleNotifications :exec
DELETE FROM schedule_notifications WHERE org_id = @org_id AND schedule_id = @schedule_id;

-- name: InsertScheduleNotification :exec
INSERT INTO schedule_notifications (org_id, schedule_id, channel_id, events)
VALUES (@org_id, @schedule_id, @channel_id, @events);

-- name: NotifyRuleChannels :many
-- The enabled channels a schedule's rules route any of the categories to.
SELECT n.channel_id
FROM schedule_notifications AS n
JOIN notification_channels AS c ON c.org_id = n.org_id AND c.id = n.channel_id
WHERE n.org_id = @org_id AND n.schedule_id = @schedule_id AND c.enabled
  AND n.events && @categories::text[]
ORDER BY n.channel_id;

-- name: NotifyScheduleContext :one
-- The names a message carries, read when the event is enqueued.
SELECT o.slug AS org_slug, o.name AS org_name, s.name AS schedule_name, s.template_id, t.name AS template_name
FROM schedules AS s
JOIN organizations AS o ON o.id = s.org_id
JOIN job_templates AS t ON t.org_id = s.org_id AND t.id = s.template_id
WHERE s.org_id = @org_id AND s.id = @schedule_id;
```

`internal/notify/rules_test.go` (package `notify`):

```go
package notify

import (
	"errors"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

func TestMergeAndSplitRules(t *testing.T) {
	a, b, c := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	merged, err := mergeRules([]Rule{
		{On: CategoryChange, Channels: []uuid.UUID{b}},
		{On: CategoryFailure, Channels: []uuid.UUID{a, b}},
		{On: CategorySkipped, Channels: []uuid.UUID{c}},
		{On: CategoryFailure, Channels: []uuid.UUID{a}}, // repeated: merged away
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(merged) != 3 || merged[0].ChannelID != b || len(merged[0].Events) != 2 ||
		merged[0].Events[0] != CategoryFailure || merged[0].Events[1] != CategoryChange {
		t.Fatalf("merged = %+v", merged)
	}
	merged[0].ChannelName, merged[1].ChannelName, merged[2].ChannelName = "b", "a", "c"
	views := splitRules(merged)
	if len(views) != 3 || views[0].On != CategoryFailure || len(views[0].Channels) != 2 ||
		views[1].On != CategoryChange || views[1].Channels[0].Name != "b" || views[2].On != CategorySkipped {
		t.Fatalf("views = %+v", views)
	}
	for _, bad := range [][]Rule{
		{{On: "always", Channels: []uuid.UUID{a}}},
		{{On: CategoryFailure}},
		make([]Rule, maxRules+1),
	} {
		if _, err := mergeRules(bad); !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidInput) {
			t.Errorf("mergeRules(%v) = %v", bad, err)
		}
	}
	if got := splitRules(nil); got != nil {
		t.Fatalf("no rules = %+v", got)
	}
}
```

`internal/notify/outbox_test.go`:

```go
package notify_test

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"encoding/json"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/testdb"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/notify"
)

type outboxWorld struct {
	d                    *db.DB
	org, schedule        uuid.UUID
	chA, chB, chDisabled notify.Channel
	outbox               *notify.Outbox
	svc                  *notify.Service
}

// newOutboxWorld seeds an organization with a template, a schedule and
// three channels (one disabled), and rules: failure → A and B, change → B,
// skipped → A and the disabled one.
func newOutboxWorld(t *testing.T, slug string) *outboxWorld {
	t.Helper()
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	w := &outboxWorld{d: d, org: seedOrg(t, d, slug)}
	q := gen.New(d.Pool())
	inline := "- hosts: all\n  tasks: []\n"
	pb, err := q.CreatePlaybook(ctx, gen.CreatePlaybookParams{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: w.org, Inline: &inline, Source: "editor"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tmpl, err := q.InsertTemplate(ctx, gen.InsertTemplateParams{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: w.org, Name: "patch-all", PlaybookID: pb.ID, Scope: []byte(`{}`), ExtraVars: []byte(`{}`)})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	sch, err := q.CreateSchedule(ctx, gen.CreateScheduleParams{
		ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: w.org, TemplateID: tmpl.ID, Name: "Nightly", Mode: "cron", Cron: "0 3 * * *",
		Timezone: "UTC", Guards: []byte(`{"overlap":"skip","catch_up":{"enabled":false,"window":"1h"}}`),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	w.schedule = sch.ID
	w.chA, w.chB, w.chDisabled = seedChannel(t, d, w.org), seedChannel(t, d, w.org), seedChannel(t, d, w.org)
	if _, err := d.Pool().Exec(ctx, `UPDATE notification_channels SET enabled = false WHERE id = $1`, w.chDisabled.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	store := notify.NewSQLStore(d)
	w.svc = notify.NewService(notify.Deps{Channels: store, Deliveries: store, Rules: store, Box: fakeBox{}, Mail: &fakeMail{configured: true}})
	if _, err := w.svc.SetScheduleRules(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, w.org), w.org, w.schedule, []notify.Rule{
		{On: notify.CategoryFailure, Channels: []uuid.UUID{w.chA.ID, w.chB.ID}},
		{On: notify.CategoryChange, Channels: []uuid.UUID{w.chB.ID}},
		{On: notify.CategorySkipped, Channels: []uuid.UUID{w.chA.ID, w.chDisabled.ID}},
	}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	w.outbox = notify.NewOutbox(d, "https://pulsix.example.test/", func() time.Time { return time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC) }, rand.Reader, nil)
	return w
}

type queued struct {
	channel uuid.UUID
	key     string
	ev      notify.Event
}

func (w *outboxWorld) queued(t *testing.T) []queued {
	t.Helper()
	rows, err := w.d.Pool().Query(ctx, `SELECT channel_id, event_key, payload FROM notification_deliveries WHERE org_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at, channel_id`, w.org)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	var out []queued
	for rows.Next() {
		var q queued
		var payload []byte
		if err := rows.Scan(&q.channel, &q.key, &payload); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if err := json.Unmarshal(payload, &q.ev); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		out = append(out, q)
	}
	return out
}

// finish reports a run group the way the job queue does: inside a write
// transaction bound to the organization.
func (w *outboxWorld) finish(t *testing.T, r jobs.RunGroupResult) {
	t.Helper()
	if err := w.d.WithWriteTx(ctx, w.org, func(ctx context.Context) error { return w.outbox.RunGroupFinished(ctx, r) }); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func TestRunGroupFinishedQueuesOneMessagePerChannel(t *testing.T) {
	w := newOutboxWorld(t, "outbox-run")
	start := time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 1, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	end := start.Add(252 * time.Second)
	first := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	r := jobs.RunGroupResult{
		OrgID: w.org, GroupID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), FirstJobID: first, Trigger: jobs.TriggerSchedule, TriggerRef: &w.schedule,
		Status: jobs.StatusFailed, Jobs: 2, Hosts: 12, HostsChanged: 9, HostsFailed: 1, StartedAt: &start, FinishedAt: &end,
	}
	w.finish(t, r)
	w.finish(t, r) // a retried finish enqueues nothing new
	got := w.queued(t)
	// failure → A, B; change → B: B gets ONE message for both.
	if len(got) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("queued = %+v, want one message each for A and B", got)
	}
	for _, q := range got {
		if q.key != "run:"+r.GroupID.String() || q.ev.Kind != notify.EventRunFinished || q.ev.Run.Result != "failed" ||
			q.ev.Run.HostsOK != 11 || q.ev.Run.DurationSeconds != 252 || q.ev.Schedule.Name != "Nightly" || q.ev.Template.Name != "patch-all" ||
			q.ev.URL != "https://pulsix.example.test/o/outbox-run/runs/"+first.String() || len(q.ev.Categories) != 2 {
			t.Fatalf("queued = %+v", q)
		}
	}
}

func TestRunGroupFinishedFiltersByCategoryAndTrigger(t *testing.T) {
	w := newOutboxWorld(t, "outbox-filter")
	// A clean success: no rule listens to success.
	w.finish(t, jobs.RunGroupResult{OrgID: w.org, GroupID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Trigger: jobs.TriggerSchedule, TriggerRef: &w.schedule, Status: jobs.StatusSucceeded, Hosts: 1})
	// A manual run: schedules' rules do not apply.
	w.finish(t, jobs.RunGroupResult{OrgID: w.org, GroupID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Trigger: jobs.TriggerManual, Status: jobs.StatusFailed})
	if got := w.queued(t); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("queued = %+v, want nothing", got)
	}
	// A success with changes reaches B (change) only.
	w.finish(t, jobs.RunGroupResult{OrgID: w.org, GroupID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Trigger: jobs.TriggerSchedule, TriggerRef: &w.schedule, Status: jobs.StatusSucceeded, Hosts: 2, HostsChanged: 1})
	if got := w.queued(t); len(got) != 1 || got[0].channel != w.chB.ID || got[0].ev.Run.Result != "succeeded" {
		t.Fatalf("queued = %+v, want B only", got)
	}
}

func TestEnqueueDecisionSkipsDisabledChannelsAndTriggeredDecisions(t *testing.T) {
	w := newOutboxWorld(t, "outbox-decision")
	decide := func(outcome string, runID uuid.UUID) {
		t.Helper()
		tx, err := w.d.Pool().Begin(ctx)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		defer tx.Rollback(ctx) //nolint:errcheck
		if err := w.outbox.EnqueueDecision(ctx, tx, notify.Decision{
			OrgID: w.org, ScheduleID: w.schedule, RunID: runID, Outcome: outcome, Reason: "every targeted host is in a maintenance window",
			DueAt: time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 3, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC), DecidedAt: time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 3, 0, 1, 0, time.UTC),
		}); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	decide("triggered", uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()))
	if got := w.queued(t); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("a triggered decision queued %+v", got)
	}
	run := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	decide("skipped_maintenance", run)
	decide("skipped_maintenance", run) // idempotent per decision
	got := w.queued(t)
	if len(got) != 1 || got[0].channel != w.chA.ID || got[0].key != "decision:"+run.String() || got[0].ev.Kind != notify.EventScheduleSkipped ||
		got[0].ev.Decision.Outcome != "skipped_maintenance" || got[0].ev.URL != "https://pulsix.example.test/o/outbox-decision/schedules?id="+w.schedule.String() {
		t.Fatalf("queued = %+v, want one skipped message to A (the disabled channel gets none)", got)
	}
	decide("failed", uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()))
	if got := w.queued(t); len(got) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("a failed decision reaches the failure channels A and B, queued = %d", len(got))
	}
}

func TestOutboxIgnoresADeletedSchedule(t *testing.T) {
	w := newOutboxWorld(t, "outbox-gone")
	if _, err := w.d.Pool().Exec(ctx, `DELETE FROM schedules WHERE id = $1`, w.schedule); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	w.finish(t, jobs.RunGroupResult{OrgID: w.org, GroupID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Trigger: jobs.TriggerSchedule, TriggerRef: &w.schedule, Status: jobs.StatusFailed})
	if got := w.queued(t); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("queued = %+v", got)
	}
}

func TestScheduleRulesRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	w := newOutboxWorld(t, "outbox-rules")
	scoped := db.WithOrgScope(ctx, w.org)
	views, err := w.svc.ScheduleRules(scoped, w.org, w.schedule)
	if err != nil || len(views) != 3 || views[0].On != notify.CategoryFailure || len(views[0].Channels) != 2 || views[2].On != notify.CategorySkipped {
		t.Fatalf("rules = %+v, %v", views, err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.SetScheduleRules(scoped, w.org, w.schedule, []notify.Rule{{On: notify.CategorySuccess, Channels: []uuid.UUID{uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())}}}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("an unknown channel was accepted")
	}
	if views, err := w.svc.SetScheduleRules(scoped, w.org, w.schedule, nil); err != nil || len(views) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("clearing = %+v, %v", views, err)
	}
}
```

`internal/notify/rules.go`:

```go
package notify

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"slices"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// Rule is one entry of a schedule's notify_rules (spec §7): the channels
// told when a run or decision falls into the category.
type Rule struct {
	On       Category
	Channels []uuid.UUID
}

// ChannelRule is the stored, merged form: every category one channel is
// told about. One row per (schedule, channel) is what makes "at most one
// message per run and channel" hold by construction.
type ChannelRule struct {
	ChannelID   uuid.UUID
	ChannelName string
	ChannelKind Kind
	Events      []Category
}

// RuleView is a rule with its channels' names, for reading.
type RuleView struct {
	On       Category
	Channels []ChannelRef
}

// ChannelRef names a channel.
type ChannelRef struct {
	ID   uuid.UUID
	Name string
	Kind Kind
}

// RuleStore persists schedule rules (org-scoped).
type RuleStore interface {
	ScheduleRules(ctx context.Context, orgID, scheduleID uuid.UUID) ([]ChannelRule, error)
	ReplaceScheduleRules(ctx context.Context, orgID, scheduleID uuid.UUID, rules []ChannelRule) error
}

const maxRules = 16

// mergeRules folds per-category rules into one entry per channel, in the
// order channels first appear; categories are kept in canonical order.
func mergeRules(rules []Rule) ([]ChannelRule, error) {
	if len(rules) > maxRules {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: at most %d rules", ErrInvalidInput, maxRules)
	}
	byChannel := map[uuid.UUID]map[Category]bool{}
	var order []uuid.UUID
	for _, r := range rules {
		if _, ok := ParseCategory(string(r.On)); !ok {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: a rule's on must be failure, change, success or skipped", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
		if len(r.Channels) == 0 {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: the %s rule names no channel", ErrInvalidInput, r.On)
		}
		for _, ch := range r.Channels {
			if byChannel[ch] == nil {
				byChannel[ch] = map[Category]bool{}
				order = append(order, ch)
			}
			byChannel[ch][r.On] = true
		}
	}
	out := make([]ChannelRule, 0, len(order))
	for _, ch := range order {
		cr := ChannelRule{ChannelID: ch}
		for _, c := range Categories() {
			if byChannel[ch][c] {
				cr.Events = append(cr.Events, c)
			}
		}
		out = append(out, cr)
	}
	return out, nil
}

// splitRules turns the stored per-channel form back into per-category
// rules, in canonical category order; categories nobody listens to are
// left out.
func splitRules(stored []ChannelRule) []RuleView {
	var out []RuleView
	for _, c := range Categories() {
		v := RuleView{On: c}
		for _, cr := range stored {
			if slices.Contains(cr.Events, c) {
				v.Channels = append(v.Channels, ChannelRef{ID: cr.ChannelID, Name: cr.ChannelName, Kind: cr.ChannelKind})
			}
		}
		if len(v.Channels) > 0 {
			out = append(out, v)
		}
	}
	return out
}

// ScheduleRules reads a schedule's rules. The caller has checked the
// schedule exists and may be read.
func (s *Service) ScheduleRules(ctx context.Context, orgID, scheduleID uuid.UUID) ([]RuleView, error) {
	stored, err := s.d.Rules.ScheduleRules(ctx, orgID, scheduleID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return splitRules(stored), nil
}

// SetScheduleRules replaces a schedule's rules and returns them as
// stored. An unknown channel is ErrInvalidInput.
func (s *Service) SetScheduleRules(ctx context.Context, orgID, scheduleID uuid.UUID, rules []Rule) ([]RuleView, error) {
	merged, err := mergeRules(rules)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := s.d.Rules.ReplaceScheduleRules(ctx, orgID, scheduleID, merged); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return s.ScheduleRules(ctx, orgID, scheduleID)
}
```

`internal/notify/outbox.go` (Task 10 appends `EnqueueDigest`):

```go
package notify

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
)

// Outbox turns facts into queued deliveries inside the transaction that
// records the fact (a transactional outbox): a finished run group inside
// the job's terminal transaction, a schedule decision inside the ticker's
// row savepoint, the weekly digest inside the digest ticker's. Each event
// has a key — run:<group>, decision:<schedule run>, digest:<org>:<date> —
// and (org, key, channel) is unique, so a retried source never enqueues
// twice. Every write runs in its own savepoint: a notification failure
// rolls back only the notification, never the fact.
type Outbox struct {
	db        *db.DB
	publicURL string
	now       func() time.Time
	rand      io.Reader
	log       *slog.Logger
}

// NewOutbox wires the outbox; nil now and log take defaults. rand is the
// entropy for delivery ids.
func NewOutbox(d *db.DB, publicURL string, now func() time.Time, rand io.Reader, log *slog.Logger) *Outbox {
	if now == nil {
		now = time.Now
	}
	if log == nil {
		log = slog.Default()
	}
	return &Outbox{db: d, publicURL: strings.TrimRight(publicURL, "/"), now: now, rand: rand, log: log}
}

// Decision is a schedule decision the ticker recorded.
type Decision struct {
	OrgID, ScheduleID, RunID uuid.UUID
	Outcome, Reason          string
	DueAt, DecidedAt         time.Time
}

// RunGroupFinished implements jobs.RunGroupSink: a scheduled run group
// that finished is reported to the channels its schedule's rules name for
// the run's categories. ctx carries the finishing transaction.
func (o *Outbox) RunGroupFinished(ctx context.Context, r jobs.RunGroupResult) error {
	if r.Trigger != jobs.TriggerSchedule || r.TriggerRef == nil {
		return nil
	}
	result := "succeeded"
	switch r.Status {
	case jobs.StatusFailed, jobs.StatusError:
		result = "failed"
	case jobs.StatusCancelled:
		result = "cancelled"
	}
	sp, err := o.db.Begin(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("begin notification savepoint: %w", err)
	}
	return o.inSavepoint(ctx, sp, func(q *gen.Queries) error {
		return o.enqueueSchedule(ctx, q, r.OrgID, *r.TriggerRef, "run:"+r.GroupID.String(), runCategories(result, r.HostsChanged),
			func(sc gen.NotifyScheduleContextRow, cats []Category) Event {
				run := &RunSummary{
					ID: r.FirstJobID, Result: result, Hosts: r.Hosts, HostsOK: r.HostsOK(), HostsChanged: r.HostsChanged,
					HostsFailed: r.HostsFailed, StartedAt: r.StartedAt, FinishedAt: r.FinishedAt,
				}
				if r.StartedAt != nil && r.FinishedAt != nil {
					run.DurationSeconds = int64(r.FinishedAt.Sub(*r.StartedAt).Round(time.Second) / time.Second)
				}
				at := o.now()
				if r.FinishedAt != nil {
					at = *r.FinishedAt
				}
				return Event{
					Kind: EventRunFinished, At: at, Org: OrgRef{ID: r.OrgID, Slug: sc.OrgSlug, Name: sc.OrgName}, Categories: cats,
					Schedule: &NamedRef{ID: *r.TriggerRef, Name: sc.ScheduleName}, Template: &NamedRef{ID: sc.TemplateID, Name: sc.TemplateName},
					Run: run, URL: o.publicURL + "/o/" + sc.OrgSlug + "/runs/" + r.FirstJobID.String(),
				}
			})
	})
}

// EnqueueDecision reports a decision that started no run (skipped,
// missed, failed) inside tx, the ticker's row savepoint. A triggered
// decision is reported when its run group finishes, so it enqueues
// nothing here.
func (o *Outbox) EnqueueDecision(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, d Decision) error {
	cat, kind, ok := decisionCategory(d.Outcome)
	if !ok {
		return nil
	}
	sp, err := tx.Begin(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("begin notification savepoint: %w", err)
	}
	return o.inSavepoint(ctx, sp, func(q *gen.Queries) error {
		return o.enqueueSchedule(ctx, q, d.OrgID, d.ScheduleID, "decision:"+d.RunID.String(), []Category{cat},
			func(sc gen.NotifyScheduleContextRow, cats []Category) Event {
				return Event{
					Kind: kind, At: d.DecidedAt, Org: OrgRef{ID: d.OrgID, Slug: sc.OrgSlug, Name: sc.OrgName}, Categories: cats,
					Schedule: &NamedRef{ID: d.ScheduleID, Name: sc.ScheduleName}, Template: &NamedRef{ID: sc.TemplateID, Name: sc.TemplateName},
					Decision: &DecisionSummary{Outcome: d.Outcome, Reason: d.Reason, DueAt: d.DueAt},
					URL:      o.publicURL + "/o/" + sc.OrgSlug + "/schedules?id=" + d.ScheduleID.String(),
				}
			})
	})
}

// inSavepoint runs fn on sp and releases it, or rolls it back on error.
func (o *Outbox) inSavepoint(ctx context.Context, sp pgx.Tx, fn func(q *gen.Queries) error) error {
	if err := fn(gen.New(sp)); err != nil {
		_ = sp.Rollback(context.WithoutCancel(ctx))
		return err
	}
	if err := sp.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("release notification savepoint: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

// enqueueSchedule queues ev for every enabled channel the schedule's
// rules route any of cats to. A schedule deleted meanwhile (its rules go
// with it) enqueues nothing.
func (o *Outbox) enqueueSchedule(ctx context.Context, q *gen.Queries, orgID, scheduleID uuid.UUID, key string, cats []Category,
	build func(sc gen.NotifyScheduleContextRow, cats []Category) Event) error {
	names := make([]string, len(cats))
	for i, c := range cats {
		names[i] = string(c)
	}
	channels, err := q.NotifyRuleChannels(ctx, gen.NotifyRuleChannelsParams{OrgID: orgID, ScheduleID: scheduleID, Categories: names})
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("read notification rules: %w", err)
	}
	if len(channels) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	sc, err := q.NotifyScheduleContext(ctx, gen.NotifyScheduleContextParams{OrgID: orgID, ScheduleID: scheduleID})
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("read notification context: %w", err)
	}
	return o.insert(ctx, q, orgID, key, build(sc, cats), channels)
}

// insert queues one delivery per channel; the unique event key makes a
// repeated insert a no-op.
func (o *Outbox) insert(ctx context.Context, q *gen.Queries, orgID uuid.UUID, key string, ev Event, channels []uuid.UUID) error {
	payload, err := json.Marshal(ev)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("encode notification event: %w", err)
	}
	now := o.now()
	for _, ch := range channels {
		id, err := uuid.NewV7FromReader(o.rand)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("delivery id: %w", err)
		}
		if _, err := q.InsertNotificationDelivery(ctx, gen.InsertNotificationDeliveryParams{
			ID: id, OrgID: orgID, ChannelID: ch, EventKey: key, EventKind: string(ev.Kind), Payload: payload,
			Status: string(DeliveryPending), NextAttemptAt: now, CreatedAt: now,
		}); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("queue notification delivery: %w", err)
		}
	}
	return nil
}
```

```go
// SQLStore is the org-scoped notification store.
type SQLStore struct {
	d *db.DB
	q *gen.Queries
}

// NewSQLStore builds the store on *db.DB so every statement runs in the
// request's org scope.
func NewSQLStore(d *db.DB) *SQLStore { return &SQLStore{d: d, q: gen.New(d)} }
```

and add before `toChannel`:

```go
func (st *SQLStore) ScheduleRules(ctx context.Context, orgID, scheduleID uuid.UUID) ([]ChannelRule, error) {
	rows, err := st.q.ListScheduleNotifications(ctx, gen.ListScheduleNotificationsParams{OrgID: orgID, ScheduleID: scheduleID})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list schedule notifications: %w", err)
	}
	out := make([]ChannelRule, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		out[i] = ChannelRule{ChannelID: r.ChannelID, ChannelName: r.ChannelName, ChannelKind: Kind(r.ChannelKind)}
		for _, e := range r.Events {
			out[i].Events = append(out[i].Events, Category(e))
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

// ReplaceScheduleRules swaps a schedule's rules in one transaction. A
// foreign key violation means a channel that is not the organization's
// (the schedule's existence is the caller's check).
func (st *SQLStore) ReplaceScheduleRules(ctx context.Context, orgID, scheduleID uuid.UUID, rules []ChannelRule) error {
	tx, err := st.d.Begin(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("begin schedule notifications: %w", err)
	}
	defer tx.Rollback(context.WithoutCancel(ctx)) //nolint:errcheck // no-op once committed
	q := gen.New(tx)
	if err := q.DeleteScheduleNotifications(ctx, gen.DeleteScheduleNotificationsParams{OrgID: orgID, ScheduleID: scheduleID}); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("clear schedule notifications: %w", err)
	}
	for _, r := range rules {
		events := make([]string, len(r.Events))
		for i, e := range r.Events {
			events[i] = string(e)
		}
		err := q.InsertScheduleNotification(ctx, gen.InsertScheduleNotificationParams{OrgID: orgID, ScheduleID: scheduleID, ChannelID: r.ChannelID, Events: events})
		if db.IsForeignKeyViolation(err) {
			return fmt.Errorf("%w: unknown channel %s", ErrInvalidInput, r.ChannelID)
		}
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("store schedule notification: %w", err)
		}
	}
	if err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("commit schedule notifications: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}
```

`internal/schedules/decision_outbox_test.go`:

```go
package schedules_test

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/testdb"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/notify"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/schedules"
)

// recordingOutbox records every decision it is handed, checks it got the
// row's savepoint, and answers err.
type recordingOutbox struct {
	mu        sync.Mutex
	decisions []notify.Decision
	err       error
}

func (o *recordingOutbox) EnqueueDecision(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, d notify.Decision) error {
	o.mu.Lock()
	defer o.mu.Unlock()
	o.decisions = append(o.decisions, d)
	if tx == nil {
		return errors.New("no transaction")
	}
	return o.err
}

func TestTickerHandsEveryDecisionToTheOutbox(t *testing.T) {
	for _, outboxErr := range []error{nil, errors.New("outbox down")} {
		d := testdb.Open(t)
		org, templateID := seedTemplate(t, d, "sched-outbox-"+uuid.NewString()[:6])
		st := schedules.NewSQLStore(d)
		now := time.Date(2026, 9, 18, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
		dueAt := now.Add(-5 * time.Second)
		sch, err := st.Create(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org), schedules.Schedule{
			ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: org, TemplateID: templateID, Name: "outboxed", Mode: schedules.ModeCron, Cron: "0 * * * *", Timezone: "UTC",
			Guards: schedules.Guards{Overlap: schedules.OverlapSkip, CatchUp: schedules.CatchUp{Window: "1h"}}, NextRunAt: &dueAt,
		})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		quiet := slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil))
		runs := &fakeRuns{active: true} // overlap skip: a decision without a run
		svc := schedules.NewService(st, &fakeTemplates{}, runs, &fakeAudit{}, func() time.Time { return now }, rand.Reader, quiet)
		due := schedules.NewDueSQLStore(d, rand.Reader, quiet)
		outbox := &recordingOutbox{err: outboxErr}
		due.SetOutbox(outbox)
		if _, err := schedules.NewTicker(svc, due, 15*time.Second, func() time.Time { return now }, nil, quiet).Tick(ctx); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if len(outbox.decisions) != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("decisions = %+v", outbox.decisions)
		}
		got := outbox.decisions[0]
		if got.OrgID != org || got.ScheduleID != sch.ID || got.Outcome != "skipped_overlap" || got.RunID == uuid.Nil || !got.DueAt.Equal(dueAt) {
			t.Fatalf("decision = %+v", got)
		}
		// The decision is recorded and the schedule advanced even when the
		// outbox failed.
		runsList, err := st.Runs(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org), org, sch.ID, 10)
		if err != nil || len(runsList) != 1 || runsList[0].ID != got.RunID {
			t.Fatalf("outbox error %v: runs = %+v, %v", outboxErr, runsList, err)
		}
	}
}
```

```go
// DecisionOutbox queues the notifications of a recorded decision inside
// the row's savepoint (notify.Outbox), so the notification commits with
// the decision or not at all.
type DecisionOutbox interface {
	EnqueueDecision(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, d notify.Decision) error
}

// SetOutbox injects the decision outbox (spec 2026-09-18 §7); nil sends
// no decision notifications.
func (st *DueSQLStore) SetOutbox(o DecisionOutbox) { st.outbox = o }
```

```go
package api_test

import (
	"context"
	"net/http"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
)

// TestScheduleNotificationRules: rules round-trip in the spec's
// {on, channels} shape, a channel named by two rules gets one message per
// run, and a finished scheduled run queues it.
func TestScheduleNotificationRules(t *testing.T) {
	f := newRunFixture(t, "notify-rules")
	hook := map[string]any{"kind": "webhook", "name": "ops hook", "webhook": map[string]any{"url": "https://hooks.example.test/x"}}
	resp := postJSON(t, f.adminC, f.channelsURL(), hook)
	var ch struct {
		Channel channelOut `json:"channel"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &ch)
	resp = postJSON(t, f.operatorC, f.schedulesURL(), f.scheduleBody("Nightly"))
	var sch struct {
		Schedule struct {
			ID string `json:"id"`
		} `json:"schedule"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &sch)
	rulesURL := f.schedulesURL() + "/" + sch.Schedule.ID + "/notifications"

	body := map[string]any{"rules": []map[string]any{
		{"on": "failure", "channels": []string{ch.Channel.ID}},
		{"on": "change", "channels": []string{ch.Channel.ID}},
	}}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.viewerC, http.MethodPut, rulesURL, body); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("viewer put = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	resp = requestJSON(t, f.operatorC, http.MethodPut, rulesURL, body)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	var rules struct {
		Rules []struct {
			On       string `json:"on"`
			Channels []struct {
				ID   string `json:"id"`
				Name string `json:"name"`
			} `json:"channels"`
		} `json:"rules"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, f.viewerC, http.MethodGet, rulesURL, nil), &rules)
	if len(rules.Rules) != 2 || rules.Rules[0].On != "failure" || rules.Rules[1].On != "change" || rules.Rules[0].Channels[0].Name != "ops hook" {
		t.Fatalf("rules = %+v", rules)
	}
	unknown := map[string]any{"rules": []map[string]any{{"on": "failure", "channels": []string{uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()).String()}}}}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.operatorC, http.MethodPut, rulesURL, unknown); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("unknown channel = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.operatorC, http.MethodGet, f.schedulesURL()+"/"+uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()).String()+"/notifications", nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("unknown schedule = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}

	// A scheduled run that fails with a changed host queues ONE message.
	resp = postJSON(t, f.operatorC, f.schedulesURL()+"/"+sch.Schedule.ID+"/run", nil)
	var started struct {
		Jobs []struct {
			ID string `json:"id"`
		} `json:"jobs"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &started)
	jobID := uuid.MustParse(started.Jobs[0].ID)
	if _, err := f.s.DB.Pool().Exec(context.Background(), `UPDATE jobs SET status = 'running', started_at = now() WHERE id = $1`, jobID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := f.s.Queue.Finish(context.Background(), jobID, jobs.StatusFailed, map[string]any{"changed_hosts": []string{f.hostA.String()}}, "exited with code 2"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var log struct {
		Deliveries []struct {
			EventKind string `json:"event_kind"`
			Status    string `json:"status"`
		} `json:"deliveries"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, f.operatorC, http.MethodGet, f.channelsURL()+"/"+ch.Channel.ID+"/deliveries", nil), &log)
	if len(log.Deliveries) != 1 || log.Deliveries[0].EventKind != "run.finished" || log.Deliveries[0].Status != "pending" {
		t.Fatalf("deliveries = %+v, want one queued run.finished", log.Deliveries)
	}
}
```

`internal/api/notify_rules_handlers.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"context"

	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/notify"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/tenancy"
)

type notifyRuleBody struct {
	On       string   `json:"on" enum:"failure,change,success,skipped"`
	Channels []string `json:"channels" minItems:"1" maxItems:"20" doc:"Channel ids"`
}

// NotifyRulesBody replaces a schedule's notification rules.
type NotifyRulesBody struct {
	Rules []notifyRuleBody `json:"rules" maxItems:"16"`
}

type notifyRuleChannelDTO struct {
	ID   string `json:"id"`
	Name string `json:"name"`
	Kind string `json:"kind" enum:"email,webhook"`
}

type notifyRuleDTO struct {
	On       string                 `json:"on" enum:"failure,change,success,skipped"`
	Channels []notifyRuleChannelDTO `json:"channels"`
}

type notifyRulesOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Rules []notifyRuleDTO `json:"rules"`
	}
}

type notifyRulesInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	ID   string `path:"id"`
	Body NotifyRulesBody
}

// registerScheduleNotifyRoutes wires a schedule's notify_rules (spec
// §7) as a sub-resource: reading them needs templates.read like the
// schedule itself, replacing them templates.write. A run yields at most one
// message per channel however many rules name the channel.
func registerScheduleNotifyRoutes(hapi huma.API, deps Deps) {
	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}/notifications", func(ctx context.Context, in *scheduleIDInput) (*notifyRulesOutput, error) {
		scope, id, err := scheduleScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermTemplatesRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if _, err := deps.Schedules.Get(ctx, scope.OrgID, id); err != nil {
			return nil, mapSchedulesErr(ctx, err)
		}
		rules, err := deps.Notify.ScheduleRules(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapNotifyErr(ctx, err)
		}
		return notifyRulesOut(rules), nil
	})

	huma.Put(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}/notifications", func(ctx context.Context, in *notifyRulesInput) (*notifyRulesOutput, error) {
		scope, id, err := scheduleScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermTemplatesWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		sch, err := deps.Schedules.Get(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapSchedulesErr(ctx, err)
		}
		rules := make([]notify.Rule, len(in.Body.Rules))
		for i, r := range in.Body.Rules {
			rules[i].On = notify.Category(r.On)
			for _, raw := range r.Channels {
				ch, err := parseUUID(raw, "channel id")
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				rules[i].Channels = append(rules[i].Channels, ch)
			}
		}
		stored, err := deps.Notify.SetScheduleRules(ctx, scope.OrgID, id, rules)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapNotifyErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, id.String(), "updated the notifications of schedule "+sch.Name)
		return notifyRulesOut(stored), nil
	}, withAudit("setScheduleNotifications", "schedule"))
}

func notifyRulesOut(rules []notify.RuleView) *notifyRulesOutput {
	out := &notifyRulesOutput{}
	out.Body.Rules = make([]notifyRuleDTO, len(rules))
	for i, r := range rules {
		dto := notifyRuleDTO{On: string(r.On), Channels: make([]notifyRuleChannelDTO, len(r.Channels))}
		for j, c := range r.Channels {
			dto.Channels[j] = notifyRuleChannelDTO{ID: c.ID.String(), Name: c.Name, Kind: string(c.Kind)}
		}
		out.Body.Rules[i] = dto
	}
	return out
}
```
