```ts
export function useScheduleNotifications(org: string, scheduleId: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['schedule-notifications', org, scheduleId],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}/notifications', { params: { path: { org, id: scheduleId } } })),
    enabled: !!org && !!scheduleId,
  });
}

export type NotifyRuleInput = { on: 'failure' | 'change' | 'success' | 'skipped'; channels: string[] };

export function useSetScheduleNotifications(org: string, scheduleId: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (rules: NotifyRuleInput[]) =>
      unwrap(api.PUT('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}/notifications', { params: { path: { org, id: scheduleId } }, body: { rules } })),
    onSuccess: () => void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['schedule-notifications', org, scheduleId] }),
  });
}
```

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { ToastProvider } from '../../ui/toast.tsx';
import { mockApi, type MockRequest } from '../../test/mockApi.ts';
import { ScheduleNotifications } from './ScheduleNotifications.tsx';

const channels = {
  channels: [
    { id: 'c1', kind: 'webhook', name: 'ops hook', enabled: true, revision: 1, created_at: '', updated_at: '' },
    { id: 'c2', kind: 'email', name: 'ops mail', enabled: true, revision: 1, created_at: '', updated_at: '' },
  ],
  mail_available: true,
};
const rules = {
  rules: [
    { on: 'failure', channels: [{ id: 'c1', name: 'ops hook', kind: 'webhook' }, { id: 'c2', name: 'ops mail', kind: 'email' }] },
    { on: 'change', channels: [{ id: 'c1', name: 'ops hook', kind: 'webhook' }] },
  ],
};

function renderIt(canWrite: boolean) {
  const qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <ToastProvider>
        <ScheduleNotifications org="acme" scheduleId="s1" canWrite={canWrite} />
      </ToastProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

describe('ScheduleNotifications', () => {
  it('shows the rules as text to readers', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/schedules/s1/notifications': rules });
    renderIt(false);
    expect(await screen.findByText('→ ops hook, ops mail')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('on change')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Save notifications' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('edits the rules as a channel × category matrix', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    let put: unknown;
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/schedules/s1/notifications': rules,
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/notification-channels': channels,
      'PUT /api/v1/orgs/acme/schedules/s1/notifications': (req: MockRequest) => {
        put = req.body;
        return rules;
      },
    });
    renderIt(true);
    const hookChange = await screen.findByRole('checkbox', { name: 'ops hook on change' });
    expect(hookChange).toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'ops mail on failure' })).toBeChecked();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'ops mail on failure' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'ops mail skipped / missed' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save notifications' }));
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(put).toEqual({
        rules: [
          { on: 'failure', channels: ['c1'] },
          { on: 'change', channels: ['c1'] },
          { on: 'skipped', channels: ['c2'] },
        ],
      }),
    );
    expect(await screen.findByText('Notifications saved')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('points to the settings when the organization has no channel', async () => {
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/schedules/s1/notifications': { rules: [] },
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/notification-channels': { channels: [], mail_available: true },
    });
    renderIt(true);
    expect(await screen.findByText(/admins add them under Settings → Notifications/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save notifications' })).toBeDisabled();
  });
});
```

`web/src/features/schedules/ScheduleNotifications.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Pill, type Tone } from '../../ui/badge.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { type NotifyRuleInput, useChannels, useScheduleNotifications, useSetScheduleNotifications } from '../../api/hooks/useNotifications.ts';

type Category = NotifyRuleInput['on'];
const CATEGORIES: Category[] = ['failure', 'change', 'success', 'skipped'];
const LABEL: Record<Category, string> = { failure: 'on failure', change: 'on change', success: 'on success', skipped: 'skipped / missed' };
const TONE: Record<Category, Tone> = { failure: 'crit', change: 'warn', success: 'ok', skipped: 'unknown' };

type Matrix = Record<string, Category[]>;

/** ScheduleNotifications edits a schedule's notify rules (spec 2026-09-18
 * §7) as a channel × category matrix — the stored shape: each channel gets
 * at most one message per run, whichever categories it listens to.
 * Readers see the rules as text. */
export function ScheduleNotifications({ org, scheduleId, canWrite }: { org: string; scheduleId: string; canWrite: boolean }) {
  const rules = useScheduleNotifications(org, scheduleId);
  const channels = useChannels(org, canWrite);
  if (!rules.data) return null;
  const list = rules.data.rules ?? [];
  if (!canWrite || !channels.data) {
    return (
      <section aria-label="Notifications" className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-muted-foreground">Notifications</span>
        {list.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No notifications — runs appear in the activity feed only.</p>}
        {list.map((r) => (
          <div key={r.on} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
            <Pill tone={TONE[r.on as Category]}>{LABEL[r.on as Category]}</Pill>
            <span>→ {(r.channels ?? []).map((c) => c.name).join(', ')}</span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </section>
    );
  }
  const initial: Matrix = {};
  for (const r of list) for (const c of r.channels ?? []) initial[c.id] = [...(initial[c.id] ?? []), r.on as Category];
  return <RuleMatrix key={JSON.stringify(initial)} org={org} scheduleId={scheduleId} initial={initial} channels={channels.data.channels ?? []} />;
}

function RuleMatrix({ org, scheduleId, initial, channels }: { org: string; scheduleId: string; initial: Matrix; channels: { id: string; name: string }[] }) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const save = useSetScheduleNotifications(org, scheduleId);
  const [matrix, setMatrix] = useState<Matrix>(initial);

  function toggle(channel: string, cat: Category) {
    const cur = matrix[channel] ?? [];
    setMatrix({ ...matrix, [channel]: cur.includes(cat) ? cur.filter((c) => c !== cat) : [...cur, cat] });
  }

  async function onSave() {
    const body: NotifyRuleInput[] = CATEGORIES.map((on) => ({ on, channels: channels.filter((c) => (matrix[c.id] ?? []).includes(on)).map((c) => c.id) })).filter(
      (r) => r.channels.length > 0,
    );
    try {
      await save.mutateAsync(body);
      toast.show('Notifications saved');
    } catch (err) {
      toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Saving notifications failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  return (
    <section aria-label="Notifications" className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-muted-foreground">Notifications</span>
      {channels.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No channels yet — admins add them under Settings → Notifications.</p>
      ) : (
        <table className="text-xs">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th className="text-left font-medium text-muted-foreground">Channel</th>
              {CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
                <th key={c} className="px-1 font-medium">
                  <Pill tone={TONE[c]}>{LABEL[c]}</Pill>
                </th>
              ))}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {channels.map((ch) => (
              <tr key={ch.id}>
                <td className="truncate py-1">{ch.name}</td>
                {CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
                  <td key={c} className="text-center">
                    <input type="checkbox" aria-label={`${ch.name} ${LABEL[c]}`} checked={(matrix[ch.id] ?? []).includes(c)} onChange={() => toggle(ch.id, c)} />
                  </td>
                ))}
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      )}
      <Button type="button" className="self-end" onClick={() => void onSave()} disabled={save.isPending || channels.length === 0}>
        Save notifications
      </Button>
    </section>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Docs**

In `~/_dev/noix-docs/src/content/docs/pulsix/user/notifications.md`, insert before "## Who may do what":

```markdown
## Schedule notifications

Each schedule decides which channels hear about its runs, in the *Notifications*
part of the schedule's panel: a row per channel, a column per event.

| Event | When |
|---|---|
| **on failure** | a run failed, errored or was cancelled — or the schedule could not start its run at all |
| **on change** | at least one host reported a change |
| **on success** | a run succeeded |
| **skipped / missed** | the schedule skipped a run (a run was still active, or a maintenance window) or missed it (Pulsix was down) |

A run that belongs to several events — it failed *and* changed hosts — sends **one**
message per channel, listing every event it matched. A run that spans several zones
is still one run: its message is sent once, when the last zone has finished, with the
counts of all zones together.

Messages carry the schedule and template names, the result, the host counts
(targeted, ok, changed, failed), the duration and a link to the run — never run
output, variables or secrets. *Run now* runs are reported like scheduled ones.

Operators (and above) edit a schedule's notifications; everyone who can see the
schedule sees them.
```

```markdown
A schedule's notifications are a sub-resource: `GET` and `PUT`
`/schedules/{id}/notifications` read and replace
`{rules: [{on: "failure" | "change" | "success" | "skipped", channels: [ids]}]}`.
The response names each channel (`{id, name, kind}`). Reading needs the right to see
schedules, replacing them the right to edit schedules.
```

### Task 10: The weekly digest

This task covers spec §7's weekly digest per organization: channels, weekday, time and time zone. It covers runs per schedule, failures, skips and misses, and the slowest runs of the past 7 days. The digest has its own ticker in `notify` (every minute, `SKIP LOCKED` over `notification_digests`). It asks `schedules.Service.Digest` for the week's summary, queues the message through the outbox inside the row's savepoint, and advances to the next week. The key `digest:<occurrence>` makes it idempotent per organization and week. The settings live on the Notifications page.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/notify/digest.go`, `internal/notify/digest_ticker.go`; Test: `internal/notify/digest_test.go`, `internal/notify/digest_sql_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/notify/outbox.go` (`EnqueueDigest`), `internal/notify/service.go` (`Deps.Digests`), `internal/notify/store_sql.go` (`GetDigest`, `PutDigest`), `internal/db/queries/notifications.sql` (digest queries)
- Create: `internal/schedules/digest.go`; Test: `internal/schedules/digest_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/schedules/service.go` (`Store.RunsBetween`, `RunEnqueuer.GroupSummaries`, `Service.RunsBetween`), `internal/schedules/store_sql.go` (`RunsBetween`), `internal/db/queries/schedules.sql` (`ListScheduleRunsBetween`), the fakes in `fakes_test.go`, `decide_test.go`, `due_sql_test.go`
- Create: `internal/api/notify_digest_handlers.go`; Test: `internal/api/notify_digest_handlers_test.go`; Modify: `internal/api/api.go`, `internal/api/apitest/apitest.go`, `internal/app/app.go` (the digest ticker, `App.stopDigest`)
- Create: `web/src/features/notifications/DigestCard.tsx`; Modify: `web/src/api/hooks/useNotifications.ts`, `web/src/features/notifications/ChannelsPage.tsx` (+ test)
- Regenerate: `internal/db/gen`, `web/src/api/schema.d.ts`
- Docs (noix-docs): `user/notifications.md`, `reference/api.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5's `jobs.Service.GroupSummaries`, `jobs.GroupSummary.Duration`. Task 9's `Outbox.inSavepoint`, `Outbox.insert`. Task 2's `DigestSummary`, `DigestSchedule`, `DigestRun`.
- Produces (package `notify`):
  - `type DigestSettings struct{ Enabled bool; Weekday int /*0=Sunday*/; Time, Timezone string; Channels []ChannelRef; NextRunAt *time.Time }`, `type DigestInput struct{ Enabled bool; Weekday, Time, Timezone string; Channels []uuid.UUID }`
  - `type DigestStore interface{ GetDigest(ctx, orgID) (DigestSettings, bool, error); PutDigest(ctx, orgID, s DigestSettings, channels []uuid.UUID) error }`, `Deps.Digests DigestStore`
  - `Service.Digest(ctx, orgID) (DigestSettings, error)` (defaults: disabled, Monday 08:00 UTC), `Service.SetDigest(ctx, orgID, in DigestInput) (DigestSettings, error)`
  - `func NextDigestRun(weekday int, at, timezone string, now time.Time) (time.Time, error)`, `func WeekdayName(d int) string`
  - `type DueDigest struct{ OrgID uuid.UUID; Occurrence time.Time; Timezone string }`, `func (d DueDigest) Key() string`
  - `type DigestBuilder interface{ Digest(ctx, orgID uuid.UUID, from, to time.Time) (DigestSummary, error) }`, `type DigestEnqueuer`, `type DigestQueue`
  - `type DigestTicker`, `func NewDigestTicker(queue DigestQueue, builder DigestBuilder, outbox DigestEnqueuer, tick time.Duration, now func() time.Time, sleep func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error, log *slog.Logger) *DigestTicker`, `Run`, `Tick`; `const DefaultDigestTick = time.Minute`
  - `type DigestSQLStore`, `func NewDigestSQLStore(d *db.DB, log *slog.Logger) *DigestSQLStore`
  - `func (o *Outbox) EnqueueDigest(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, d DueDigest, sum DigestSummary) error`
- Produces (package `schedules`): `func (s *Service) Digest(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, from, to time.Time) (notify.DigestSummary, error)` (implements `notify.DigestBuilder`), `func (s *Service) RunsBetween(ctx, orgID uuid.UUID, from, to time.Time) ([]Run, error)` (Task 11's calendar), `Store.RunsBetween`, `RunEnqueuer.GroupSummaries`.
- Produces (HTTP): `GET /api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-digest` (channels.read) and `PUT` (channels.write, audited `updateNotificationDigest`, object `channel`), body `NotifyDigestBody{enabled, weekday, time, timezone, channels}`.
- Produces (SPA): `useDigest(org)`, `useSetDigest(org)`, `type DigestInput`.

- [ ] **Step 1: The queries**

Append to `internal/db/queries/notifications.sql`:

```sql
-- name: NotifyOrgContext :one
SELECT slug, name FROM organizations WHERE id = @org_id;

-- name: GetNotificationDigest :one
SELECT * FROM notification_digests WHERE org_id = @org_id;

-- name: ListNotificationDigestChannels :many
SELECT d.channel_id, c.name AS channel_name, c.kind AS channel_kind
FROM notification_digest_channels AS d
JOIN notification_channels AS c ON c.org_id = d.org_id AND c.id = d.channel_id
WHERE d.org_id = @org_id
ORDER BY c.name;

-- name: UpsertNotificationDigest :one
INSERT INTO notification_digests (org_id, enabled, weekday, at_time, timezone, next_run_at, updated_at)
VALUES (@org_id, @enabled, @weekday, @at_time, @timezone, sqlc.narg(next_run_at), now())
ON CONFLICT (org_id) DO UPDATE SET enabled = EXCLUDED.enabled, weekday = EXCLUDED.weekday,
  at_time = EXCLUDED.at_time, timezone = EXCLUDED.timezone, next_run_at = EXCLUDED.next_run_at, updated_at = now()
RETURNING *;

-- name: DeleteNotificationDigestChannels :exec
DELETE FROM notification_digest_channels WHERE org_id = @org_id;

-- name: InsertNotificationDigestChannel :exec
INSERT INTO notification_digest_channels (org_id, channel_id) VALUES (@org_id, @channel_id);

-- name: LockDueDigests :many
-- The digest ticker's due scan: raw pool, bypass policy, every org, SKIP
-- LOCKED like the schedule ticker.
SELECT * FROM notification_digests
WHERE enabled AND next_run_at <= @now::timestamptz
ORDER BY next_run_at
LIMIT @lim
FOR NO KEY UPDATE SKIP LOCKED;

-- name: DigestChannelsForSend :many
SELECT d.channel_id
FROM notification_digest_channels AS d
JOIN notification_channels AS c ON c.org_id = d.org_id AND c.id = d.channel_id
WHERE d.org_id = @org_id AND c.enabled
ORDER BY d.channel_id;

-- name: AdvanceNotificationDigest :exec
UPDATE notification_digests SET next_run_at = @next_run_at, last_period = @last_period
WHERE org_id = @org_id;
```

Append to `internal/db/queries/schedules.sql`:

```sql
-- name: ListScheduleRunsBetween :many
-- The calendar's past occurrences and the digest's week: every decision
-- whose due time falls in [from, to).
SELECT * FROM schedule_runs
WHERE org_id = @org_id AND due_at >= @from_at::timestamptz AND due_at < @to_at::timestamptz
ORDER BY due_at, id;
```

`internal/schedules/digest_test.go`:

```go
package schedules_test

import (
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/schedules"
)

func TestDigestSummarisesTheWeek(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	a, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, validInput(w.templateID))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	in := validInput(w.templateID)
	in.Name = "Quiet"
	quiet, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	from := time.Date(2026, 9, 21, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	to := from.AddDate(0, 0, 7)
	record := func(outcome schedules.Outcome, due time.Time, group *uuid.UUID) {
		t.Helper()
		if err := w.store.RecordRun(ctx, schedules.Run{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: w.org, ScheduleID: a.ID, DueAt: due, DecidedAt: due, Outcome: outcome, JobGroupID: group}); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	w.runs.summaries = map[uuid.UUID]jobs.GroupSummary{}
	var slow uuid.UUID
	for i, d := range []time.Duration{2 * time.Minute, 9 * time.Minute, 4 * time.Minute, 1 * time.Minute, 7 * time.Minute, 3 * time.Minute} {
		g := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
		start := from.Add(time.Duration(i) * time.Hour)
		end := start.Add(d)
		status := jobs.StatusSucceeded
		if i == 1 {
			status, slow = jobs.StatusFailed, g
		}
		w.runs.summaries[g] = jobs.GroupSummary{GroupID: g, FirstJobID: g, Status: status, StartedAt: &start, FinishedAt: &end}
		record(schedules.OutcomeTriggered, start, &g)
	}
	record(schedules.OutcomeSkippedMaintenance, from.Add(10*time.Hour), nil)
	record(schedules.OutcomeMissed, from.Add(11*time.Hour), nil)
	record(schedules.OutcomeFailed, from.Add(12*time.Hour), nil)
	record(schedules.OutcomeTriggered, to.Add(time.Hour), nil) // outside the week

	sum, err := w.svc.Digest(ctx, w.org, from, to)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(sum.Schedules) != 2 || !sum.From.Equal(from) || !sum.To.Equal(to) {
		t.Fatalf("summary = %+v", sum)
	}
	var got, idle = sum.Schedules[0], sum.Schedules[1]
	if got.ID != a.ID {
		got, idle = idle, got
	}
	if got.Runs != 6 || got.Failures != 2 || got.Skipped != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("schedule = %+v, want 6 runs, 2 failures (a failed run, a failed start), 2 skipped", got)
	}
	if idle.ID != quiet.ID || idle.Runs != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("idle schedule = %+v", idle)
	}
	if len(sum.Slowest) != 5 || sum.Slowest[0].RunID != slow || sum.Slowest[0].DurationSeconds != 540 || sum.Slowest[4].DurationSeconds != 120 {
		t.Fatalf("slowest = %+v", sum.Slowest)
	}
}
```

```go
func (f *fakeStore) RunsBetween(_ context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, from, to time.Time) ([]schedules.Run, error) {
```

`internal/schedules/digest.go`:

```go
package schedules

import (
	"context"
	"slices"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/notify"
)

// digestSlowest is how many of the week's slowest runs a digest lists.
const digestSlowest = 5

// Digest summarises [from, to) for the weekly digest (spec §7): per
// schedule the runs it started, its failures (runs that failed or were
// cancelled, and occurrences that could not start) and its skipped or
// missed occurrences; plus the slowest finished runs. It implements
// notify.DigestBuilder. Links are the outbox's business: a run is named by
// its first job.
func (s *Service) Digest(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, from, to time.Time) (notify.DigestSummary, error) {
	list, err := s.store.List(ctx, orgID, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return notify.DigestSummary{}, err
	}
	runs, err := s.store.RunsBetween(ctx, orgID, from, to)
	if err != nil {
		return notify.DigestSummary{}, err
	}
	var groups []uuid.UUID
	for _, r := range runs {
		if r.JobGroupID != nil {
			groups = append(groups, *r.JobGroupID)
		}
	}
	summaries, err := s.runs.GroupSummaries(ctx, orgID, groups)
	if err != nil {
		return notify.DigestSummary{}, err
	}
	per := map[uuid.UUID]*notify.DigestSchedule{}
	names := map[uuid.UUID]string{}
	out := notify.DigestSummary{From: from, To: to, Schedules: make([]notify.DigestSchedule, len(list)), Slowest: []notify.DigestRun{}}
	for i, sch := range list {
		out.Schedules[i] = notify.DigestSchedule{ID: sch.ID, Name: sch.Name}
		per[sch.ID], names[sch.ID] = &out.Schedules[i], sch.Name
	}
	for _, r := range runs {
		ds := per[r.ScheduleID]
		if ds == nil {
			continue
		}
		switch r.Outcome {
		case OutcomeTriggered:
			ds.Runs++
			if r.JobGroupID == nil {
				continue
			}
			g, ok := summaries[*r.JobGroupID]
			if !ok {
				continue
			}
			if g.Status == jobs.StatusFailed || g.Status == jobs.StatusCancelled {
				ds.Failures++
			}
			if d := g.Duration(); d > 0 {
				out.Slowest = append(out.Slowest, notify.DigestRun{
					Schedule: names[r.ScheduleID], RunID: g.FirstJobID, StartedAt: *g.StartedAt, DurationSeconds: int64(d.Round(time.Second) / time.Second),
				})
			}
		case OutcomeFailed:
			ds.Failures++
		case OutcomeSkippedOverlap, OutcomeSkippedMaintenance, OutcomeMissed:
			ds.Skipped++
		}
	}
	slices.SortFunc(out.Slowest, func(a, b notify.DigestRun) int { return int(b.DurationSeconds - a.DurationSeconds) })
	if len(out.Slowest) > digestSlowest {
		out.Slowest = out.Slowest[:digestSlowest]
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

```go
package notify_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/notify"
)

type fakeDigests struct {
	mu       sync.Mutex
	settings *notify.DigestSettings
	channels []uuid.UUID
}

func (f *fakeDigests) GetDigest(context.Context, uuid.UUID) (notify.DigestSettings, bool, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	if f.settings == nil {
		return notify.DigestSettings{}, false, nil
	}
	return *f.settings, true, nil
}

func (f *fakeDigests) PutDigest(_ context.Context, _ uuid.UUID, s notify.DigestSettings, channels []uuid.UUID) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.settings, f.channels = &s, channels
	return nil
}

func TestDigestSettings(t *testing.T) {
	store := &fakeDigests{}
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC) // a Tuesday
	svc := notify.NewService(notify.Deps{Digests: store, Now: func() time.Time { return now }})
	d, err := svc.Digest(ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()))
	if err != nil || d.Enabled || notify.WeekdayName(d.Weekday) != "mon" || d.Time != "08:00" || d.Timezone != "UTC" {
		t.Fatalf("default = %+v, %v", d, err)
	}
	ch := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	d, err = svc.SetDigest(ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), notify.DigestInput{Enabled: true, Weekday: "Mon", Time: "08:00", Timezone: "Europe/Berlin", Channels: []uuid.UUID{ch, ch}})
	if err != nil || !d.Enabled || d.Weekday != 1 || d.NextRunAt == nil {
		t.Fatalf("set = %+v, %v", d, err)
	}
	// Monday 28 Sep 08:00 CEST is 06:00 UTC.
	if !d.NextRunAt.Equal(time.Date(2026, 9, 28, 6, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)) || len(store.channels) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("next = %s, channels %v", d.NextRunAt, store.channels)
	}
	d, err = svc.SetDigest(ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), notify.DigestInput{Enabled: false, Weekday: "fri", Time: "17:30", Timezone: "UTC"})
	if err != nil || d.Enabled || d.NextRunAt != nil {
		t.Fatalf("disabled = %+v, %v", d, err)
	}
	for name, in := range map[string]notify.DigestInput{
		"bad weekday":     {Weekday: "funday", Time: "08:00", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"bad time":        {Weekday: "mon", Time: "8am", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"late time":       {Weekday: "mon", Time: "24:00", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"no timezone":     {Weekday: "mon", Time: "08:00"},
		"unknown zone":    {Weekday: "mon", Time: "08:00", Timezone: "Mars/Olympus"},
		"enabled, no ch":  {Enabled: true, Weekday: "mon", Time: "08:00", Timezone: "UTC"},
		"too many ch":     {Weekday: "mon", Time: "08:00", Timezone: "UTC", Channels: manyIDs(21)},
		"local time zone": {Weekday: "mon", Time: "08:00", Timezone: "Local"},
	} {
		if _, err := svc.SetDigest(ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), in); !errors.Is(err, notify.ErrInvalidInput) {
			t.Errorf("%s: err = %v", name, err)
		}
	}
}

func manyIDs(n int) []uuid.UUID {
	out := make([]uuid.UUID, n)
	for i := range out {
		out[i] = uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	}
	return out
}

type fakeDigestQueue struct {
	due     []notify.DueDigest
	handled map[uuid.UUID]error
}

func (f *fakeDigestQueue) ProcessDue(ctx context.Context, _ time.Time, _ int, handle func(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, d notify.DueDigest) error) (int, error) {
	f.handled = map[uuid.UUID]error{}
	for _, d := range f.due {
		f.handled[d.OrgID] = handle(ctx, nil, d)
	}
	return len(f.due), nil
}

type fakeBuilder struct{ err error }

func (b fakeBuilder) Digest(_ context.Context, _ uuid.UUID, from, to time.Time) (notify.DigestSummary, error) {
	return notify.DigestSummary{From: from, To: to}, b.err
}

type recordingDigestOutbox struct {
	sums map[uuid.UUID]notify.DigestSummary
}

func (o *recordingDigestOutbox) EnqueueDigest(_ context.Context, _ pgx.Tx, d notify.DueDigest, sum notify.DigestSummary) error {
	o.sums[d.OrgID] = sum
	return nil
}

func TestDigestTicker(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 28, 6, 0, 30, 0, time.UTC)
	onTime, late, broken := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	q := &fakeDigestQueue{due: []notify.DueDigest{
		{OrgID: onTime, Occurrence: now.Add(-30 * time.Second), Timezone: "Europe/Berlin"},
		{OrgID: late, Occurrence: now.Add(-49 * time.Hour), Timezone: "UTC"},
	}}
	outbox := &recordingDigestOutbox{sums: map[uuid.UUID]notify.DigestSummary{}}
	tk := notify.NewDigestTicker(q, fakeBuilder{}, outbox, time.Minute, func() time.Time { return now }, nil, quiet())
	if n, err := tk.Tick(ctx); err != nil || n != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("Tick = %d, %v", n, err)
	}
	sum, ok := outbox.sums[onTime]
	if !ok || sum.Timezone != "Europe/Berlin" || !sum.To.Equal(now.Add(-30*time.Second)) || !sum.From.Equal(sum.To.AddDate(0, 0, -7)) {
		t.Fatalf("on-time digest = %+v, %v", sum, ok)
	}
	if _, sent := outbox.sums[late]; sent || q.handled[late] != nil {
		t.Fatalf("a digest two days late must be skipped (and advanced), got sent=%v err=%v", sent, q.handled[late])
	}
	q.due = []notify.DueDigest{{OrgID: broken, Occurrence: now}}
	failing := notify.NewDigestTicker(q, fakeBuilder{err: errors.New("db down")}, outbox, time.Minute, func() time.Time { return now }, nil, quiet())
	if _, err := failing.Tick(ctx); err != nil || q.handled[broken] == nil {
		t.Fatalf("a builder failure must keep the digest due: handled %v", q.handled[broken])
	}
	if got := (notify.DueDigest{Occurrence: time.Date(2026, 9, 28, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.FixedZone("CEST", 7200))}).Key(); got != "digest:2026-09-28T06:00:00Z" {
		t.Fatalf("key = %s", got)
	}
}
```

```go
package notify_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/notify"
)

func TestDigestStoreTickAndOutbox(t *testing.T) {
	w := newOutboxWorld(t, "digest-flow")
	scoped := db.WithOrgScope(ctx, w.org)
	store := notify.NewSQLStore(w.d)
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	svc := notify.NewService(notify.Deps{Channels: store, Digests: store, Now: func() time.Time { return now }})
	set, err := svc.SetDigest(scoped, w.org, notify.DigestInput{Enabled: true, Weekday: "mon", Time: "08:00", Timezone: "UTC", Channels: []uuid.UUID{w.chA.ID, w.chDisabled.ID}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(set.Channels) != 2 || !set.NextRunAt.Equal(time.Date(2026, 9, 28, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)) {
		t.Fatalf("stored = %+v", set)
	}
	if _, err := svc.SetDigest(scoped, w.org, notify.DigestInput{Enabled: true, Weekday: "mon", Time: "08:00", Timezone: "UTC", Channels: []uuid.UUID{uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())}}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("an unknown channel was accepted")
	}
	// Not due yet.
	due := now.AddDate(0, 0, 6) // Monday 28 Sep 08:00
	ticker := func(at time.Time, b notify.DigestBuilder) *notify.DigestTicker {
		return notify.NewDigestTicker(notify.NewDigestSQLStore(w.d, quiet()), b, w.outbox, time.Minute, func() time.Time { return at }, nil, quiet())
	}
	week := notify.DigestSummary{Schedules: []notify.DigestSchedule{{Name: "Nightly", Runs: 2}}, Slowest: []notify.DigestRun{{Schedule: "Nightly", RunID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), DurationSeconds: 60}}}
	if n, _ := ticker(due.Add(-time.Minute), fixedBuilder{sum: week}).Tick(ctx); n != 0 {
		t.Fatal("a digest was due before its time")
	}
	if n, err := ticker(due.Add(30*time.Second), fixedBuilder{sum: week}).Tick(ctx); err != nil || n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("Tick = %d, %v", n, err)
	}
	got := w.queued(t)
	if len(got) != 1 || got[0].channel != w.chA.ID || got[0].key != "digest:2026-09-28T08:00:00Z" || got[0].ev.Kind != notify.EventDigest {
		t.Fatalf("queued = %+v, want one digest for the enabled channel A", got)
	}
	if got[0].ev.Digest.Timezone != "UTC" || got[0].ev.Digest.Slowest[0].URL != "https://pulsix.example.test/o/digest-flow/runs/"+week.Slowest[0].RunID.String() {
		t.Fatalf("digest = %+v", got[0].ev.Digest)
	}
	after, err := svc.Digest(scoped, w.org)
	if err != nil || !after.NextRunAt.Equal(time.Date(2026, 10, 5, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)) {
		t.Fatalf("next after the send = %+v, %v", after.NextRunAt, err)
	}
	var period string
	if err := w.d.Pool().QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT last_period FROM notification_digests WHERE org_id = $1`, w.org).Scan(&period); err != nil || period != "digest:2026-09-28T08:00:00Z" {
		t.Fatalf("last_period = %q, %v", period, err)
	}
	// A builder failure leaves the next digest due.
	if _, err := ticker(time.Date(2026, 10, 5, 8, 0, 30, 0, time.UTC), fixedBuilder{err: true}).Tick(ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if still, _ := svc.Digest(scoped, w.org); !still.NextRunAt.Equal(time.Date(2026, 10, 5, 8, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)) {
		t.Fatalf("a failed digest advanced to %s", still.NextRunAt)
	}
	// Settings that no longer evaluate disable the digest.
	if _, err := w.d.Pool().Exec(ctx, `UPDATE notification_digests SET timezone = 'Mars/Olympus' WHERE org_id = $1`, w.org); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := ticker(time.Date(2026, 10, 5, 8, 1, 0, 0, time.UTC), fixedBuilder{sum: week}).Tick(ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var enabled bool
	if err := w.d.Pool().QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT enabled FROM notification_digests WHERE org_id = $1`, w.org).Scan(&enabled); err != nil || enabled {
		t.Fatalf("enabled = %v, %v; want the broken digest disabled", enabled, err)
	}
}

var errBuild = errors.New("db down")

type fixedBuilder struct {
	sum notify.DigestSummary
	err bool
}

func (b fixedBuilder) Digest(context.Context, uuid.UUID, time.Time, time.Time) (notify.DigestSummary, error) {
	if b.err {
		return notify.DigestSummary{}, errBuild
	}
	return b.sum, nil
}
```

`internal/notify/digest.go`:

```go
package notify

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/cronexpr"
)

// maxDigestChannels bounds a digest's channel list.
const maxDigestChannels = 20

// weekdays are the digest's day names, Sunday first like cron.
func weekdays() []string { return []string{"sun", "mon", "tue", "wed", "thu", "fri", "sat"} }

// WeekdayName is the digest day name of a cron weekday (0 = Sunday).
func WeekdayName(d int) string { return weekdays()[d%7] }

// DigestSettings is an organization's weekly digest (spec §7).
type DigestSettings struct {
	Enabled bool
	// Weekday is 0 (Sunday) to 6, as in cron.
	Weekday   int
	Time      string // HH:MM in Timezone
	Timezone  string
	Channels  []ChannelRef
	NextRunAt *time.Time
}

// DigestInput is what an operator submits; Weekday is mon..sun.
type DigestInput struct {
	Enabled  bool
	Weekday  string
	Time     string
	Timezone string
	Channels []uuid.UUID
}

// DigestStore persists the digest settings (org-scoped).
type DigestStore interface {
	// GetDigest returns the settings; ok is false when none are stored.
	GetDigest(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (DigestSettings, bool, error)
	PutDigest(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, s DigestSettings, channels []uuid.UUID) error
}

// defaultDigest is what an organization without stored settings sees.
func defaultDigest() DigestSettings {
	return DigestSettings{Enabled: false, Weekday: 1, Time: "08:00", Timezone: "UTC"}
}

// digestExpr is the cron expression of a weekly digest.
func digestExpr(weekday int, at string) (cronexpr.Expr, error) {
	var h, m int
	if _, err := fmt.Sscanf(at, "%02d:%02d", &h, &m); err != nil || len(at) != 5 || h > 23 || m > 59 || h < 0 || m < 0 {
		return cronexpr.Expr{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: time must be HH:MM", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	return cronexpr.Parse(fmt.Sprintf("%d %d * * %d", m, h, weekday))
}

// NextDigestRun is the first digest occurrence strictly after now.
func NextDigestRun(weekday int, at, timezone string, now time.Time) (time.Time, error) {
	expr, err := digestExpr(weekday, at)
	if err != nil {
		return time.Time{}, err
	}
	loc, err := time.LoadLocation(timezone)
	if err != nil {
		return time.Time{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: unknown timezone %q", ErrInvalidInput, timezone)
	}
	return expr.Next(now, loc), nil
}

// Digest returns the organization's digest settings (defaults when none
// are stored).
func (s *Service) Digest(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (DigestSettings, error) {
	d, ok, err := s.d.Digests.GetDigest(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return DigestSettings{}, err
	}
	if !ok {
		return defaultDigest(), nil
	}
	return d, nil
}

// SetDigest validates and stores the settings; an enabled digest needs a
// channel and gets its next run computed from now.
func (s *Service) SetDigest(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, in DigestInput) (DigestSettings, error) {
	day := -1
	for i, n := range weekdays() {
		if strings.EqualFold(in.Weekday, n) {
			day = i
		}
	}
	if day < 0 {
		return DigestSettings{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: weekday must be mon, tue, wed, thu, fri, sat or sun", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	if in.Timezone == "" || in.Timezone == "Local" {
		return DigestSettings{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: timezone must be an IANA name such as Europe/Berlin or UTC", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	channels := make([]uuid.UUID, 0, len(in.Channels))
	seen := map[uuid.UUID]bool{}
	for _, ch := range in.Channels {
		if !seen[ch] {
			seen[ch] = true
			channels = append(channels, ch)
		}
	}
	if len(channels) > maxDigestChannels {
		return DigestSettings{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: at most %d channels", ErrInvalidInput, maxDigestChannels)
	}
	if in.Enabled && len(channels) == 0 {
		return DigestSettings{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: an enabled digest needs at least one channel", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	next, err := NextDigestRun(day, in.Time, in.Timezone, s.d.Now())
	if err != nil {
		return DigestSettings{}, err
	}
	settings := DigestSettings{Enabled: in.Enabled, Weekday: day, Time: in.Time, Timezone: in.Timezone}
	if in.Enabled {
		settings.NextRunAt = &next
	}
	if err := s.d.Digests.PutDigest(ctx, orgID, settings, channels); err != nil {
		return DigestSettings{}, err
	}
	return s.Digest(ctx, orgID)
}
```

`internal/notify/digest_ticker.go`:

```go
package notify

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"runtime/debug"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
)

// Digest ticker tuning: it looks for due digests every minute; a digest
// more than maxDigestLate overdue (the manager was down) is skipped, not
// sent days late, and the next week's is scheduled.
const (
	DefaultDigestTick = time.Minute
	maxDigestLate     = 24 * time.Hour
	digestBatch       = 20
)

// DueDigest is one organization's digest occurrence that is due.
type DueDigest struct {
	OrgID      uuid.UUID
	Occurrence time.Time
	Timezone   string
}

// Key is the digest's idempotency key: one digest per organization and
// occurrence however often the ticker retries.
func (d DueDigest) Key() string { return "digest:" + d.Occurrence.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339) }

// DigestBuilder computes a week's summary (schedules.Service).
type DigestBuilder interface {
	Digest(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, from, to time.Time) (DigestSummary, error)
}

// DigestEnqueuer queues a digest's deliveries inside tx (Outbox).
type DigestEnqueuer interface {
	EnqueueDigest(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, d DueDigest, sum DigestSummary) error
}

// DigestQueue locks due digests and advances each one whose handle
// succeeds (DigestSQLStore).
type DigestQueue interface {
	ProcessDue(ctx context.Context, now time.Time, limit int, handle func(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, d DueDigest) error) (int, error)
}

// DigestTicker sends weekly digests. Several managers may run one each:
// the due scan is FOR NO KEY UPDATE SKIP LOCKED, like the schedule
// ticker's, and the digest key dedupes what slips through.
type DigestTicker struct {
	queue   DigestQueue
	builder DigestBuilder
	outbox  DigestEnqueuer
	tick    time.Duration
	now     func() time.Time
	sleep   func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error
	log     *slog.Logger
}

// NewDigestTicker wires the ticker; zero tick, nil now, sleep and log take
// defaults.
func NewDigestTicker(queue DigestQueue, builder DigestBuilder, outbox DigestEnqueuer, tick time.Duration, now func() time.Time, sleep func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error, log *slog.Logger) *DigestTicker {
	if tick <= 0 {
		tick = DefaultDigestTick
	}
	if now == nil {
		now = time.Now
	}
	if sleep == nil {
		sleep = func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error {
			select {
			case <-ctx.Done():
				return ctx.Err()
			case <-time.After(d):
				return nil
			}
		}
	}
	if log == nil {
		log = slog.Default()
	}
	return &DigestTicker{queue: queue, builder: builder, outbox: outbox, tick: tick, now: now, sleep: sleep, log: log}
}

// Run ticks until ctx is cancelled.
func (t *DigestTicker) Run(ctx context.Context) {
	for {
		t.tickSafely(ctx)
		if err := t.sleep(ctx, t.tick); err != nil {
			return
		}
	}
}

func (t *DigestTicker) tickSafely(ctx context.Context) {
	defer func() {
		if r := recover(); r != nil {
			t.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "digest tick panicked", "panic", r, "stack", string(debug.Stack()))
		}
	}()
	if _, err := t.Tick(ctx); err != nil && ctx.Err() == nil {
		t.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "digest tick failed", "err", err)
	}
}

// Tick handles every due digest once.
func (t *DigestTicker) Tick(ctx context.Context) (int, error) {
	now := t.now()
	return t.queue.ProcessDue(ctx, now, digestBatch, func(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, d DueDigest) error {
		if now.Sub(d.Occurrence) > maxDigestLate {
			t.log.WarnContext(ctx, "weekly digest more than a day late; skipping it", "org", d.OrgID, "due", d.Occurrence)
			return nil
		}
		sum, err := t.builder.Digest(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, d.OrgID), d.OrgID, d.Occurrence.AddDate(0, 0, -7), d.Occurrence)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("build digest: %w", err)
		}
		sum.Timezone = d.Timezone
		return t.outbox.EnqueueDigest(ctx, tx, d, sum)
	})
}

// DigestSQLStore is the digest ticker's store: the raw pool, bypass
// policy, every statement names its org_id.
type DigestSQLStore struct {
	d   *db.DB
	log *slog.Logger
}

// NewDigestSQLStore builds the store; nil log is slog.Default.
func NewDigestSQLStore(d *db.DB, log *slog.Logger) *DigestSQLStore {
	if log == nil {
		log = slog.Default()
	}
	return &DigestSQLStore{d: d, log: log}
}

// ProcessDue locks due digests and runs handle for each inside its own
// savepoint. A digest whose handle succeeds is advanced to its next
// occurrence (computed from now, so a long outage never replays weeks);
// one whose handle fails stays due for the next tick.
func (st *DigestSQLStore) ProcessDue(ctx context.Context, now time.Time, limit int, handle func(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, d DueDigest) error) (int, error) {
	tx, err := st.d.Pool().Begin(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("begin digest tick: %w", err)
	}
	defer tx.Rollback(context.WithoutCancel(ctx))                                                       //nolint:errcheck // no-op once committed
	rows, err := gen.New(tx).LockDueDigests(ctx, gen.LockDueDigestsParams{Now: now, Lim: int32(limit)}) //nolint:gosec // limit is digestBatch
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("lock due digests: %w", err)
	}
	for _, r := range rows {
		st.processOne(ctx, tx, r, now, handle)
	}
	if err := tx.Commit(context.WithoutCancel(ctx)); err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("commit digest tick: %w", err)
	}
	return len(rows), nil
}

func (st *DigestSQLStore) processOne(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, r gen.NotificationDigest, now time.Time, handle func(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, d DueDigest) error) {
	due := DueDigest{OrgID: r.OrgID, Timezone: r.Timezone}
	if t := db.TimePtr(r.NextRunAt); t != nil {
		due.Occurrence = *t
	}
	sp, err := tx.Begin(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		st.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "begin digest savepoint", "org", r.OrgID, "err", err)
		return
	}
	if err := handle(ctx, sp, due); err != nil {
		_ = sp.Rollback(context.WithoutCancel(ctx))
		st.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "weekly digest failed; retrying next tick", "org", r.OrgID, "err", err)
		return
	}
	next, err := NextDigestRun(int(r.Weekday), r.AtTime, r.Timezone, now)
	if err != nil {
		// Stored settings that no longer evaluate stop the digest rather
		// than retry forever (the CHECK needs next_run_at while enabled, so
		// disable it).
		st.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "weekly digest settings do not evaluate; disabling the digest", "org", r.OrgID, "err", err)
		_, err = sp.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE notification_digests SET enabled = false, next_run_at = NULL WHERE org_id = $1`, r.OrgID)
	} else {
		err = gen.New(sp).AdvanceNotificationDigest(ctx, gen.AdvanceNotificationDigestParams{NextRunAt: db.NullTime(&next), LastPeriod: due.Key(), OrgID: r.OrgID})
	}
	if err != nil {
		_ = sp.Rollback(context.WithoutCancel(ctx))
		st.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "advance weekly digest", "org", r.OrgID, "err", err)
		return
	}
	if err := sp.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
		st.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "commit digest savepoint", "org", r.OrgID, "err", err)
	}
}
```

Append to `internal/notify/outbox.go`:

```go
// EnqueueDigest queues a weekly digest for the organization's enabled
// digest channels inside tx (the digest ticker's row savepoint).
func (o *Outbox) EnqueueDigest(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, d DueDigest, sum DigestSummary) error {
	sp, err := tx.Begin(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("begin notification savepoint: %w", err)
	}
	return o.inSavepoint(ctx, sp, func(q *gen.Queries) error {
		channels, err := q.DigestChannelsForSend(ctx, d.OrgID)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("read digest channels: %w", err)
		}
		if len(channels) == 0 {
			return nil
		}
		org, err := q.NotifyOrgContext(ctx, d.OrgID)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("read organization: %w", err)
		}
		base := o.publicURL + "/o/" + org.Slug
		for i := range sum.Slowest {
			sum.Slowest[i].URL = base + "/runs/" + sum.Slowest[i].RunID.String()
		}
		ev := Event{Kind: EventDigest, At: d.Occurrence, Org: OrgRef{ID: d.OrgID, Slug: org.Slug, Name: org.Name}, Digest: &sum, URL: base + "/schedules"}
		return o.insert(ctx, q, d.OrgID, d.Key(), ev, channels)
	})
}
```

In `internal/notify/service.go`, add `Digests DigestStore` to `Deps` after `Rules`. In `internal/notify/store_sql.go`, before `toChannel`:

```go
func (st *SQLStore) GetDigest(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (DigestSettings, bool, error) {
	row, err := st.q.GetNotificationDigest(ctx, orgID)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return DigestSettings{}, false, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return DigestSettings{}, false, fmt.Errorf("get notification digest: %w", err)
	}
	chans, err := st.q.ListNotificationDigestChannels(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return DigestSettings{}, false, fmt.Errorf("list digest channels: %w", err)
	}
	d := DigestSettings{Enabled: row.Enabled, Weekday: int(row.Weekday), Time: row.AtTime, Timezone: row.Timezone, NextRunAt: db.TimePtr(row.NextRunAt)}
	for _, c := range chans {
		d.Channels = append(d.Channels, ChannelRef{ID: c.ChannelID, Name: c.ChannelName, Kind: Kind(c.ChannelKind)})
	}
	return d, true, nil
}

// PutDigest upserts the settings and replaces the channel list in one
// transaction; an unknown channel is ErrInvalidInput.
func (st *SQLStore) PutDigest(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, s DigestSettings, channels []uuid.UUID) error {
	tx, err := st.d.Begin(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("begin notification digest: %w", err)
	}
	defer tx.Rollback(context.WithoutCancel(ctx)) //nolint:errcheck // no-op once committed
	q := gen.New(tx)
	if _, err := q.UpsertNotificationDigest(ctx, gen.UpsertNotificationDigestParams{
		OrgID: orgID, Enabled: s.Enabled, Weekday: int16(s.Weekday), AtTime: s.Time, Timezone: s.Timezone, //nolint:gosec // 0-6
		NextRunAt: db.NullTime(s.NextRunAt),
	}); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("store notification digest: %w", err)
	}
	if err := q.DeleteNotificationDigestChannels(ctx, orgID); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("clear digest channels: %w", err)
	}
	for _, ch := range channels {
		err := q.InsertNotificationDigestChannel(ctx, gen.InsertNotificationDigestChannelParams{OrgID: orgID, ChannelID: ch})
		if db.IsForeignKeyViolation(err) {
			return fmt.Errorf("%w: unknown channel %s", ErrInvalidInput, ch)
		}
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("store digest channel: %w", err)
		}
	}
	if err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("commit notification digest: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}
```

```go
package api_test

import (

func TestNotificationDigestSettings(t *testing.T) {
	f := newRunFixture(t, "notify-digest")
	url := f.s.HTTP.URL + "/api/v1/orgs/" + f.orgSlug + "/notification-digest"
	var got struct {
		Digest struct {
			Enabled   bool    `json:"enabled"`
			Weekday   string  `json:"weekday"`
			Time      string  `json:"time"`
			NextRunAt *string `json:"next_run_at"`
			Channels  []struct {
				Name string `json:"name"`
			} `json:"channels"`
		} `json:"digest"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, f.operatorC, http.MethodGet, url, nil), &got)
	if got.Digest.Enabled || got.Digest.Weekday != "mon" || got.Digest.Time != "08:00" {
		t.Fatalf("default = %+v", got.Digest)
	}
	resp := postJSON(t, f.adminC, f.channelsURL(), map[string]any{"kind": "webhook", "name": "ops hook", "webhook": map[string]any{"url": "https://hooks.example.test/x"}})
	var ch struct {
		Channel channelOut `json:"channel"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &ch)
	body := map[string]any{"enabled": true, "weekday": "fri", "time": "16:30", "timezone": "Europe/Berlin", "channels": []string{ch.Channel.ID}}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.operatorC, http.MethodPut, url, body); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("operator put = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, f.adminC, http.MethodPut, url, body), &got)
	if !got.Digest.Enabled || got.Digest.Weekday != "fri" || got.Digest.NextRunAt == nil || len(got.Digest.Channels) != 1 || got.Digest.Channels[0].Name != "ops hook" {
		t.Fatalf("stored = %+v", got.Digest)
	}
	body["channels"] = []string{}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.adminC, http.MethodPut, url, body); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("enabled without channels = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	body["time"] = "4pm"
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.adminC, http.MethodPut, url, body); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusUnprocessableEntity {
		t.Fatalf("bad time = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}
```

`internal/api/notify_digest_handlers.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"time"

	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"
	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/notify"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/tenancy"
)

type notifyDigestDTO struct {
	Enabled   bool                   `json:"enabled"`
	Weekday   string                 `json:"weekday" enum:"sun,mon,tue,wed,thu,fri,sat"`
	Time      string                 `json:"time" doc:"HH:MM in timezone"`
	Timezone  string                 `json:"timezone"`
	Channels  []notifyRuleChannelDTO `json:"channels"`
	NextRunAt *time.Time             `json:"next_run_at,omitempty"`
}

// NotifyDigestBody replaces the organization's weekly digest settings.
type NotifyDigestBody struct {
	Enabled  bool     `json:"enabled"`
	Weekday  string   `json:"weekday" enum:"sun,mon,tue,wed,thu,fri,sat"`
	Time     string   `json:"time" pattern:"^[0-2][0-9]:[0-5][0-9]$"`
	Timezone string   `json:"timezone" minLength:"1" maxLength:"64"`
	Channels []string `json:"channels" maxItems:"20"`
}

type notifyDigestOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Digest notifyDigestDTO `json:"digest"`
	}
}

// registerNotifyDigestRoutes wires the weekly digest (spec §7):
// channels.read to see it, channels.write to change it.
func registerNotifyDigestRoutes(hapi huma.API, deps Deps) {
	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-digest", func(ctx context.Context, in *struct {
		Org string `path:"org"`
	}) (*notifyDigestOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermChannelsRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		d, err := deps.Notify.Digest(ctx, scope.OrgID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapNotifyErr(ctx, err)
		}
		return notifyDigestOut(d), nil
	})

	huma.Put(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-digest", func(ctx context.Context, in *struct {
		Org  string `path:"org"`
		Body NotifyDigestBody
	}) (*notifyDigestOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermChannelsWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		channels := make([]uuid.UUID, len(in.Body.Channels))
		for i, raw := range in.Body.Channels {
			if channels[i], err = parseUUID(raw, "channel id"); err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
		}
		d, err := deps.Notify.SetDigest(ctx, scope.OrgID, notify.DigestInput{
			Enabled: in.Body.Enabled, Weekday: in.Body.Weekday, Time: in.Body.Time, Timezone: in.Body.Timezone, Channels: channels,
		})
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapNotifyErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, "", "updated the weekly digest")
		return notifyDigestOut(d), nil
	}, withAudit("updateNotificationDigest", "channel"))
}

func notifyDigestOut(d notify.DigestSettings) *notifyDigestOutput {
	out := &notifyDigestOutput{}
	out.Body.Digest = notifyDigestDTO{
		Enabled: d.Enabled, Weekday: notify.WeekdayName(d.Weekday), Time: d.Time, Timezone: d.Timezone,
		Channels: make([]notifyRuleChannelDTO, len(d.Channels)), NextRunAt: d.NextRunAt,
	}
	for i, c := range d.Channels {
		out.Body.Digest.Channels[i] = notifyRuleChannelDTO{ID: c.ID.String(), Name: c.Name, Kind: string(c.Kind)}
	}
	return out
}
```

```ts
export function useDigest(org: string, enabled = true) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['notification-digest', org],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-digest', { params: { path: { org } } })),
    enabled: !!org && enabled,
  });
}

export type DigestInput = { enabled: boolean; weekday: 'sun' | 'mon' | 'tue' | 'wed' | 'thu' | 'fri' | 'sat'; time: string; timezone: string; channels: string[] };

export function useSetDigest(org: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: DigestInput) => unwrap(api.PUT('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/notification-digest', { params: { path: { org } }, body })),
    onSuccess: () => void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['notification-digest', org] }),
  });
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '../../ui/input.tsx';
import { Label } from '../../ui/label.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { type DigestInput, useDigest, useSetDigest } from '../../api/hooks/useNotifications.ts';
import type { NotifyChannel } from '../../api/types.ts';

const SELECT = 'h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground';
const DAYS: DigestInput['weekday'][] = ['mon', 'tue', 'wed', 'thu', 'fri', 'sat', 'sun'];
const DAY_LABEL: Record<DigestInput['weekday'], string> = { mon: 'Monday', tue: 'Tuesday', wed: 'Wednesday', thu: 'Thursday', fri: 'Friday', sat: 'Saturday', sun: 'Sunday' };

/** DigestCard is the weekly digest (spec 2026-09-18 §7): runs per
 * schedule, failures, skips and misses, and the slowest runs of the past
 * seven days, sent to the chosen channels on one weekday. */
export function DigestCard({ org, channels, canWrite }: { org: string; channels: NotifyChannel[]; canWrite: boolean }) {
  const digest = useDigest(org);
  if (!digest.data) return null;
  const d = digest.data.digest;
  return <DigestForm key={JSON.stringify(d)} org={org} channels={channels} canWrite={canWrite} initial={d} />;
}

interface DigestFormProps {
  org: string;
  channels: NotifyChannel[];
  canWrite: boolean;
  initial: { enabled: boolean; weekday: DigestInput['weekday']; time: string; timezone: string; channels: { id: string }[] | null; next_run_at?: string };
}

function DigestForm({ org, channels, canWrite, initial }: DigestFormProps) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const save = useSetDigest(org);
  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(initial.enabled);
  const [weekday, setWeekday] = useState(initial.weekday);
  const [time, setTime] = useState(initial.time);
  const [timezone, setTimezone] = useState(initial.timezone);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string[]>((initial.channels ?? []).map((c) => c.id));

  async function onSave() {
    try {
      await save.mutateAsync({ enabled, weekday, time, timezone, channels: selected });
      toast.show('Weekly digest saved');
    } catch (err) {
      toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Saving the digest failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  return (
    <section aria-label="Weekly digest" className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="text-[15px] font-semibold">Weekly digest</h2>
        {initial.next_run_at && <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">next: {new Date(initial.next_run_at).toLocaleString()}</span>}
      </div>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Runs per schedule, failures, skips and misses, and the slowest runs of the past seven days.</p>
      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
        <input type="checkbox" checked={enabled} onChange={(e) => setEnabled(e.target.checked)} disabled={!canWrite} />
        Send the weekly digest
      </label>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="digest-day">Day</Label>
          <select id="digest-day" className={SELECT} value={weekday} onChange={(e) => setWeekday(e.target.value as DigestInput['weekday'])} disabled={!canWrite}>
            {DAYS.map((d) => (
              <option key={d} value={d}>
                {DAY_LABEL[d]}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="digest-time">Time</Label>
          <Input id="digest-time" type="time" value={time} onChange={(e) => setTime(e.target.value)} disabled={!canWrite} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="digest-tz">Timezone</Label>
          <Input id="digest-tz" className="font-mono" value={timezone} onChange={(e) => setTimezone(e.target.value)} disabled={!canWrite} />
        </div>
      </div>
      <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-muted-foreground">Send to</span>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        {channels.map((c) => (
          <label key={c.id} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={selected.includes(c.id)}
              onChange={() => setSelected(selected.includes(c.id) ? selected.filter((x) => x !== c.id) : [...selected, c.id])}
              disabled={!canWrite}
            />
            {c.name}
          </label>
        ))}
        {channels.length === 0 && <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Add a channel first.</span>}
      </div>
      {canWrite && (
        <Button type="button" className="self-end" onClick={() => void onSave()} disabled={save.isPending}>
          Save digest
        </Button>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Docs**

In `~/_dev/noix-docs/src/content/docs/pulsix/user/notifications.md`, insert before "## Who may do what":

```markdown
## Weekly digest

The weekly digest summarizes the organization's schedules once a week: for every
schedule, how many runs it started, how many failed (including occurrences that
could not start) and how many were skipped or missed, plus the five slowest runs of
the past seven days with links. Set it up at the bottom of *Settings →
Notifications*: the day, the time and time zone, and the channels it goes to.

If Pulsix was down at the digest's time, it sends the digest when it is back, as
long as that is within a day; a digest more than a day late is skipped, and the next
one follows on schedule. Admins change the digest; operators see it.
```

In `reference/api.md`, append to the "Notification channels" section:

```markdown
`GET` and `PUT` `/api/v1/orgs/{slug}/notification-digest` read and replace the weekly
digest: `{enabled, weekday: "mon" … "sun", time: "HH:MM", timezone, channels: [ids]}`.
The response adds each channel's name and `next_run_at`. An enabled digest needs at
least one channel. Operators can read it; admins change it.
```

### Task 11: The calendar, the runs list filters and the activity types

This task covers spec §8. The calendar API computes occurrences server-side. Past occurrences carry their `schedule_runs` outcome and their run's status, future ones come from each active schedule's timetable, and maintenance windows come with them. The range is at most six weeks. The SPA draws the mockup's week grid under the schedules list, plus a month grid and a list. The runs page filters by trigger and schedule, and the activity page offers the automation object types.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/calendar/calendar.go`; Test: `internal/calendar/calendar_test.go`
- Create: `internal/api/calendar_handlers.go`; Test: `internal/api/calendar_handlers_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/api/api.go` (`Deps.Calendar`, `validate`, route), `internal/api/api_test.go`, `internal/api/apitest/apitest.go`, `internal/app/app.go`, `scripts/coverage-gate.sh` (add `internal/calendar` to the 90 % list)
- Create: `web/src/api/hooks/useCalendar.ts`, `web/src/features/calendar/{calendar.ts,ScheduleCalendar.tsx}`; Test: `web/src/features/calendar/ScheduleCalendar.test.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/api/types.ts`, `web/src/features/schedules/SchedulesPage.tsx`, `web/src/api/hooks/useJobs.ts`, `web/src/features/runs/RunsPage.tsx` (+ test), `web/src/features/runs/RunDetailPage.tsx`, `web/src/ui/shell.tsx`, `web/src/router.tsx`, `web/src/features/activity/ActivityPage.tsx` (+ test)

- Produces (package `calendar`):
  - `const MaxRange = 42 * 24 * time.Hour`, `const StatePlanned = "planned"`, `var ErrInvalidRange`

- Produces (HTTP): `GET /api/v1/orgs/{org}/calendar?from=<RFC 3339>&to=<RFC 3339>` (templates.read) → `{from, to, entries: [CalendarEntryDTO], maintenance: [CalendarWindowDTO], truncated}`; 400 for unparsable times, an empty or reversed range, or more than 42 days.

```go
package calendar_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/calendar"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/maintenance"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/schedules"
)

var ctx = context.Background()

type fakeSchedules struct {
	list    []schedules.Schedule
	runs    []schedules.Run
	listErr error
	runsErr error
}

func (f fakeSchedules) List(context.Context, uuid.UUID, *uuid.UUID) ([]schedules.Schedule, map[uuid.UUID]schedules.Run, error) {
	return f.list, nil, f.listErr
}

func (f fakeSchedules) RunsBetween(_ context.Context, _ uuid.UUID, from, to time.Time) ([]schedules.Run, error) {
	var out []schedules.Run
	for _, r := range f.runs {
		if !r.DueAt.Before(from) && r.DueAt.Before(to) {
			out = append(out, r)
		}
	}
	return out, f.runsErr
}

type fakeGroups struct {
	sums map[uuid.UUID]jobs.GroupSummary
	err  error
}

func (f fakeGroups) GroupSummaries(context.Context, uuid.UUID, []uuid.UUID) (map[uuid.UUID]jobs.GroupSummary, error) {
	return f.sums, f.err
}

type fakeWindows struct {
	occ []maintenance.Occurrence
	err error
}

func (f fakeWindows) OccurrencesBetween(context.Context, uuid.UUID, time.Time, time.Time) ([]maintenance.Occurrence, error) {
	return f.occ, f.err
}

func TestRangeCombinesPastPlannedAndMaintenance(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 23, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC) // Wednesday noon
	from, to := time.Date(2026, 9, 21, 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC), time.Date(2026, 9, 28, 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	nightly := schedules.Schedule{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Name: "Nightly", TemplateID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Cron: "0 3 * * *", Timezone: "UTC"}
	paused := schedules.Schedule{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Name: "Paused", Cron: "0 * * * *", Timezone: "UTC", Paused: true}
	group := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	firstJob := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	src := fakeSchedules{
		list: []schedules.Schedule{nightly, paused},
		runs: []schedules.Run{
			{ScheduleID: nightly.ID, DueAt: time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 3, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC), Outcome: schedules.OutcomeTriggered, JobGroupID: &group},
			{ScheduleID: nightly.ID, DueAt: time.Date(2026, 9, 23, 3, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC), Outcome: schedules.OutcomeSkippedMaintenance, Reason: "in a window"},
			{ScheduleID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), DueAt: time.Date(2026, 9, 23, 4, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC), Outcome: schedules.OutcomeTriggered}, // deleted schedule
		},
	}
	window := maintenance.Occurrence{Name: "db upgrade", Start: time.Date(2026, 9, 23, 2, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC), End: time.Date(2026, 9, 23, 4, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)}
	svc := calendar.NewService(src, fakeGroups{sums: map[uuid.UUID]jobs.GroupSummary{group: {GroupID: group, FirstJobID: firstJob, Status: jobs.StatusFailed}}},
		fakeWindows{occ: []maintenance.Occurrence{window}}, func() time.Time { return now })
	v, err := svc.Range(ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), from, to)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// 2 past decisions + planned 24th, 25th, 26th and 27th at 03:00.
	if len(v.Entries) != 6 || v.Truncated || len(v.Maintenance) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("view = %+v", v)
	}
	past := v.Entries[0]
	if past.State != "triggered" || past.JobID == nil || *past.JobID != firstJob || past.JobStatus != "failed" || past.ScheduleName != "Nightly" {
		t.Fatalf("past = %+v", past)
	}
	if v.Entries[1].State != "skipped_maintenance" || v.Entries[1].Reason != "in a window" {
		t.Fatalf("skipped = %+v", v.Entries[1])
	}
	if p := v.Entries[2]; p.State != calendar.StatePlanned || !p.At.Equal(time.Date(2026, 9, 24, 3, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)) || p.TemplateID != nightly.TemplateID {
		t.Fatalf("planned = %+v", p)
	}
}

func TestRangeIncludesAnOccurrenceExactlyAtFrom(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 20, 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	from := time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 3, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	sch := schedules.Schedule{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Name: "Nightly", Cron: "0 3 * * *", Timezone: "UTC"}
	svc := calendar.NewService(fakeSchedules{list: []schedules.Schedule{sch}}, fakeGroups{}, fakeWindows{}, func() time.Time { return now })
	v, err := svc.Range(ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), from, from.Add(24*time.Hour))
	if err != nil || len(v.Entries) != 1 || !v.Entries[0].At.Equal(from) {
		t.Fatalf("entries = %+v, %v", v.Entries, err)
	}
}

func TestRangeTruncatesFrequentSchedules(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 20, 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	every := schedules.Schedule{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Name: "Every minute", Cron: "* * * * *", Timezone: "UTC"}
	broken := schedules.Schedule{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Name: "Broken", Cron: "nope", Timezone: "UTC"}
	badZone := schedules.Schedule{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Name: "Bad zone", Cron: "@daily", Timezone: "Mars/Olympus"}
	svc := calendar.NewService(fakeSchedules{list: []schedules.Schedule{every, broken, badZone}}, fakeGroups{}, fakeWindows{}, func() time.Time { return now })
	v, err := svc.Range(ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), now, now.Add(7*24*time.Hour))
	if err != nil || len(v.Entries) != 500 || !v.Truncated {
		t.Fatalf("entries = %d, truncated %v, %v", len(v.Entries), v.Truncated, err)
	}
	many := make([]schedules.Schedule, 12)
	for i := range many {
		many[i] = schedules.Schedule{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Name: "m", Cron: "* * * * *", Timezone: "UTC"}
	}
	v, err = calendar.NewService(fakeSchedules{list: many}, fakeGroups{}, fakeWindows{}, func() time.Time { return now }).Range(ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), now, now.Add(7*24*time.Hour))
	if err != nil || len(v.Entries) != 5000 || !v.Truncated {
		t.Fatalf("entries = %d, truncated %v, %v", len(v.Entries), v.Truncated, err)
	}
}

func TestRangeValidationAndErrors(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 20, 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	ok := calendar.NewService(fakeSchedules{}, fakeGroups{}, fakeWindows{}, nil)
	for _, r := range [][2]time.Time{{now, now}, {now, now.Add(-time.Hour)}, {now, now.Add(43 * 24 * time.Hour)}} {
		if _, err := ok.Range(ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), r[0], r[1]); !errors.Is(err, calendar.ErrInvalidRange) {
			t.Errorf("range %v = %v", r, err)
		}
	}
	boom := errors.New("db down")
	for name, svc := range map[string]*calendar.Service{
		"list":    calendar.NewService(fakeSchedules{listErr: boom}, fakeGroups{}, fakeWindows{}, nil),
		"runs":    calendar.NewService(fakeSchedules{runsErr: boom}, fakeGroups{}, fakeWindows{}, nil),
		"groups":  calendar.NewService(fakeSchedules{}, fakeGroups{err: boom}, fakeWindows{}, nil),
		"windows": calendar.NewService(fakeSchedules{}, fakeGroups{}, fakeWindows{err: boom}, nil),
	} {
		if _, err := svc.Range(ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), now, now.Add(time.Hour)); !errors.Is(err, boom) {
			t.Errorf("%s failure = %v", name, err)
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The calendar**

`internal/calendar/calendar.go`:

```go
// Package calendar lays scheduled runs and maintenance windows out over
// a time range (spec 2026-09-18 §8): future occurrences computed from each
// active schedule's timetable, past ones from the recorded decisions with
// their run's status, and every maintenance window's stretches.
package calendar

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/cronexpr"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/maintenance"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/schedules"
)

// Bounds: a range is at most six weeks (spec §8); an every-minute
// schedule over six weeks would be 60 000 occurrences, so each schedule
// contributes at most maxPerSchedule planned ones and the whole view at
// most maxEntries — Truncated says when either cut applied.
const (
	MaxRange       = 42 * 24 * time.Hour
	maxPerSchedule = 500
	maxEntries     = 5000
)

// ErrInvalidRange is a from/to pair that is empty, reversed or too long.
var ErrInvalidRange = errors.New("invalid range")

// StatePlanned is an occurrence still ahead; past entries carry their
// decision's outcome instead.
const StatePlanned = "planned"

// ScheduleSource is the slice of schedules.Service the calendar reads.
type ScheduleSource interface {
	List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, templateID *uuid.UUID) ([]schedules.Schedule, map[uuid.UUID]schedules.Run, error)
	RunsBetween(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, from, to time.Time) ([]schedules.Run, error)
}

// RunGroups summarises run groups (jobs.Service).
type RunGroups interface {
	GroupSummaries(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, groupIDs []uuid.UUID) (map[uuid.UUID]jobs.GroupSummary, error)
}

// Windows lists maintenance windows' stretches (maintenance.Service).
type Windows interface {
	OccurrencesBetween(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, from, to time.Time) ([]maintenance.Occurrence, error)
}

// Entry is one schedule occurrence in the range.
type Entry struct {
	ScheduleID   uuid.UUID
	ScheduleName string
	TemplateID   uuid.UUID
	At           time.Time
	// State is StatePlanned or the recorded decision's outcome.
	State      string
	Reason     string
	JobGroupID *uuid.UUID
	JobID      *uuid.UUID
	JobStatus  string
}

// View is the calendar of one range.
type View struct {
	From, To    time.Time
	Entries     []Entry
	Maintenance []maintenance.Occurrence
	Truncated   bool
}

// Service builds calendar views.
type Service struct {
	schedules ScheduleSource
	groups    RunGroups
	windows   Windows
	now       func() time.Time
}

// NewService wires the service; nil now is time.Now.
func NewService(s ScheduleSource, groups RunGroups, windows Windows, now func() time.Time) *Service {
	if now == nil {
		now = time.Now
	}
	return &Service{schedules: s, groups: groups, windows: windows, now: now}
}

// Range returns the calendar of [from, to).
func (s *Service) Range(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, from, to time.Time) (View, error) {
	if !to.After(from) || to.Sub(from) > MaxRange {
		return View{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: to must be after from, at most 6 weeks later", ErrInvalidRange)
	}
	now := s.now()
	list, _, err := s.schedules.List(ctx, orgID, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return View{}, err
	}
	byID := make(map[uuid.UUID]schedules.Schedule, len(list))
	for _, sch := range list {
		byID[sch.ID] = sch
	}
	view := View{From: from, To: to}
	if err := s.past(ctx, orgID, from, to, byID, &view); err != nil {
		return View{}, err
	}
	for _, sch := range list {
		s.planned(sch, from, to, now, &view)
	}
	slices.SortFunc(view.Entries, func(a, b Entry) int {
		if c := a.At.Compare(b.At); c != 0 {
			return c
		}
		return strings.Compare(a.ScheduleName, b.ScheduleName)
	})
	if len(view.Entries) > maxEntries {
		view.Entries, view.Truncated = view.Entries[:maxEntries], true
	}
	if view.Maintenance, err = s.windows.OccurrencesBetween(ctx, orgID, from, to); err != nil {
		return View{}, err
	}
	return view, nil
}

// past adds the recorded decisions due in the range, with their run
// group's first job and status.
func (s *Service) past(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, from, to time.Time, byID map[uuid.UUID]schedules.Schedule, view *View) error {
	runs, err := s.schedules.RunsBetween(ctx, orgID, from, to)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	var groups []uuid.UUID
	for _, r := range runs {
		if r.JobGroupID != nil {
			groups = append(groups, *r.JobGroupID)
		}
	}
	summaries, err := s.groups.GroupSummaries(ctx, orgID, groups)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	for _, r := range runs {
		sch, ok := byID[r.ScheduleID]
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		e := Entry{ScheduleID: sch.ID, ScheduleName: sch.Name, TemplateID: sch.TemplateID, At: r.DueAt, State: string(r.Outcome), Reason: r.Reason, JobGroupID: r.JobGroupID}
		if r.JobGroupID != nil {
			if g, ok := summaries[*r.JobGroupID]; ok {
				first := g.FirstJobID
				e.JobID, e.JobStatus = &first, string(g.Status)
			}
		}
		view.Entries = append(view.Entries, e)
	}
	return nil
}

// planned adds an active schedule's occurrences after now within the
// range. A stored timetable that no longer parses contributes nothing.
func (s *Service) planned(sch schedules.Schedule, from, to, now time.Time, view *View) {
	if sch.Paused {
		return
	}
	expr, err := cronexpr.Parse(sch.Cron)
	if err != nil {
		return
	}
	loc, err := time.LoadLocation(sch.Timezone)
	if err != nil {
		return
	}
	// Occurrences are whole minutes: starting one second before from
	// keeps an occurrence exactly at from.
	after := from.Add(-time.Second)
	if now.After(after) {
		after = now
	}
	n := 0
	for t := expr.Next(after, loc); !t.IsZero() && t.Before(to); t = expr.Next(t, loc) {
		if n == maxPerSchedule {
			view.Truncated = true
			return
		}
		view.Entries = append(view.Entries, Entry{ScheduleID: sch.ID, ScheduleName: sch.Name, TemplateID: sch.TemplateID, At: t, State: StatePlanned})
		n++
	}
}
```

`internal/api/calendar_handlers.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"time"

	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/calendar"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/tenancy"
)

type calendarEntryDTO struct {
	ScheduleID   string    `json:"schedule_id"`
	ScheduleName string    `json:"schedule_name"`
	TemplateID   string    `json:"template_id"`
	At           time.Time `json:"at"`
	State        string    `json:"state" enum:"planned,triggered,skipped_overlap,skipped_maintenance,missed,failed"`
	Reason       string    `json:"reason,omitempty"`
	JobGroupID   string    `json:"job_group_id,omitempty"`
	JobID        string    `json:"job_id,omitempty" doc:"The run's first job, for the run view"`
	JobStatus    string    `json:"job_status,omitempty" enum:"queued,running,succeeded,failed,cancelled"`
}

type calendarWindowDTO struct {
	WindowID   string    `json:"window_id"`
	Name       string    `json:"name"`
	TargetKind string    `json:"target_kind" enum:"host,inventory_group,org"`
	TargetID   string    `json:"target_id,omitempty"`
	TargetName string    `json:"target_name,omitempty"`
	Start      time.Time `json:"start"`
	End        time.Time `json:"end"`
}

type calendarInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	From string `query:"from" required:"true" doc:"RFC 3339 start of the range"`
	To   string `query:"to" required:"true" doc:"RFC 3339 end of the range (exclusive), at most 6 weeks after from"`
}

type calendarOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		From        time.Time           `json:"from"`
		To          time.Time           `json:"to"`
		Entries     []calendarEntryDTO  `json:"entries"`
		Maintenance []calendarWindowDTO `json:"maintenance"`
		Truncated   bool                `json:"truncated" doc:"Some frequent schedules' occurrences were left out"`
	}
}

// registerCalendarRoutes wires spec §8's calendar: templates.read, like
// the schedules it shows.
func registerCalendarRoutes(hapi huma.API, deps Deps) {
	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/calendar", func(ctx context.Context, in *calendarInput) (*calendarOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermTemplatesRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		from, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, in.From)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, huma.Error400BadRequest("from must be an RFC 3339 time")
		}
		to, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, in.To)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, huma.Error400BadRequest("to must be an RFC 3339 time")
		}
		v, err := deps.Calendar.Range(ctx, scope.OrgID, from, to)
		if errors.Is(err, calendar.ErrInvalidRange) {
			return nil, huma.Error400BadRequest(err.Error())
		}
		if err != nil {
			slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "calendar", "err", err)
			return nil, huma.Error500InternalServerError("internal error")
		}
		out := &calendarOutput{}
		out.Body.From, out.Body.To, out.Body.Truncated = v.From, v.To, v.Truncated
		out.Body.Entries = make([]calendarEntryDTO, len(v.Entries))
		for i, e := range v.Entries {
			dto := calendarEntryDTO{
				ScheduleID: e.ScheduleID.String(), ScheduleName: e.ScheduleName, TemplateID: e.TemplateID.String(),
				At: e.At, State: e.State, Reason: e.Reason, JobStatus: e.JobStatus,
			}
			if e.JobGroupID != nil {
				dto.JobGroupID = e.JobGroupID.String()
			}
			if e.JobID != nil {
				dto.JobID = e.JobID.String()
			}
			out.Body.Entries[i] = dto
		}
		out.Body.Maintenance = make([]calendarWindowDTO, len(v.Maintenance))
		for i, w := range v.Maintenance {
			dto := calendarWindowDTO{WindowID: w.WindowID.String(), Name: w.Name, TargetKind: string(w.TargetKind), TargetName: w.TargetName, Start: w.Start, End: w.End}
			if w.TargetID != nil {
				dto.TargetID = w.TargetID.String()
			}
			out.Body.Maintenance[i] = dto
		}
		return out, nil
	})
}
```

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { createMemoryHistory, createRootRoute, createRoute, createRouter, RouterProvider } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { mockApi, type MockRequest } from '../../test/mockApi.ts';
import type { CalendarEntry } from '../../api/types.ts';
import { ScheduleCalendar } from './ScheduleCalendar.tsx';
import { addDays, entryTone, monthOf, rangeFor, shift, startOfWeek, weekCells, windowsIn } from './calendar.ts';

const monday = startOfWeek(new Date());
// Local times this week, so the test holds in every time zone.
const at = (day: number, hour: number) => new Date(monday.getFullYear(), monday.getMonth(), monday.getDate() + day, hour).toISOString();

const entry = (over: Partial<CalendarEntry>): CalendarEntry => ({
  schedule_id: 's1', schedule_name: 'Nightly OS patching', template_id: 't1', at: at(1, 3), state: 'planned', ...over,
});

function renderIt() {

  const page = createRoute({
    getParentRoute: () => orgRoute, path: '/schedules', component: () => <ScheduleCalendar org="acme" />,
    validateSearch: (s: Record<string, unknown>) => ({ id: typeof s.id === 'string' ? s.id : undefined, template: undefined }),
  });
  const run = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => orgRoute, path: '/runs/$id', component: () => <div>RUN VIEW</div> });
  const router = createRouter({
    routeTree: rootRoute.addChildren([orgRoute.addChildren([page, run])]),
    history: createMemoryHistory({ initialEntries: ['/o/acme/schedules'] }),
  });
  render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <RouterProvider router={router} />
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
  return router;
}

describe('ScheduleCalendar', () => {

    renderIt();

    renderIt();

    await user.click(within(cal).getByRole('tab', { name: 'month' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(cal.querySelectorAll('[data-day]')).toHaveLength(42));
```
